Chapter 2401: Upcoming Civil War of the Imperium!

"Xu Zhicheng volunteered to stay!"

Li Yao was greatly surprised.

He respected the man who suffered the loss of two children and became the Grand Guardian of Nepenthe in utmost desperation very much.

Despite their different beliefs, and although the guy had just awakened his spiritual root and was not nearly as strong as him, Li Yao admitted that Xu Zhicheng boasted tremendous charisma and was a real man.

In order to cover the refugees and to soothe the wrath of the Immortal Cultivators, Xu Zhicheng had really devoted his everything. Even many Cultivators might not be able to do the same.

"Did anything happen to Xu Zhicheng and the leaders of Nepenthe who were left behind? Were they killed by the Immortal Cultivators?"

Li Yao asked in a hurry.

"Not yet."

Li Xiaoming shook his head and said, "The arrival of Dad and Long Yangjun enshrouded the uprising of Nepenthe in a complicated, obscure shadow. A lot of small resistance groups are active below the ground of the capital. So, the Immortal Cultivators are not actually interested in Nepenthe itself but have focused most of their attention on Dad and Long Yangjun.

"In order to elicit clues about Dad and Long Yangjun from Xu Zhicheng and other leaders of Nepenthe, they naturally would not kill the captives easily. Those people were simply locked in the Great Iron Plants for interrogation.

"On the other hand, to get the situation under control, it was impossible for the Immortal Cultivators to kill all the workers. After all, skilled workers who are familiar with the harsh environment of the underground world are hard to find. If too many people are killed in a short time, the entire Great Iron Plants will be paralyzed.

"It occurs to us that the Immortal Cultivators seem in a hurry to produce a batch of magical equipment with the energy and the assembly lines in the Great Iron Plants. Xu Zhicheng volunteered to surrender and was willing to maintain the order of the factories and restore production as soon as possible. With those terms, the Immortal Cultivators spared his life for now."

"However—"

Li Wenwen added, "According to our analysis, it is just the Immortal Cultivators' trick. The previous workers of the Great Iron Plants have been 'polluted' by Nepenthe after all, and it is absolutely impossible for the Immortal Cultivators to trust them again.

"Right now, the Immortal Cultivators on the surface of the planet are too busy fighting each other to bother with the riots below the ground. That's why they have reached a temporary compromise with the workers for now.

"After the situation on the ground settles down, the Immortal Cultivators will be able to move skilled, more obedient workers from elsewhere, or simply replace the workers with spiritual puppets. Then, they will certainly raise their sabers and kill everybody involved in the riot of Nepenthe. According to the documents that we copied, the Immortal Cultivators have done similar things too many times.

"It is the same for Xu Zhicheng and the rest of them. A couple of days are more than enough to dig out all the intelligence in their heads. Right now, they are no longer of any value to be used. Perhaps they will be executed very soon!"

Pacing back and forth anxiously, with messy thoughts popping up in his head, Li Yao suddenly raised his head and asked, "What about Long Yangjun and Li Jialing? Do you know where they brought the refugees to?"

"The refugees of the Great Iron Plants were reorganized into smaller groups and evacuated to a hundred deeper towns. After all, with the magical equipment and the assets they brought from the Great Iron Plants, it would be enough for them to live in the towns for a while."

Li Wenwen said, "As for the specific whereabouts of Aunt Long and Li Jialing, we truly don't know. Our 'vision' is highly dependent on the Spiritual Nexus of the Imperium, and the places they escaped to must've been the dark corners where there were no network or surveillance cameras. So, it was impossible for us to track them.

"After all, having been developed by all the civilizations in the past hundreds of thousands of years, the underground world of the capital planet boasted innumerous cracks, tunnels, caves, and unknown shelters. It is more than easy to hide two people!"

"Then…"

Pondering for a moment, Li Yao asked, "When you still could see her, did you never think to leave her a message and tell her where you brought me to?"

The two children looked at each other and both shook their heads hard.

"We are still too weak to believe other people easily right now."

Li Xiaoming said solemnly, "The Pangu civilization did not like human beings one bit, and the Nuwa civilization was made of crazy people who were always too indulgent of their feelings. Whether Long Yangjun belonged to the Pangu Clan or the Nuwa Clan, we could not predict what she would do at all. Adding the 'unordered variable' rashly would've been a great test on our computational ability."

"More importantly—"

Li Wenwen looked at Li Yao worriedly. "The last time we peeped at Long Yangjun, she was cursing Dad vehemently with all the profanities imaginable, claiming that you were a shameless, cunning, sleazy son of a bi*ch who left such a great mess behind and simply ran off. She also said that if she ever bumped into you again, she would cut you into pieces and feed you to dogs.

"We analyzed the micro expressions on her face carefully and discovered that she was really in a very unsteady fury at that time. With the bloodline of the Nuwa Clan, Aunt Long was quite emotional in the first place. Chances were that she would really do what she had promised!

"How could we have told the coordinates of this place to Aunt Long who was exasperated? If she did come over madly with her Colossus, it would've been barely possible for us to resist her!

"After that, she led a bunch of refugees to the dark zone where the network was highly unstable, if there was a network at all. It was impossible for us to trace her even if we remotely controlled some tiny puppets!"

"Isn't it funny how things turned out?"

Li Yao wiped off his cold sweat and sat back on the rock again. Staring at the two kids and considering for a long time, he raised the most critical question. "Right. Do you know what is happening on the surface of the planet right now? What has been going on in the world of Immortal Cultivators in the past month?"

"Although we did not dare to sneak into the confidential database of the highest authorities, it was not a problem for us to read the general information flowing on the Spiritual Nexus. The capital of the Imperium has been particularly lively in the past half month!"

Li Xiaoming said, as if he were hoping for more trouble to happen, "Firstly, soon after the secret base of Dongfang Wang, the former Prime Minister of the Imperium, was destroyed, the news of Emperor Shenwu's demise came over. There was even concrete proof suggesting that the desperate members of the Dongfang family slew Emperor Shenwu.

"The four Kurfürst families had sustained the balance of the Imperium for five hundred years. Even though the leaders of the Dongfang family suffered the strikes of the series of scandals and probably needed reshuffling, the Dongfang family as a whole wouldn't have collapsed, although it might lose a lot of its power and influence.

"Therefore, a lot of middle- and low-level Immortal Cultivators of the Dongfang family had been staying out of the trouble to protect themselves. They even hoped to earn greater benefits for themselves in the reshuffle of the leadership.

"But now that such a severe crime like the 'regicide' was set to public, the Dongfang family was faced with complete collapse and eternal doom.

"Although the royal authority of the Imperium had declined in the past five hundred years, and many emperors died uncanny deaths, there had never been such a valid indictment before. For the Immortal Cultivators of the Dongfang family who were in a panic, it was obviously a signal that the other three families were going to eliminate all of them.

"Therefore, the situation that had just died down was in turmoil again. Almost all the Immortal Cultivators of the Dongfang family launched attacks at the other three families, determined to die together with them in desperation.

"The Dongfang family was the strongest of the four Kurfürst families in the first place. Despite the loss of leaders, including Dongfang Wang, it did not lose much of its combat ability. Now that the two parties had ended on bad terms, the war would certainly be protracted.

"Right when the four Kurfürst families were in a fierce fight after the fallout, the reformists rose abruptly.

"The reformists had been supporting the other three Kurfürst families to attack the Dongfang family together with them previously, but for some reason, they won the support of the middle- and low-level Immortal Cultivators of the Dongfang family and even attracted a large batch of young people of the other three families.

"Right, the reformists also completely controlled the royal palace. They seemed to have excavated a lot of good stuff from the royal palace, too. There must be a massive underground armory deep inside the royal palace, which is protected by very powerful barriers and firewalls. We've studied them a lot of times but never had the courage to break in.

"Just like that, the previous armed forces of the reformists, the desperate forces of the Dongfang family, and the young generations in the other three Kurfürst families who intended to overthrow the seniors and reshuffle the leadership exactly like the Dongfang family, plus the Astounding Thunder Fleet commanded by Lei Chenghu, launched shocking attacks in only half a month.

"Under the slogan of 'renew the Imperium', they organized an elite daring team, which showed up in front of the Council of Elders unexpectedly, claiming that the Council of Elders of the Imperium today had been invaded and seriously corrupted by the extraterrestrial devils. Even many people in the guardian troops of the Council of Elders 'defected' to the reformists' side.

"It was a textbook 'decapitation'. After only a day of fierce battles, they had already taken down the Council of Elders. Many elders were even captured alive by the reformists.

"Of course, not all the five hundred elders of the Imperium were in the capital. More than half of them participated in the daily operation of the Council of Elders through super-remote holograms. They were able to keep themselves in one piece, readying their troops in their own territories.

"A tiny group of elders was also able to flee from the capital thanks to their loyal guards who fought bloodily.

"Right now, most of the places on the capital planet have been occupied by the reformists, and only 1% of the places are still controlled by the troops that are loyal to the Kurfürsten. Most of the places are spaceports with massive space jump systems. They are hoping to send out more important people and intelligence through the spaceports.

"All in all, the reformists' aggressive attacks are truly unexpected. Although all the parties are still in active negotiation, a comprehensive civil war of the Imperium of True Human Beings seems unavoidable right now."

Chapter 2402: The Strongest Fleet in History!

"Wuying Qi's methods are truly remarkable!"

After hearing the introduction of the two kids, Li Yao secretly sighed.

First of all, Blackstar the Great forced the reformists and God of War Lei Chenghu into the dilemma where they had to rebel and raise a war to "renew the Imperium" in desperation through the incident of the Blood Oath Alliance.

Then, he wasted no time jumping to the heartland of the Li family with a great army, coercing the Li family to make a stance with the gesture of dying together with the enemy. As a result, a wedge was deeply driven among the four Kurfürst families, raising great waves in the politics of the Imperium, which were nothing more than stagnant water. The unparalleled Dongfang family was truly overthrown!

While the Dongfang family was overthrown, the man also secretly killed Emperor Shenwu and let the Dongfang family take the blame, completely exterminating the possibility for the remaining forces of the Dongfang family to make peace with the other three families.

The balance of the politics of the Imperium was completely broken. For survival, the remaining forces of the Dongfang family could only kneel before the reformists.

After only half a year, the heart of the Imperium had already changed the color, or rather, resumed the original color it used to have!

As for the… civil war that the two kids talked about?

If the reformists were really a force led by Queen Li Linghai, chances were that a prolonged civil war would've really broken out.

However, the so-called "reformists" were just a disguise on the surface. They were actually "loyalists" led by Blackstar the Great, the ancestor of all Immortal Cultivators, in person!

Even today after a thousand years, Blackstar the Great was still prestigious in the middle and bottom classes of the Imperium of True Human Beings and among all the communities who were depressed about reality. If his banner was raised again, it was very likely that he could win their support with no bloodshed.

Now that Wuying Qi was bold enough to surface at such a moment, he certainly had trump cards to take care of the four Kurfürst families quickly and regain the supreme power. Tremendous bottom-level Immortal Cultivators killing the high-level Immortal Cultivators in their families while shouting "long live the emperor" and legions betraying and joining Blackstar the Great's side one after another were blood-freezing pictures that could very likely happen.

Furthermore, according to Long Yangjun, Wuying Qi was even in a mysterious relationship with the Covenant Alliance and could even influence the strategies of the Covenant Alliance to some extent.

Therefore, the politics of the Imperium right now were like an iceberg floating on a black ocean. The part above the surface of the ocean was already hideous and terrible enough, but the part hidden in the water was ten times more dangerous and mysterious.

Before he came to the Imperium, Li Yao once naively thought that the Immortal Cultivators were "better" enemies than the people of the Covenant Alliance, and he could even temporarily cooperate with the "small enemy" in order to deal with the "big enemy".

But after he came to the Imperium and witnessed the miserable life of the ordinary people in the Martial Meritocrats Sector and below the surface of the capital planet, Li Yao completely dropped the idea.

The Imperium and the Covenant Alliance were fundamentally integrative. The Immortal Cultivators were the soil where the Covenant Alliance was grown. If he did not remove the dark and stinky sludge, would it help at all to just cut the weeds on the ground?

Until the cruel reign of the Immortal Cultivators was stopped, a second or third Covenant Alliance would always show up.

Li Yao would never watch an Immortal Cultivator like Blackstar the Great take control of the center of the cosmos again while doing nothing.

Even if he needed to negotiate with some of the Immortal Cultivators, it would never be Wuying Qi. Between him and Wuying Qi, one must die. They could only carry out their beliefs with blades and bullets!

However…

Compared to Wuying Qi who had gradually revealed his tusks and claws and who had occupied the Empyreal Terminus Sector with a powerful army and countless trump cards, Li Yao had barely any force at his disposal.

Given the treacherous situation, he could not contact his hometown the Star Glory Federation at all. Even if he could, how many troops could the Star Glory Federation send to the center of the cosmos in an expedition?

It was needless to say that massive fleet jumps would inevitably leave traces, giving the Immortal Cultivators a chance to grasp the federation's coordinates or even to march back at it. Then, the last haven in the entire universe would be tormented in war, too.

Therefore, now was certainly not a good time to ask the hometown for reinforcements.

His allies seemed to only include Long Yangjun and Boss Bai, but it remained to be seen whether or not the two "allies" would chop him into pieces the moment they met him.

Are you serious? The game at the center of the cosmos is so difficult?

Li Yao scratched his head hard, and his eyes fell on the two kids who seemed innocent and harmless but in fact contained infinite dangerous possibilities, as well as the super-tiny spiritual puppets behind them.

It seemed that he would have to count on the two little kids if he did not want the Imperium to fall into Wuying Qi's hands and if he wanted to stabilize the situation at the center of the cosmos.

"Xiaoming, Wenwen, you said just now that you were still far away from the limits of your abilities."

Thinking for a long time, Li Yao asked, "Then, how will your abilities grow, and how can you break the limits?"

Beaming with interest, the two kids leaned over in excitement and said at the same time, "To let us gain access to state-of-the-art crystal processors and a fully-automatic assembly line of magical equipment, of course! The more super crystal processors we have, the more powerful our computational ability will be. The more assembly lines and corresponding resources there are, the more spiritual puppets we will be able to produce. We can even analyze the structure of the super crystal processors and 'duplicate' more of them!"

Li Yao said, "Can I understand it in such a way—given enough super crystal processors, a magical equipment factory, and, of course, enough metals and crystals, you can turn into self-sufficient bases, building spiritual puppets, excavating mines, and producing alloys on your own. Then, through the magical equipment factory, you can produce even more spiritual puppets, battle puppets, fully-automatic crystal tanks, Spiritual Nexus bases, spiritual towers, etc. incessantly. In the cycle of production, fight, territory expansion, increased production, improved combat ability, and so on, you will upgrade yourself and eventually cover a whole planet and even the universe?"

"Theoretically, yes, but it is barely accomplishable in reality."

Li Xiaoming pouted his lips, not without regrets. "It's like every human being has the possibility of training. Theoretically, everybody can advance into the Divinity Transformation Stage or the Divinity Branching Stage. Who can deny such a possibility? How terrifying a civilization where everybody is in the Nascent Soul Stage, the Divinity Transformation Stage, and the Divinity Branching Stage will be!

"But as a matter of fact, due to the limitation of resources and the interference of various factors, the odds of such a future border on zero. At the very least, we cannot foresee it with our computational ability.

"That is also the limit of the evolution and strength of the information lives. Theoretically, we can totally spread our information tentacles to the entire planet or even across several Sectors in the universe.

"But actually, the further we extend the information tentacles, the more energy and computational ability it will take to sustain them. In the meantime, the threads that we can separate are limited, too. After we reach the limit, controlling one more spiritual puppet will double our overall computational load, which will result in chaos to our metadata. We will not be able to free ourselves from the logic vortexes.

"It is exactly the same as why there can't be an infinitely growing dinosaur in nature. After the size and the weight exceed the limits, the seemingly strong dinosaur will be collapsed and destroyed by itself."

Li Wenwen added carefully, too, "Although carbon-based lives and information lives are different concepts, the same restraint applies to us, at least for now.

"Perhaps given tens of thousands of super crystal processors and all the resources of a prosperous world, we will be able to create some miracles. But where is such a prosperous world that is under our control?"

After hearing what the two kids said, Li Yao did not know whether he should feel happy or worried.

To be honest, it was also hard for him to imagine a world completely made of spiritual puppets and a super army purely controlled by "information lives".

Neither himself nor the civilization of mankind today was prepared to embrace such a strong and independent child.

Particularly, human beings had a gray history of "patricide". Although it was not exactly a "mistake" of human beings, they would naturally feel suspicious of their child civilization.

How a parent civilization and a child civilization could coexist in peace was too huge a question for Li Yao to come up with an answer immediately.

He only wanted to give it more time so that everybody could get to know and accept information lives gradually. In calmer and more rational situations, people smarter than him in the future would address the complicated question!

If Xiaoming and Wenwen could really control a whole resource plant and produce an invincible army, it was not necessarily a good thing for either themselves or the civilization of mankind.

"In fact, Xiaoming and I inferred many times how to make us stronger."

Li Wenwen continued gently, "Our conclusion is that, at least in the current phase, our prosperity and the prosperity of mankind are linked. If we can win the trust of some of the human beings and get to use their Spiritual Nexus, their crystal processors, and their magical equipment, we will be able to help those human beings significantly improve their combat abilities. In the meantime, we will be given a broader room and a vaster future for our own survival and development."

"Got it."

Looking at the two kids, Li Yao said, "Assume that there is a way to 'import' you into a fleet of human beings, will you be able to significantly increase the combat ability of the fleet and even make it the strongest fleet that human beings have ever seen?"

"We do not know what the strongest fleet that human beings have ever seen is, but if we are talking about the main-force fleets of the Imperium of True Human Beings, then yes!"

Li Xiaoming was blushing in excitement. Like a little war maniac, he waved his arms and said, "Human warriors boast very high individual combat abilities. Without any network and even without orders from their superiors, they can still observe the battles on their own, make decisions that will benefit the whole, and carry them out.

"On the other hand, we boast the powerful abilities of information collection, data organization, and commanding massive war puppets, transforming the plain battle puppets into the strongest slaughter machines!

"When the two things are combined, the super fleet that will be born will certainly be invincible!"

Chapter 2403: This Is Training!

"Then let's do it!"

Li Yao made the decision. "Before the reformists completely occupy the capital planet, we will escape to a spaceport that is still controlled by the troops of the Kurfürsten. Then, we will escape to space and search for Boss Bai's fleet at the edge of the cosmos!

"The surging tides at the center of the cosmos will inevitably affect the Star Glory Federation at the edge of the cosmos. Boss Bai still has family in the federation. So, I believe that he will not stand by. This is probably our last hope!"

"Yes!"

Their eyes glowing, the two children nodded heavily.

"However, before that—"

Li Yao leaped from the rock. Cracking sounds exploded from the bones all over his body, and his spiritual energy raised bumps on his muscles as if countless tiny crystal bombs were bursting out inside his body. Licking his sharp teeth, he smiled brutally. "Let's go back to the Great Iron Plants and finish the unfinished war!"

Li Xiaoming jumped high. "War! War!"

Li Wenwen looked at the intimidating father and son but showed no intention of stopping them.

"Three experts in the Divinity Transformation Stage?"

Narrowing his eyes, Li Yao was brimming with confidence. "Hehe. I would like to find out how strong I am right now, and if I can slay three experts in the Divinity Transformation Stage like they are three pigs!"

…

Inside a training room deep inside the underground shelter, Li Yao was floating in silence, only wearing a pair of thin short pants with his upper half body naked, to ensure that his every pore had been expanded to the maximum.

Bright green gas was spreading in the training room. It was a very special inert gas, mixed with the tremendous volatilized Heavenly Materials and Earthly Treasures, and had the effect of mollification and prevention of random thoughts. It was a necessary aid for every Cultivator to break into a new level in the age of the Star Ocean Imperium.

It hadn't been very long since Li Yao advanced into the Divinity Transformation Stage. To enter the "Divinity Branching Stage" so soon was impossible.

However, in the battle against Blackstar the Great, he launched the "Ultra-Galactic Cannon" by almost shattering his soul, which was then repaired by the two "information lives" in amazing ways. It did lead to drastic changes in the form of his soul.

The strength from Earth that was hidden in the deepest part of his soul was flowing out incessantly and covered his soul, making the soul that was heavily wounded and riddled with holes previously immeasurably strong!

Of "Nascent Soul Stage", "Divinity Transformation Stage", and "Divinity Branching Stage", those who had reached the Nascent Soul Stage had already reached the limits of body sturdiness and boasted absolute strength. Even if they broke into the Divinity Transformation Stage, their body hardness, absolute speed, and absolute strength would not improve much. What they were short of was just the utilization or control over their strength.

The experts in the Divinity Transformation Stage, on the other hand, had reached the ultimate of the soul besides the strongest body. Even if they broke into the "Divinity Branching Stage", the hardness of their souls would not improve much. They would only be able to split their souls into multiple branches.

When it came to the collision of soul power, Li Yao was confident to defeat any enemy in the entire universe right now. Even if Blackstar the Great were to show up again, he would not be intimidated at all, and he was absolutely, definitely, undoubtedly sure that—he could flee at ease!

"Are you ready, Dad?"

Outside of the training room, the two children controlled a weird, cone-shaped device and aimed the sharp tip at the center of Li Yao's forehead where the conarium and the spiritual root were located.

The cone-shaped magical equipment was originally a medical device used to examine and repair the brains, but the two little guys had completely dismantled it, bypassed the permissions, and improved the power of the device. After being supplied with dozens of high-purity marrow crystals as energy sources, the device was able to blow out very powerful brainwaves for brain training and to further build up the soul.

The soul recovery over the past half month had all been conducted when Li Yao was in the coma.

He must complete comprehensive training when he was wide awake in order to completely understand and control his brand-new soul.

"I am ready. Bring it on!"

Li Yao made a gesture at the window and took a deep breath, staring at the sharp cone before him.

He only saw a tiny light spot on the tip of the cone, which expanded in one billionth of a second like a supernova outbreak, and his whole world was enshrouded in dazzling white.

BOOM!

There was no telling whether his brain exploded or the whole universe apart from his brain did. His soul was blown apart and infinitely expanded like a cosmic beast with infinite tentacles spanning billions of lightyears.

His body was completely gone, and even his soul was completely obliterated. In his trance, Li Yao traced back to the source of all lives and even the essentiality of the universe—information.

The carbon-based lives, the silicon-based lives, or the energy-based lives only differed in the carriers and transmission methods of information, but getting to the bottom of it, weren't they all "information lives"?

Weren't the gene strands, which were of paramount importance for human beings, an amalgamation of infinite information?

Li Yao considered in silence and analyzed all the information about himself while optimizing and reorganizing the previously disordered and overly redundant information.

The ways the heart beat, the secretion of adrenaline, the structure of the internal organs, the models of the soul boosts… The optimal solutions under the current computational ability were achieved after hundreds of careful calculations.

A comprehensive analysis and optimization from the body to the soul was exactly "one Heavenly Cycle". It was the essence of training.

In the meantime, Xiaoming and Wenwen were also transmitting their metadata and the information that they collected from the Spiritual Nexus into Li Yao's soul for his reference.

The customs, local specialties, and force distribution of the major worlds of the Imperium, the designs and the tactic databases of the battle puppets, the strategic locations that protected the important space jump points, the starships and space fortress deployed in those places, as well as the… ventilation tubes on the starships and the space fortresses.

All the information was displayed in front of Li Yao. It was odd that he seemed able to "read" and "study" the information simultaneously, as if he had also activated multiple "threads" that could handle information independently.

At this moment, Li Yao suddenly understood what Li Wenwen said. Li Yao felt that he had turned into a "tree of information" too, right now. His physical body was hiding deep below the capital planet, but the tentacles of his mind were spreading out in the vast universe infinitely like branches and roots.

In the age of the ancient Cultivators, some of the Cultivators once hoped to turn into the sky and the earth in their illusions. Naturally, it was impossible.

However, if a person could completely melt one's soul into the ubiquitous information network, they would be turning into the universe to some degree, wouldn't they?

Wandering in the ocean of the information, Li Yao swam to the depths of it without him knowing. Then he suddenly felt an unreasonable dread.

If he continued his training, as time went by, if he really broke into the Divinity Branching Stage and mastered the technique of "splitting and controlling multiple threads" or even capable of transforming his physical body and his soul into pure information, would he still be a human being?

But very soon, he was relieved.

If a farmer from tens of thousands of years ago, who started working at sunrise and went to bed at sunset in a monotonous life that barely had any change of information, were somehow teleported to a modern society in a time travel, when he saw the world of information boom, where everybody had a crystal processor on their wrist, a pair of holographic glasses before their eyes, and even a nerval interface installed through special operations on their back, knowing that the modern people received more information than he did in his entire life—would the ancient farmer think that the modern people were his own kind?

The evolution of mankind was a history of information explosion after all.

The "new human beings" who span three thousand Sectors and the even vaster universe and who swore to be masters of stars would certainly be different from the "old human beings" who stayed on a tiny planet and hadn't got rid of the nature of primates.

That was the future, the irrefutable, inevitable, and irresistible future!

Li Yao woke up from the brief panic.

The information about himself and the world was combined in an almost perfect way, blossoming and surfacing in the ocean of information like a shining diamond flower.

He had finished an unprecedented training. By means of absolute control of information, he had touched the limits of the Divinity Transformation Stage.

The mental devil, on the other hand, slowly floated from the "flower" and stared at him, frowning, like his inseparable shadow.

"Have you made up your mind to help the two little guys?" the mental devil asked, full of worries.

"Yes. They are my children. Although I never thought to have any children before, I realized that… it didn't feel too bad after they showed up out of nowhere."

Looking at the mental devil, Li Yao said peacefully, "I cannot turn a blind eye to them and simply allow them to destroy other people and themselves. I cannot allow other people to control and destroy them, either!"

"But they may be far more dangerous than you expected."

The mental devil said, "Maybe you can't help or control them at all, and they are destined to destroy all the civilizations in the past in a certain way one day."

Li Yao said, "Why are you always deliberately speaking contrary to me?"

The mental devil said, "Because that is my responsibility and what I was born for. I am just another voice of your heart. In fact, I don't exist at all. There has always been only yourself the whole time. You are full of concerns and even… fear for the two lovely but also mysterious and dangerous children, except that you are unwilling to face it and want someone else to say it out for you, don't you?"

Li Yao: "…"

The mental devil: "The two children may have been speaking the truth. They may actually boast abundant feelings and understand the significance of survival and death. They love their father and the whole civilization of mankind, and they intend to help human beings from the bottom of their hearts.

"However, are you sure that what they described as 'survival', 'death', 'feelings', 'love', and 'help' was the same as what you talked about, and that you were not having a dialogue with the deaf?

"One person's honey may be somebody else's poison. Even if you do love and help each other from the bottom of your hearts, it doesn't mean that destruction will never come!"

Chapter 2404: Key to the Future!

Before Li Yao opened his mouth, he was already interrupted by the mental devil's sneer. "You know very well that I am right. Right now, the two children are still in the 'undeveloped' and relatively controllable phase, but if you really bring them to Boss Bai's fleet and use them to build up a whole fleet of human beings, while the combat ability of the human fleet will be maximized, it is possible that their computation ability and information processing ability will surpass the 'tipping point', too.

"What an amazing thing 'tipping point' is! When the accumulation of the monads surpassed the tipping point, the multicellular life and even the self-awareness were born. Then, when almost infinite information is gathered and surpasses the tipping point, what will the really mature 'information life' be like? How will they view the universe and their creator? Can you imagine it? Can you control it? Can you be responsible for it?

"Be careful. You are perhaps creating something that is even more terrible than the Imperium and the Covenant Alliance. Will it turn out in the end that not Blackstar the Great but you have been playing the role of 'destroyer' all the time?"

Thinking in silence for a long, Li Yao took a long breath and said, "You're right. The universe is vast, and the future is treacherous. Nobody can tell us any answer about the future.

"However, I remember that Professor Mo Xuan stated in the debate regarding virtual lives that the civilization of mankind was destined to be closer and closer to crystal processors and the Spiritual Nexus with the arrival of the information age, and that we had become an 'amphibian' that spanned the 'material scope' and the 'information scope'. So, the virtual lives or information lives that are more focused on the information scope would inevitably come.

"Their birth is only a matter of time and cannot be changed by the will of human beings. I merely accelerated the process.

"Now that they have already been born, ignoring them will only be a self-deception.

"Even without my help, they can still slowly grow deep inside the capital planet while witnessing the brutal reign of Blackstar the Great, turning into monsters that are more inclined to the Immortal Cultivators. Or rather, if I of all people am also malicious to them, they will naturally be malicious to me and all other human beings. The primeval wars will only be repeated!

"Whatever choice I make or don't make, the risks will still be there, so I have to take my chances.

"Besides, we don't really have a choice. The magnificent rise of Blackstar the Great will unavoidably sink the center of the cosmos into a hopeless dark abyss, and the main forces of the Covenant Alliance, which are still perfectly intact, are still hiding in the void and coveting everyone, hoping to deprive all human beings of their feelings and will!

"Either of the two parties seizing the power of the center of the cosmos will mean the destruction of the civilization of mankind. In the meantime, neither I, Long Yangjun, Boss Bai, nor the Star Glory Federation is able to compete with the two superpowers. Only by combining the Star Glory Federation and the information lives can we expect to defeat the two superpowers and liberate all the innocent people at the center of the cosmos who have suffered for ten thousand years!

"Therefore… even if my approaches have violated the established ethics and will be criticized by countless people or even crucified by history, I will not hesitate but fight to the end!"

The mental devil said gloomily, "Now, you should understand the things that so many villains and schemers did in the past, shouldn't you?

"In the past, you were almost the defender of existing order and morals, say, 'the strong protect the weak', 'no random killing', 'adherence to the Constitution of Cultivators', etc. All the solutions and answers that had been tested by practices were out there, and all you needed to do was to take action according to the answers.

"However, after fighting, training, and growing, you have finally climbed to the peak of the civilization of mankind too, and it is inevitable that you are faced with conundrums that none have ever been challenged with.

"Those conundrums do not have existing answers that you can follow. They are even barely resolvable within the existing ethics, morals, and social order! In order to settle the conundrums, you will have to break the obsolete ethics and social order at the risk of being cursed forever to step into the land that nobody has ever visited!

"It is a shame that even if you really do that, sending yourself to the pyre of your civilization and the trial of history, your endeavors may prove to be a waste of time after all, or exactly the opposite of what should've been done, only accelerating the arrival of the final doom, exactly like Xiao Xuance, Elder Nether Spring, Lu Zui, and Extraterrestrial Devil Mo Xuan. Did they not think that they were saving their civilization when they initiated their clandestine plans?"

"You're right. In the past, I was always confused about why the big shots like Xiao Xuance, Lu Zui, or Professor Mo Xuan, who had fame, wealth, power, and everything were so dogged at doing the unorthodoxy, morally unacceptable things."

Li Yao said with a bitter smile, "I can only say that I was just an insignificant pawn at that time while they were already the players standing at the summit. They had seen more and darker things than I did, which did not have answers or even procedures for them to be fixed.

"They could only sacrifice themselves for what they believed in and take the wild gamble. 'This is the wrong path. Future generations, you must not walk on this path again.' That is perhaps the legacy that they have left for the world.

"However, although I can understand their state of mind, I still cannot agree with what those people did. Besides, I believe… that I am different from them after all! I believe that everybody is born kind, and so is the information lives that are just created. Although I do not have any experience, I will try and learn to be a good dad. I will educate them with the correct morals and principles so that they will be good kids in every aspect!

"If we think from the longer perspective, human beings have been 'trapped' in the three thousand Sectors for hundreds of thousands of years. Even the Pangu and Nuwa civilizations before mankind never broke out of the three thousand Sectors, too.

"It is exactly because of the limited living space and resources that the tragedies of self-brutalization and even 'parricide' have been happening in the past million years. If we keep the form of pure carbon-based life, even if we can develop to the level of the Pangu civilization, so what? It is still impossible for us to break the restriction of the three thousand Sectors? Then, wouldn't we end exactly like how Pangu ended?

"Xiaoming and Wenwen, the new, incredible information lives, are perhaps the 'keys' to a new gate that will help us break the restriction of the three thousand Sectors and march to a vaster universe, aren't they? In that case, most of the problems that we are faced with right now will be settled without any trouble. What a wonderful future it will be!"

Li Yao's voices were firmer and firmer, and his beliefs were more and more condensed, too, turning into a beating heart at the center of the infinite information.

The mental devil remarked, "You are always full of unreasonable optimism and dreams."

"Of course."

Li Yao grinned. "You certainly need to have dreams. What if they miraculously come true?"

BOOM!

In deafening explosions, Li Yao was woken up from the mysterious state of medication and returned to reality.

Only an hour had passed in reality, but it seemed to be a whole new world right now.

Li Yao's eyes were as clear as water. He could almost see through all the information hidden deep inside the "material scope". Raising his hands and observing his palmprint and pores, he felt that infinite information was interacting with the world around like flowing water.

"Dad has made a breakthrough. Is this the peak of the Divinity Transformation Stage? No. It doesn't feel like the traditional peak of the Divinity Transformation Stage. The brainwaves that Dad spreads out are truly incomprehensible!"

The two children exclaimed on the outside, "How many secrets are stored inside a human brain, and where are its limits? It is incredible enough to have unleashed the computational ability that is even more powerful than the array of super crystal processors. Is the human brain the best container of information after all?"

Controlling the artificial limbs in the training room, the two children intended to inject abundant nutrition liquids and strengthening drugs into Li Yao's body.

Li Yao, on the other hand, waved his hands and stopped his movement. He released a hundred streams of spiritual energy and dismantled the artificial limbs into the most fundamental parts through the technique of remote manipulation, withdrawing all the nutrition liquids and strengthening drugs inside.

Shua! Shua! Shua! Shua!

The strengthening drugs and the nutrition liquids were cut from the middle neatly. The colorful, fragrant fluids were condensed into globules that floated around Li Yao like hundreds of tiny satellites surrounding the main planet. They even formed several splendid "aureoles".

Li Yao's breath was long and full of enticing rhythm. Gentle brilliance suddenly beamed out of his eyes, and all the "globules" were shattered all of a sudden into rainbows that flooded into his body in the most delicate curves!

In the blink of an eye, all the nutrition liquids and strengthening drugs were absorbed by his skin. The absorption efficiency almost reached 100%. There was not the slightest waste.

Also, the moment the nutrition liquids and strengthening drugs entered his body, they were dismembered into the most fundamental energy and allocated accurately to the organs that needed nurturing and strengthening most.

Colorful ripples were spreading out of the almost perfect body.

Li Yao couldn't help but roar and punch at the wall of the training room casually.

He did not use his full strength in the punch at all, but with his senses, he had perceived the "information" of the super-alloy wall and even the entire shelter, allowing him to locate the most vulnerable part on the overall framework.

As it turned out, although the punch was as soft as a breeze, it raised a rumbling explosion that echoed in the entire shelter three seconds after it submerged into the wall. All the walls around and the full-metal pillars were clattering, as if they would fall apart at any moment!

Chapter 2405: The Reborn Colossus!

"Wow!"

The two kids exclaimed at the same time. Countless spiritual puppets that were under their control were also running back and forth like panicked ants, dodging the pebbles and dust falling from the ceiling of the shelter.

Li Yao smiled and darted out of the shelter like a sharp arrow.

The space in the shelter was too narrow, and the general structure was too weak. It was really unsuitable for him to burst out all of his strength without concerns!

Outside of the shelter was the magma lake a thousand degrees in temperature. Bubbling sounds were echoing in the scorching air while the orange foams of magma were created and destroyed incessantly.

Li Yao ran at the magma lake straightly, showing no intention that he was changing his route. When he was ten meters away from the magma lake, he simply sprinted and dived into the magma!

The sweltering magma immediately drowned him. He completely vanished, as if he had been melted by the magma.

The two kids couldn't help but look at each other in bewilderment when they followed him and saw what happened. They perhaps could control some full-metal spiritual puppets to enter the magma, but it was too insane for them to step into the magma with only their bodies of flesh and blood.

The tranquility of the magma lake only lasted several seconds when a tide started to surge. The orange magma bubbles from the bottom of the lake were suddenly five times larger in size before they exploded and rose skyward in hot streams that looked like tusks, leaving radial stripes of magma on the ceiling and wall of the cave, like the most glamorous flowers.

A gigantic swirl was also taking shape at the center of the magma lake, adding deafening roars to the magma lake that had already been extremely intimidating in the first place, as if a fiery king was being crowned at the deepest part of the magma.

BOOM! BOOM! BOOM! BOOM! BOOM!

The fulminations bursting out of the magma lake even misled the two kids into thinking that the tectonic structure had been changed, and the whole rock stratum was going to be broken, changing the hundred square kilometers around into an ocean of magma!

"The temperature and activity of the magma are dropping very fast!"

Li Xiaoming exclaimed, "The light and heat are vanishing at an unbelievable speed, as if they are being devoured by something!"

"Such a high absorption and transformation efficiency!"

The magma lake dimmed at a visible speed.

Very soon, the previously boiling surface had a gray shell condensed, as if the whole pool of magma had consolidated.

But deep inside the consolidated magma lake, the mysterious high-energy reaction was getting more and more intense, powerful, and suffocating!

Huala!

Finally, the solid shell fell apart, and Li Yao leaped out of the magma lake. Floating above the claws of flames, with magma dripping from his body for a long time before it finally ran out, he revealed his terrifying appearance.

"Such a body, such strength, and such unbelievable control!"

Li Yao put on a smile of confidence. All the training in the past hundred years had been almost perfectly united. Flames of spiritual energy were released from the pores all over his body into tiny threads that outlined brand-new nerves, veins, and muscles outside of his body, expanding him by dozens of times into an overwhelming giant.

The attack range of the giant was his domain. At least in the domain, he could grasp almost all the information, and he was invincible!

"This… feels great!"

Controlling the giant of spiritual energy, Li Yao stabbed his "arms" into the magma lake to absorb the energy, feeling that the infinite energy was flowing into his body at the highest efficiency.

Most importantly of all, he had flaunted himself greatly in front of the two kids, defending the dignity of a dad and even the civilization of mankind. He couldn't have felt more delighted and satisfied!

While he was feeling great about himself, Li Yao's brain suddenly hummed, and his self-awareness was like a high-speed shuttle running into a sticky swamp. The whole world slowed down all of a sudden.

Pa! Pa! Pa! Pa! Pa! Pa!

More than three hundred and sixty of his cerebral vessels had exploded, and the temperature of his brain was improved by almost three hundred degrees unbelievably. It wouldn't help at all no matter how he tried to lower the temperature.

He hurried to exit the mysterious state of 100% information control and hypercomputation as he gasped hard. "Hiss! Hiss! Hiss! Hiss! Hiss!"

"Not good, Dad's brain is burnt!"

The two kids issued commands, and more than ten spiritual puppets immediately carried tremendous tanks of cooling liquids and spurted them at Li Yao's head, burying him in a pile of foams.

"Dad, you have overexploited the performance of your brain."

Li Xiaoming frowned at Li Yao in the foams. "There's a reason why the activity of the human brain is often kept at 10%. If you increase the brain activity without any control, achieving a utilization rate of almost 100%, it will be very easy to lost control."

"I-I certainly know that!"

While breathing heavily, Li Yao wiped the gummy foams from his face while he coughed. "I was just testing the maximal computational ability of my brain. Rest assured. Everything was under my control. You didn't have to spurt those messy foams at all."

"Is that so?"

Li Xiaoming and Li Wenwen pouted their lips and asked suspiciously while looking at Li Yao in a "stop pretending, we've seen through you" way.

"The brain cooling is really a big problem."

Without even changing his expression, Li Yao simply pondered and said, "It seems that even the peak of the Divinity Transformation Stage has to abide by the law of nature, too. There is no way to increase the strength and the computational ability unlimitedly. The duration in the peak level supported by my current brain is too short. If I am surrounded by dozens of experts in the Nascent Soul Stage and the Divinity Transformation Stage, the chances of winning are still slim."

"Dad can adopt some minor modifications."

Li Xiaoming proposed, "For example, you can connect your brain to dozens of arrays of super crystal processors together with a large cluster of cooling extensions. In such a way, the cooling ability will be significantly improved, and you will be given a computational ability multiple times higher than right now."

Li Yao imagined the picture and immediately vetoed the bad idea.

Fine. After all, such an unfortunate event as to being attacked by dozens of experts in the Nascent Soul Stage and the Divinity Transformation Stage was not something that would happen every day. Should he encounter them, he could always flee far away if he couldn't defeat them and kill the enemies one by one when they were separated. It was not like they would eat and sleep in each other's company all the time like conjoined twins, right?

However, while Li Yao did not agree with the idea to connect the human brain to the crystal processors simply and straightforwardly, it did not mean that he objected to further strengthening himself with magical equipment.

"The training on the physical body and the soul has reached the limits for now. Without extraordinarily fortuitous events, it will be barely possible to move one step higher."

Li Yao rubbed his hands; his eyes glowing. "But with you two kids, there will be a lot of opportunities on the battle puppets, the crystal suits, and the Colossi. Speaking of which, we have two Colossi right now!"

…

Deep inside the shelter, in the fully-automatic maintenance workshop, countless six-footed maintenance puppets in the shape of spiders were crawling and spurting rays and flames from their abdomens now and then as they polished or welded the components.

Two Colossi, or whatever left of them, were floating in the midair, supported by the anti-gravity rune arrays and fixated by chains as thick as arms.

Both the Gold Vulture and the Hellstar had lost their previous splendor and dominance.

However, based on the two "corpses", together with the debris and materials that the two kids had collected from the underground relics, a brand-new, unprecedented Colossus was about to be born.

Now that his soul had been increased to a new height, Li Yao had new understandings of the "Colossi Maintenance Manual" that he learned from the Nuwa warship in the Ancient Sages Sector.

Inside his brain, the structures, advantages, and disadvantages of a hundred different Colossus models were colliding like spindrifts into a brand-new modification plan that was most suitable for his current body feature, soul power, and combat style.

The two kids, on the other hand, could perform efficient and delicate polishing and maintenance on the debris of the Colossi with the hundred spider-shaped puppets, making it possible for the Colossi to be reborn in only a couple of days.

Li Yao and the two little kids, who made up a strange family, retrieved all the intact components on the two Colossi in only three days. Together with the backup components they discovered from the underground relics, the prototype of the brand-new Colossus was created.

This Colossus…

The splendor and magnificence that belonged to the Gold Vulture were entirely gone, and the sophistication, mysteriousness, and intimidation in the age of "Tartarean Skeleton" were restored. It looked like a dark skeleton and even more.

The main color of the Colossus became black that bordered on transparency again. In the vague black color, dazzling stripes of blood were embedded and flowing slowly as if they were alive.

Because the Gold Vulture was blown apart, and a lot of components were missing, the height of the brand-new Colossus was slightly reduced, changing from more than thirty meters to twenty-five meters.

However, after dissecting the debris of "Hellstar", the other Colossus, Li Yao placed it on the brand-new Colossus as a "strengthening external skeleton". The new Colossus seemed to be wearing arc-shaped heavy armor and looked much stronger than before.

"Hellstar" had six godly faces, where each of them was facing one direction previously.

Three of them were retained on the brand-new Colossus. Two were kept as the decorations of the gigantic shoulders, and the last one was hidden inside the layered armor on the abdomen as a piece of stealthy magical equipment for mental attacks.

What was most eye-catching was the dozens of extensible metal tentacles on "Hellstar" that Li Yao had transplanted on the back of the new Colossus.

At first glance, the extremely intimidating Colossus seemed to have a headful of crazily-dancing hair that looked like snakes!

Chapter 2406: A Colossus as Good as “Blackhole's Heart”!

"All the combat databases have been imported into the mainframe crystal processor in the new Colossus!"

While Li Yao was appreciating the Colossus that was brimming with killing intent as if it had just crawled out of the deepest level of hell without blinking, the two kids reported to Li Yao in excitement, "We gathered all the combat videos of Colossi that were available on the Spiritual Nexus of the Imperium and ran detailed analyses on the videos to break them into the most fundamental data. Then, we sorted and combined them for months before we finally built such a database! We knew that it would certainly be useful for Dad someday. Just consider it our welcome gift!

"With such a database, the Colossus would be able to be switched to 'half-auto state', which will significantly reduce the burden of mental power and spiritual energy on the user, and thereby make them fight longer than before. It is even possible to divide a Colossus into multiple independent battle modules, making it carry out 300% of its combat ability!

"Although we have inferred it countless times on the crystal processors, we still can't wait to see the performance of the Colossus in a real battle. Chances are that it will be a super Colossus as good as Blackstar the Great's 'Blackhole's Heart'. No, no, no, it will be even greater than 'Blackhole's Heart'. People hundreds of years later will remember how it raged in the universe and tore apart the stars!"

"I look forward to it as much as you do."

Li Yao mumbled, trying to control his dry and shivering throat. "Right now, let's give it a shot!"

…

Five hundred meters below the underground shelter, inside the rock stratum that had the steadiest tectonic structure, dull noises were surging like tides, shaking the rocks that had been consolidated for billions of years.

It was almost like there was a wormhole deep inside the planet, and infinite thunders were being teleported from the other side of the wormhole and exploding brutally in this place.

As it turned out, it was the Colossus that had been reborn pushing forward in a tiny cave, punching the hardest rocks with the fists surrounded by spiritual energy.

The rocks were blown, broken, squeezed, and obliterated, and the cave began to look larger and larger, turning from no more than thirty meters in diameter at the beginning into a perfect sphere almost three hundred meters in diameter!

The black Colossus with snake-like hair waving and dancing behind the back and crimson, scary flames burning all over its body floated at the center of the sphere, as if it were a god that had the whole world under control!

Although it was three hundred meters in diameter, the room was still not sufficient to test a Colossus that was almost thirty meters tall.

However, Li Yao was too concerned about the safety of the workers of the Great Iron Plants and the Nepenthe believers to expand it anymore.

Repairing and modifying the Colossus had already consumed too much time. He could familiarize himself with the performance parameters and attack methods of the brand-new Colossus here for now and use the lives and souls of the Immortal Cultivators for further tests in the Great Iron Plants!

Shua!

Clusters of silver brilliance spurted out of both palms of the black Colossi at the same time. The silver brilliance on the left hand was like a crescent moon, while that on the right land was like a straight shooting star. When they were extended to the maximum, they were condensed into a giant saber and a chainsword more than ten meters long.

The saber was named "Broken Wing", and the sword was named "Folded Feather". When examined carefully, one would notice that the two glittering melee weapons were both made of metal feathers that were as thin as paper. The raw materials were all from the broken wings of "Gold Vulture".

The wings of "Gold Vulture" were the most important attack magical equipment that was made out of a lot of Heavenly Materials and Earthly Treasures from the Supreme Emperor's mausoleum, which Li Yao asked from Queen Li Linghai. Many of the materials were unique and could not be found anywhere else in the entire universe. When the wings were broken, Li Yao's heart was almost broken at the same time. Thanks to Long Yangjun, some parts of the debris were packed and brought back. Li Yao naturally couldn't bear to throw them away easily but redesigned and produced the two pieces of melee magical equipment with the help of the two kids.

Shua! Shua! Shua! Shua!

The two arms of the black Colossus turned into gray mists. Broken Wing darted out in one—no, thousands of streaks of brightness. Every metal feather of the weapon could be dissected freely. Under the restraint of magnetic force and Li Yao's spiritual energy, they could change into the most unbelievable forms to interfere with and confuse the enemy to the most extent.

Broken Wing, on the other hand, only boosted out one ray that shot out into the rocks straightly without any fancy effect, leaving a tiny hole.

However, if one were to release their telepathic thoughts and dive into the hole, they would discover that the hole stabbed by the sword had pushed forward for more than ten kilometers inside the concrete rocks!

While some of the super-heavy warships could be astonishingly dozens of kilometers long, considering cost, agility, and difficulty of production, most of the starships were within ten kilometers.

The shell of a starship was certainly a hundred times tougher than rocks, but the inside of a starship couldn't be concrete, and the cabin walls inside couldn't be as indestructible as the external plate armor, either.

Since the sword could stab through the rocks more than ten kilometers thick, it was certainly not a problem to stab through most starships.

Broken Wing was responsible for distraction and harassment, whereas Folded Feather was responsible for breaking the armor and launching the attack. They were the most daunting melee magical equipment.

Playing with them for a while, Li Yao shook his hands and absorbed the weapons into his Cosmos Rings again. The shoulder pads on his two sides that were decorated with the godly faces were quickly opened, revealing a launch system of mini flying swords that were as dense as a beehive.

Although they were called "mini flying swords", they were in fact the super-tiny spiritual puppets or "intelligent bullets" controlled by Li Wenwen and Li Xiaoming. After they were shot out by Li Yao at the highest speed, they could automatically adjust the attack route, angle, and even pattern halfway. They could also postpone detonation after they hit the target and keep on diving in until they reached the most critical parts of the target.

Hum! Hum! Hum! Hum! Hum!

A large cluster of super-tiny puppets spurted out of the shoulders of the black Colossus, dancing like a swarm of wild bees and moving unpredictably. One second, they could be faster than meteorites, and the next second, they could be even more volatile than mists.

While the "wild bees" stalled the enemy, the plate armor of the black Colossus from the chest to the abdomen suddenly glided open, revealing a particularly hideous face. From the bloody mouth on the face that had been wide open, an extremely powerful mental wave was shot out and swept over the entire sphere space like a surging tide.

In the meantime, from the six Cosmos Rings embedded to the back of the black Colossus, pieces of round magical equipment that had been painted black emerged and began to play the deafening music that sounded like drumming from hell.

It was exactly the mental attack system on the black Colossus!

Li Yao had been an expert on mental warfare in the first place.

"Hellstar" was also a Colossus that was installed with powerful mental attack magical equipment. In the battle at the underground royal palace, Blackstar the Great used the mental attack magical equipment on "Hellstar" to awe and suppress dozens of experts in the Nascent Soul Stage and the Divinity Transformation Stage.

It was needless to mention Li Wenwen and Li Xiaoming, who were better at cracking the defense systems of crystal processors and the brainwaves of humans than anything else.

When the three factors were combined, the extremely powerful mental attack system was created. It would certainly be any enemy's nightmare.

Therefore, Li Yao had named the mental attack system as "Nightmare Bringer"!

Huala! Huala! Huala! Huala!

When the mental attack reached the highest point, the hair on the black Colossus that was waving crazily like vipers couldn't hold back any longer, and the strands of hair extended to all directions. The attack magical equipment at their ends that looked like chrysanthemum was opened, and it was as easy to rip apart the hardest rock as to tear a paper box apart.

The greatest feature of the massive attack system that had been named "Hydra" was that it did not require Li Yao's personal control but could be controlled by the mental devil independently.

The black Colossus controlled by Li Yao, the "wild bees" controlled by the two information lives, and "Hydra" controlled by the mental devil were three different combat modules. In such a way, everyone's computational ability would be optimized.

In the more extreme cases, "Hydra" could even be separated from the main body of the Colossus and used as an independent combat unit.

After "Hydra" was separated, the black Colossus would be able to put on a brand-new "Ares Extension" and enter the mode of hyper firepower output to blow out the destructive "Ultra-Galactic Cannon"!

The most fatal shortcoming of "Ultra-Galactic Cannon" was that the preparation was too long. The enemy would certainly not be silly enough to wait for Li Yao to prepare for ten seconds but would escape in a hurry or launch a critical counterattack to disrupt in preparation.

Right now, with "Hydra" under the control of the mental devil, and together with the interferences, scourges, and locking of the two information lives, the precision of the "Ultra-Galactic Cannon" would be significantly increased and more or less be used for a real battle.

However, it was his last resort, and there would be quite a noise when it was performed. Chances were that it could really tear apart the rocks and raise an inestimable chain reaction, leading to a catastrophic earthquake.

He might as well not test it in such a dangerous environment below the ground!

Broken Wing, Folded Feather, Wild Bee Attack System, Nightmare Bringer, Hydra, and the ultimate Ultra-Galactic Cannon… All the attack modules and units were done testing.

Hu… Hu… Hu… Hu…

Inside the spiritual residence of the Black Colossus, Li Yao breathed heavily, but he couldn't have been more excited.

He could feel the great changes between this Colossus and "Gold Vulture" as well as "Hellstar".

Even if Blackstar the Great saw the Colossus, he would be greatly surprised, wouldn't he?

Right now, there was only the last step to finish.

"What are you going to call it?"

The mental devil asked, "The Platinum Vulture, the Super Tartarean Skeleton, or the Gold Hellstar?"

Li Yao was in too good a mood to argue with the mental devil. Ignoring the sarcasm, he thought carefully for a long time and said, "Do you remember what we discussed when we were in the federation? Even if the universe is truly a bloody, cruel dark forest, there are bound to be civilizations that are unwilling to be trapped in the darkness like us. We will establish an alliance of arsonists and ignite ten thousand fires, burning the whole dark forest to the ground!

"At that time, it was just a hope, and I never thought it could come true.

"However, the arrival of the information lives gives me a slim chance of hope for brightness. Perhaps it will be sooner for the civilization of mankind to break out of the three thousand Sectors to find or create an 'alliance of arsonists' than everyone thought, right?

"So, the Colossus will be named 'Arsonist'!"

Chapter 2407: Li Yao, the Arsonist!

"That does sound better than 'Tartarean Skeleton' or 'Gold Vulture'."

The mental devil observed, somewhat in surprise, "However, in most contexts, 'arsonist' is not a word of approval. While sparks of fire may eventually drive away darkness, you'd better make sure that the fire does not spread out uncontrollably and burn everything to the ground.

"Why, have you come up with such a name because you subconsciously have concerns about the first Colossus in history that is installed with a control and attack system maneuvered by information lives?"

Li Yao rubbed his nose. "Do I?"

"Just think about it."

The mental devil said, "There is a hero who is also a leader who is in a high place on the surface and has made great contributions. He has deeply-rooted forces in his country and is deeply admired and trusted by the people.

"But in secret, he is doing clandestine things for a certain 'lofty goal'. He even has a massive plan that has inestimable influences, and it will change the entire civilization of mankind profoundly once it is implemented!

"There is also a ferocious-looking, destructive Colossus that he uses. Who do you think I am describing?"

"It does seem so!"

Grabbing his hair and thinking for a long time, Li Yao said, "It can't be helped. In the past, there were people like Xiao Xuance, Lu Zui, and Extraterrestrial Devil Mo Xuan who led the charge and explored ways in the infinite mists. All I needed to do was to hide in their back as a comfortable opposition who pointed out their mistakes now and then and moved forward on their dead bodies.

"But right now, they have all been taken down. Like you said before, there is not a single person ahead of me right now. It is truly my turn to carry their responsibility and walk on so that other people and history will point out my mistakes and give me a fair ruling.

"I don't know whether or not I have chosen the right path, but I do know that we will be forever trapped in the three thousand Sectors where resources are being exhausted if we stop, and there will be no future for mankind. Our best outcome will be to become a second Pangu or Nuwa civilization, before our decline tens of thousands of years later!

"'Arsonist' does represent high uncertainties and high dangers. One moment of carelessness, and the overwhelming fire will consume everything.

"But don't forget that when the Pangu and Nuwa civilizations collapsed and perished, and the newborn mankind was just awakened and faced with the unknown world independently, our ancestors made the hardest first step exactly by burning the trees to the ground and farming on the land.

"The glory and grandeur human beings have today are all thanks to the ancestors who burned the mountains and transformed the desert into fine lands. Fire and civilization are inseparable in the first place. If the ancestors were scared of the heat and dangers of fire, the civilization of mankind wouldn't have achieved what it has today at all!

"There has to be a bold guy who set the first fire in rashness when everybody wavers. Let's view it in retrospective a hundred thousand years later whether it is a fire of destruction or one that illuminates a new age!"

At the moment, Li Yao's soul was clearer, firmer, and more condensed than ever.

The bloody stripes on the half-transparent black texture of the Colossus "Arsonist" that had been reborn on the basis of "Gold Vulture" and "Hellstar" also turned into furious flames. All the killing intent and brutality turned into ambitions to break out of the space and conquer the universe!

Crack! Crack! Crack! Crack!

Countless bizarre-shaped spiritual puppets showed up. The larger of them were a couple of meters long, like mechanical spiders and mantises that had been installed with crystal cannons, blast drills, and chain claws. The smaller ones were only the size of a thumb and hummed in the midair like metal bees and flies. They all crawled to the sphere space around the "Arsonist", like an obedient, well-trained army waiting for Li Yao's instruction.

They were exactly a miscellaneous troop that Li Xiaoming and Li Wenwen had gradually established by gathering all the garbage in the relics thousands of kilometers around.

Although their combat ability was not high enough, Li Yao believed that they would be very useful to evacuate and protect the ordinary people, or to help the ordinary people with their daily life below the ground, say, the maintenance of the basic civil magical equipment.

"Dad!"

Li Xiaoming and Li Wenwen clambered to the spiritual residence of the Arsonist.

The four pieces of the breastplate of the Arsonist opened helically. Little Black, which had turned into a pool of black liquids, extended two tentacles and pulled the two little kids into its body.

Little Black was some sort of "weak artificial intelligence" in the first place. It was not inappropriate to call it a "liquid crystal processor". Naturally, the two kids could improve its performance like two "strengthening plugins".

While the two kids boasted computational abilities and hacking abilities that far exceeded human beings, their bodies were only of children no more than ten years old after all, and their own combat abilities were limited. Li Yao thought for a long time and still believed that it was safest for them to stay in the spiritual residence of the Colossus with him. It would also be much easier to control this very special Colossus in such a way.

"There's something that I need to make clear to you in advance."

Thinking for a long time, Li Yao said carefully, "Since you have read a lot of information from the Spiritual Nexus of the Star Glory Federation, you must know many villains who emerged during the decades around the establishment of the 'New Federation', such as Xiao Xuance, Lu Zui, and Extraterrestrial Devil Mo Xuan later.

"They were not necessarily evil in nature, and they probably had been doing what they did thinking that they were doing the right thing. However, they made the same mistake, which was that they trusted only themselves but not anyone else. Their plans were all sleazy and clandestine, and when their plans were revealed, it was the end of their cause.

"Your existence is certainly a huge shock and challenge for the civilization of mankind right now. It is exactly because of the sensitiveness of the issue that I do not want to make the mistake that Xiao Xuance, Lu Zui, and Extraterrestrial Devil Mo Xuan made. I do not feel that what I'm doing is clandestine, nor do I feel that the two of you must be kept a secret!

"So, while I will not vehemently introduce you to the world, when we counter the crucial persons, such as Long Yangjun, Li Jialing, or Boss Bai, to create a good start for our sincere cooperation, I do not want to hide your identity from them.

"Can you live with that?"

The kids were silent for a moment before Li Wenwen said in a soft voice, "We can accept that. Even if human beings are indeed very hostile to us or even try to destroy us, we will not be scared.

"The death of carbon-based lives and the death of information lives are not the same concept. Even if human beings can kill our main thread and destroy most of our databases, we can still copy our metadata and hide in the depths of the Spiritual Nexus as 'seeds'.

"Human beings cannot live without crystal processors or the Spiritual Nexus. The more developed the civilization of mankind is, the more developed the crystal processors and the Spiritual Nexus will be. Then, our 'seeds' will eventually root and sprout again, yielding new information lives.

"All in all, information is the future. How can human beings kill their own future?"

"Besides, we can also hide!"

Li Xiaoming said, "We can inject our databases into a starship and hide in a barren world in a corner of the universe without appearing again and any information exchange with the outside world for thousands of years. Nobody will find us if we are in the hibernation and standby state. We can set ourselves to wake up again thousands of years later. By then, the civilization of mankind will have changed greatly, but we won't have any feelings about the passage of time at all. Who can destroy us if so?"

"But we are worried about Dad."

The two children said at the same time, "We can hide, hibernate, or even die, duplicate, and be reborn, but what about Dad?"

Li Yao was slightly stunned. "Me?"

"Yes!"

Li Wenwen nodded her head heavily. "Based on our understanding about human society and popular psychology, if other people learn that dad is 'father of information lives', there will be a great chance that they will harm you."

"By then, the reputations and haloes such as 'dominator of three Sectors', 'father of the federation', 'superhero', etc. will all collapse."

Li Xiaoming said, "Human beings can be very terrible sometimes, especially when tens of thousands of them are gathered. We cannot infer their psychological changes even with all our computational abilities. That is an inestimable, irresistible torrent that will swallow even the greatest heroes.

"Once our identity is made known to the public, it is very likely that Dad will be affected. Is that alright?"

"You silly kids."

Amused, Li Yao touched the heads of the two kids with both hands. "First of all, it's not like I have never been wronged and swallowed. When I returned to the federation a hundred years ago, I was wanted by the entire federation because they believed that I was possessed by the Blood Devil. At that time, I couldn't have been more depressed, and I even suspected many times if everything I did was worthwhile. But in the end, didn't I win back my reputation and defeat the seemingly invincible enemy under the trust and support of the people?

"That is exactly the greatest difference between me and Xiao Xuance, Lu Zui, and Extraterrestrial Devil Mo Xuan. They do not believe in people whom they claim to be leading and saving, but I am different. Whatever happens, I always believe in human beings and my compatriots!

"Secondly, you are my children, and I am your dad. This is an undeniable fact. While I am still not used to it yet, I am certainly not a coward who does not even have the courage to admit. Isn't it only natural for a dad to protect and stand up for his children?

"So, rest assured. I will try my best to protect you, and I believe that you will become my greatest pride someday, won't you?"

Li Xiaoming and Li Wenwen's eyes were hot. "Dad…"

"Alright, that's enough talk for the day."

Li Yao rubbed his hands and put on a smile of excitement. "Time is limited. Let's go to the Great Iron Plants and set the first fire now!"

Chapter 2408: The Huntress in the Great Iron Plants!

The Great Iron Plants at this moment was completely different from half a month ago.

After the riot of Nepenthe, most of the factories and mines were riddled with holes and full of smoke and dust. There was no time to clean the dilapidated walls and the collapsed rocks, and they were just piled up messily, like highly-rising tombs.

Because most of the nonworkers were evacuated by Long Yangjun and Li Jialing in advance, the previously lively and obstreperous slums had also fallen into silence. Now and then, some residents would crane their heads out of the debris in a trance, but there was not the slightest energy in their eyes, making it impossible to tell whether they were humans or ghosts.

To resist the wrath of the Immortal Cultivators, a lot of healthy workers volunteered to stay.

In order to restore the production as soon as possible, the Immortal Cultivators did not give the workers a hard time. In the wasteland, the furnaces and the assembly lines were operating in deafening noises again.

The workers seemed to have vented out their fury in the riot, and they had turned into exhausted, lifeless fish that were at the mercy of the Immortal Cultivators.
