However, the Immortal Cultivators were still increasing the battle preparations in the Great Iron Plants.

Every day, cars full of soldiers and battle puppets would be shipped to the underground world, with armors and weapons that were carved with the glittering emblem of the lightning trident. There was even a protruding skull behind the lightning trident as the background.

The lightning trident with a skull background was a symbol of the elite troops in the Imperial Guards, and also one of death in the early years of the Imperium of True Human Beings.

Although the Imperial Guards had declined, they were certainly incomparable for the miscellaneous private troops of the Black Iron Group.

It was needless to say that the elite troops of the Imperial Guards also had some women who were wearing cloaks and hiding in shadows all the time.

They seemed to be spurting out black fog from their facial organs and the pores all over their bodies, giving everybody a chilling feeling.

Those women were the devil huntresses of the Tribunal of Extraterrestrial Devils, the greatest horror on the capital planet right now.

Every day, tremendous elites of the Imperial Guards and devil huntresses would arrive at the Great Iron Plants with a lot of excavators and magical equipment that was suitable for the underground environment. After taking a brief rest in the Great Iron Plants, they would go further down. It seemed that the Immortal Cultivators were indeed determined to capture Li Yao and Long Yangjun, the masterminds of the riot of Nepenthe, without bothering the cost.

The Great Iron Plants had completely turned into a weapon factory and a camp, like a hedgehog with quills all over the body. Naturally, nobody would worry that the mob of Nepenthe would stir trouble again.

But Li Minghui, owner of the Great Iron Plants and CEO of the Black Iron Group, was not delighted at all.

After the narrow escape, Li Minghui went to his new foster father—the elder of the Li family—and asked for reinforcements, hoping that the man could help him suppress the riot of Nepenthe and take back the Great Iron Plants.

But what happened later far exceeded his expectation and control, and the situation only escalated at a lightning-like speed. Even the elites of the Imperial Guards and the Tribunal of Extraterrestrial Devils, the most powerful forces on the surface of the planet right now, had been involved in this. It was possible that Queen Li Linghai, leader of the reformists, was interested in the issue in person!

Li Minghui felt terrible.

He knew that, although he was an expert in the Nascent Soul Stage, in the high-level competitions where the giants that were at least in the Divinity Transformation Stage attacked and swallowed each other, someone in the beginning level of the Nascent Soul Stage was just one of the most insignificant chess pieces that could be crumbled to death by anyone.

Take right now for example. The operation of the Great Iron Plants had completely gone out of control, and his enterprise seemed to have been "confiscated" by the reformists. Not only had he earned nothing, but he was also forced to cater to the experts in the Divinity Transformation Stage with his life savings, as well as the tough warriors of the Imperial Guards and the Tribunal of Extraterrestrial Devils, none of whom he dared to piss off. He also had to maintain the production of the Great Iron Plants with all his wisdom in order to provide the components that the "superiors" needed badly. The cost of a single day was already astronomical. It was like cutting flesh from his chest every day!

"It is always easy to invite a guest over, but it is often hard to make them leave."—Li Minghui finally understood what it meant now.

But what could he do?

The Imperium today was no longer the Imperium one year or half a year ago. Even the prime minister of the Imperium and the emperor had been killed uncannily, and even the four Kurfürst families were about to be driven out of the capital planet by the reformists. Faced with the great turbulences, Li Minghui was absolutely nothing, and it was already unbelievably lucky of him to have kept his head over his neck.

For example, the woman who was ensconced on his chair behind his desk in his office matter-of-factly, not even showing any intention of looking at him, was a person whom he couldn't afford infuriating at all.

Li Minghui bent like a half-frozen lobster, with cold sweat pouring out of his forehead and his back, while he put on a smile that looked even more terrible than crying, not even daring to breathe aloud.

It was not just because the woman's Cultivation was one major level higher than his and that she was a renowned, experienced expert in the Divinity Transformation Stage.

It was also because she was the leader of all devil huntresses, a central person of the reformists, the right hand of the queen, the master of the Tribunal of Extraterrestrial Devils—Yue Wushuang!

After the collapse of Prime Minister Dongfang Wang, the Association of Demon Hunters fell apart, and their leader Dongfang Mingyue went missing. Right now, the Tribunal of Extraterrestrial Devils was the only notable intelligence agency in the Imperium.

In the battles to banish the four Kurfürst families for the reformists, the Tribunal of Extraterrestrial Devils had made great contributions, too. Several elders of the Imperium simply vanished without a sound after they were invited to the Tribunal of Extraterrestrial Devils, not even leaving a strand of hair behind.

As the reformists completely occupied the capital planet, the Tribunal of Extraterrestrial Devils became a terrifying organization for all the Immortal Cultivators in the capital. Some of them even killed themselves the moment they learned that they were wanted by the Tribunal of Extraterrestrial Devils.

Now that the hostess of the Tribunal of Extraterrestrial Devils was having tea on his chair, Li Minghui could already be called a tough man since he was still able to stop his legs from shaking.

But of course, the more important reason was that "Moon Devil" Yue Wushuang did not pay any attention to him after all. She had never even really looked at him since she came to the underground world, completely regarding him as an unimportant dog that was begging for mercy.

Even right now, Yue Wushuang was still completely focused on the 3D battle videos floating above the desk.

The videos recorded the chaotic battles in the entire Great Iron Plants during the riot of Nepenthe half a month ago.

Although she had watched them for at least three hundred times, Yue Wushuang was still watching them so wholeheartedly that she was almost burying her face into the light beams.

It was especially so when Li Yao, Long Yangjun, and Li Jialing appeared in the picture. She would pause the video and observe carefully from every perspective, as if the mottled, fuzzy details were the best landscape in the world.

Watching the videos from the beginning to the end again, Yue Wushuang finally withdrew her cold eyes from the light beams. But instead of looking at Li Minghui, she simply covered her face with her hood and said casually, "Elder Li and Tutor Song have gone down for hunting under the guidance of your people. Any news from them?"

Elder Li was "Devilish Abyss" Li Jianyi.

Tutor Song was naturally "Star Breaker" Song Huanzhen.

The two of them were the best experts in the four Kurfürst families, but they were suppressed by Blackstar the Great in the battle below the royal palace and switched to the reformists' side.

The three experts in the Divinity Transformation Stage who had entered the underground world to suppress the riot of Nepenthe and pursue Li Yao, Li Jialing, and "Dongfang Mingyue" were Yue Wushuang, Li Jianyi, and Song Huanzhen.

After the three of them wounded Dongfang Mingyue, or Long Yangjun, Li Jianyi and Song Huanzhen led a team to chase after her.

Occupied with other affairs, Yue Wushuang stayed in the Great Iron Plants for supervision.

Hearing her voice, Li Minghui shuddered hard in his heart. He took a breath and said, "Replying to you, Leader Yue, not… not yet. There are a lot of gaps and sideways in the underground world, and it is difficult to locate the enemy if they are deliberately hiding. Also, my men hardly left the Great Iron Plants even though they are the locals of this place. So, so…"

"Huh?"

Yue Wushuang sniffed and said, "Why are you here if there is no news?"

Li Minghui was almost scared out of wits. Standing with his face pale for a long time, he finally asked respectfully, "I… I'm here to ask Leader Yue how the leaders of Nepenthe should be dealt with."

"It is not important. Do as you see fit."

Yue Wushuang said, "Nepenthe is just a small fish. What matters is the people who raised it. The heads of the captives are all emptied now. They are no longer of any value after all. You can deal with them however you want.

"Wait, what's the progress of your production? It seems to be 15% behind the objective, isn't it?"

"It's… It's 15.5% behind."

Li Minghui said with a bitter expression, "Leader Yue, please note that quite a few supportive factories were razed to the ground in the riot half a month ago, and half of the workers have been lost, too. It is already a miracle for the production to be restored so quickly. I've really done my best."

"I don't know production. That's your business. I don't care what you do, but even if you have to feed yourself to the furnaces, you will catch up to the schedule within a month. Otherwise, I will feed you into the furnaces in person."

Yue Wushuang said emotionlessly, "Drag out the Nepenthe leaders, including Xu Zhicheng, and execute them in front of all the workers.

"Then, you will tell the workers that all their crimes will be pardoned if they can accomplish the objective in one month. Their families will be pardoned, too. But if they can't, what happens to Xu Zhicheng today will happen to them a month later.

"Let them cherish the hard-earned job and do not force me to go on a killing spree. If that day does come, not even an ant will be left alive in the Great Iron Plants!"

Chapter 2409: Old Song

The prison of the Great Iron Plants had a history of almost a hundred years. It was almost established on the very same day the Great Iron Plants was born. Over the past hundred years, too many workers had been imprisoned, tortured, and killed in the place to intimidate other workers and sustain the tyranny of the Immortal Cultivators.

The dark, iron interrogational devices were stained with dried blood and suspicious fluids. Although they were unrecognizably dim, the stink of blood was still haunting the place, as if the unrested souls that had been trapped for a hundred years were still moaning.

At this moment, the prison was crammed with a hundred people, like a deteriorated can. All of them were heavily wounded, bleeding, and foaming hard. Collapsed on the ground like mud, they were no different from dead people had it not been for the feeble breath from their chest.

They were the central forces in the riot of Nepenthe.

Holding a rusted shackle, Xu Zhicheng crouched in a corner, and his chest was almost frozen, as if it were pressed with a boulder weighing a thousand tons.

But there was still the last brilliance in his widened eyes, blossoming and unwilling to die out.

"Brother Zhicheng—"

A stout man next to him, who was less heavily wounded, said in a low voice while looking after him, "Old Meng and Shitou didn't… didn't make it. They were just carried away by the Immortal Cultivators. We have completely lost the battle!"

Xu Zhicheng stared at the cracks on the ceiling of the prison cell. When the cracks were reflected in his eyes, they bloomed like flowers. He seemed to have ignored what his companion said, and it was not until a long time later that he finally said, "Old Si, I took a nap just now. Can you guess who appeared in my dream just now?"

"Old Si", the man who was as stout as a mount, was slightly stunned. "Little Bao and Little Qi?"

"No. The two boys of my family are before my eyes all the time when my eyes are open. Do I need to meet them in dreams?"

Xu Zhicheng grinned and struggled to smile. "I dreamt of your dad."

"What?" The stout man was dazed.

"I dreamt of my dad and your dad. Weren't they brothers by an oath?"

Xu Zhicheng said feebly, "Old Si, do you remember how naughty we were a long, long time ago, when we were still little and the old men were alive? We stole the leathers and the iron beads from the factory to make slingshots, and then we would use the slingshots to hit the b*ttocks of the girls who wore fancy clothes. There was one time when the victim went to our family. As it turned out, your father and my father worked together to catch us. We naturally tried to run away. We ran past quite a few factories and climbed on the tubes, but we were still blocked by them in the end. We were spanked so hard that we almost lost our own bottoms.

"After the two old men were tired of beating us, they sat together and drank happily, and the two of us just crouched on the ground, grimacing and rubbing our bottoms, not even able to sit or to cry. Do you still remember that?"

"Old Si", the stout man, was stunned for a long time. Every wrinkle on his face was spreading as he smiled in amusement and said, "Of course I do. How can I forget that? Now that I think of it, my bottom still hurts!"

"Hahahaha!"

Xu Zhicheng laughed. "However, after the two old men beat us hard, they offered each of us a carefully-made slingshot, with rails and scopes and everything, the second day. They were a hundred times better than the slingshots of all the other kids in the factory. The old men also asked us not to hit people or windows with the slingshots. We were only allowed to shoot the lizards in the forsaken tunnels around the Great Iron Plants."

"That's right!"

"Old Si" smiled even more brilliantly. "When it came to shooting the bottoms of girls, I was not as good as you, but when it came to shooting the lizards, you were no match for me at all, Brother Zhicheng!"

"Yes. You got dozens of lizards every time. Then, we would stab them with a skewer and roast them on fire. How succulent they were! The tasteless cans could not be compared to them at all!"

Xu Zhicheng closed his eyes while tears were flowing out. "Old Si, do you still remember where your slingshot went to in the end?"

"Old Si" shook his head and said, "It's been too many years. Who can remember that?"

"Yes, I've lost my slingshot, too."

Xu Zhicheng licked his dry lips and said in reminiscences, "But I can't forget the taste of the roasted lizards in my entire life. How greasy and enjoyable they were! I really would like to have a meal of them again, but it's a shame that all the mines and tunnels have been cleaned up during the decades of expansion of the Great Iron Plants. It's been a long time since any lizards were found, hasn't it?"

"Yes."

"Old Si" said, "The children today are truly unlucky. Living their entire life without having the privilege to enjoy a mouthful of roasted lizards, that's really… Well, Brother Zhicheng…"

Old Si was not sure what Xu Zhicheng meant.

After talking about his father, his slingshot, and the roasted lizards, Xu Zhicheng, the Grand Guardian of Nepenthe, was silent for a long time. He then coughed lightly and said, "Old Si, tell me the truth. Do you regret following Brother Zhicheng on this path?"

"Old Si" shook his head quickly without any hesitation. "I don't! I never have! We couldn't have survived the goddamn Immortal Cultivators after all, and we would die whether we fought or not. Right now, we have at least killed a lot of Immortal Cultivators before we die!

"Never in my life did I think that I would have a chance to fight the Immortal Cultivators to my heart's content before I die. It's certainly worth it even if I have to die a hundred times!

"Brother Zhicheng, I know what you are thinking. Just rest assured. Everybody knew that this was coming. None of the brothers here blame you. Even if everything were to restart from the beginning, we would've done the same and killed the Immortal Cultivators with you!"

Xu Zhicheng laughed. "Well said, kill all of the damn Immortal Cultivators! I know that nobody regrets doing that. I never have, either. But what I'm asking is something else—about Nepenthe. Do you regret establishing Nepenthe with me?"

The carefree smile of "Old Si" was frozen. He hesitated for a long time but managed to reply, "There… There's nothing to regret. What's done is done. Under the circumstances at that time, we didn't have a second choice at all. When an opportunity knocks on the door, you have to grasp it. If we hadn't done it, we would still be the mud under their feet in the next five hundred years!

"I don't regret it! I don't regret it!"

"But I am more or less regretful. No, I'm rather scared."

The cracks in Xu Zhicheng's eyes turned into glistening ripples as he mumbled, "I feel that I had a long, long sleep, but I did not have any dream, not even a nightmare. I was never scared until I woke up from the sleep.

"It's been… a very, very long time since I recalled my father and my mother.

"It's also been a long time since I remembered the things in my childhood. It's odd that, although our childhood was so insufferable, with barely any food and entertainment, and we had to help the adults with their work in the factories every day, I can't remember any of the exhaustion and sufferings now. All I remember are the happy things.

"There are also my two boys, the chaps who were as excellent as when we were little. I thought that the 'Serenity Arts' could help me completely forget the pain of losing them. But as it turned out, before I forgot the pain of losing them, I had almost forgotten all the happy times we spent together.

"Thankfully, the moment before I completely forgot them, somebody woke me and everybody up in time. It was really dangerous. So close. We were so close to forgetting everything. How to make slingshots, how to shoot the lizards… You haven't forgotten them yet, Old Si, have you?"

"No, even though I haven't done them for decades, I still remember it clearly."

"Old Si" said with great delight, "Brother Zhicheng, you might not know this, but shooting lizards is not just about watching and listening. There is a trick that I haven't passed on to anybody else yet. Let… Let me tell you about it today!"

Old Si leaned to Xu Zhicheng's ears and whispered for a moment. The more Xu Zhicheng listened, the wider his eyes became. He exclaimed in an epiphany, "Old Si, that's really something! You are really an evil boy!"

The two old men who were both more than fifty years old burst into laughter, confusing the other prisoners in the cell.

"Old Si—"

Xu Zhicheng laughed for such a long time that his dirty tears were running out on his face. He asked again, "Your daughter and grandson escaped, didn't they? Do you have their news?"

"Yes, they both did. There's no news about them. They must've evaded the Immortal Cultivators."

"Old Si" replied, "Nowadays, no news is the best news. We've stalled the enemy long enough here for them to escape to the places deeper where the Immortal Cultivators are unwilling to go. As for the future, it… it will all depend on their own luck!"

"That's good, that's good. Just rest assured. While it is not easy for a human to live, it won't be easy for them to die as long as they can grit their teeth. Our ancestors persisted below the ground for ten thousand years. Maybe our offspring can persist for another ten thousand years. Who knows?"

Xu Zhicheng sighed and said, "Old Si, neither of us has forgotten our fathers and the good old days, and your children have successfully escaped, too. So, who says that we have lost the battle? In my opinion, we have won. At the very least, our children will no longer suffer the slavery and torment of the Great Iron Plants, will they?"

"Old Si" was stunned for a moment. Then he slapped his thigh and grinned. "Yes, you're right, Brother Zhicheng. We have won. Our children will never be enslaved and tortured by the Great Iron Plants and the Immortal Cultivators again!"

Hearing the answer that he wanted to hear, Xu Zhicheng closed his eyes in satisfaction and began to whistle something fuzzily.

His younger workmates did not know what exactly he was singing. Only the older generation, including "Old Si", after listening for a long time, finally recalled that the rhythm sounded like a song that their fathers used to sing to them a long, long time ago when they were still kids.

The lyrics had been lost in the river of time, but the familiar rhythm was still deeply buried in everyone's heart.

Huala!

The door of the dark cell was opened violently, and a cold voice barged in. "Everybody, out!"

What responded to the demand was the old lullaby that everybody was echoing together.

Chapter 2410: Arriving Attackers!

In the square at the center of the Great Iron Plants that was facing the geothermal well, illuminated by the scorching flames and observed by countless workers, the hundred leaders of Nepenthe were escorted out.

After days of consecutive interrogation, their bodies had been damaged to different extents. Some were only able to stagger forward by supporting each other. Their heavy manacles left earsplitting noises and feeble sparks when they were dragged on the ground.

However, there was neither the fear for the upcoming death nor the nonchalance when they practiced the Serenity Arts on their faces; only vague delight and relief could be seen now.

Everybody whistled the old and warm tunes while they walked to their doom, hand in hand and shoulder to shoulder, leaving their final smiles to the workers around.

Different from the soul-stirring battle hymn that Li Yao played above the Great Iron Plants more than half a month ago, the tunes were extremely low and lethargic, like a piece of coal that had not entirely burnt out in the amber, but they were filled with unimaginably touching power.

After the failure of the riot, the workers who volunteered to stay had already lost their hope. Dragging their tired bodies, they sent themselves to the assembly lines and the mines at the mercy of the Immortal Cultivators.

But what they were seeing before their eyes—the smile of Xu Zhicheng, "Old Si", and the rest of them, as well as their soft tunes—woke up the dormant minds of the workers again. It was almost like a handful of sugar mixed with salt, bringing back complicated feelings again.

There was no telling who started it first, but very soon, all the workers were whistling in a low voice, echoing with Xu Zhicheng and the rest of them.

Perhaps they had long forgotten the lyrics. Perhaps even the rhythms they remembered were blurred. Some of the young workers had even never heard of the rhythms before. However, the rhythms had been carved on their genes since a very long time ago, and all they needed to do was to summon their courage and stop constricting their throats. Then, the music would naturally flow out on its own.

The warm tunes, however, gave the Immortal Cultivators frigid coldness.

Li Minghui had never seen such a scene before. Even the captivating battle hymn in the riot of Nepenthe half a month ago seemed not nearly as creepy and terrifying as the workers' chant that bordered on murmur right now.

"No singing! No singing!"

The Immortal Cultivators waved their electromagnetic whips, raising dazzling balls of lightning in the midair, while they shouted at the loudest of their voices.

The tunes, however, were like a peaceful ocean that soon drowned the few tiny sparks.

Now that the workers already did not have any hope, they were certainly not scared of the threat of the Immortal Cultivators anymore.

Li Minghui, on the other, did not really dare to kill all of the disobedient workers. Most of the workers had escaped, and new workers and spiritual puppets were not so easy to be gathered. Those people were the only available labor in his hands right now. It was already terribly difficult to accomplish the production missions issued by Yue Wushuang. If he irrigated the workers, resulting in the failure of the missions, even he would get killed because of those lowly swine!

Li Minghui could only grit his teeth and ordered his subordinates and the battle puppets to try to separate the workers and not give them a chance to gather. Then, he asked the executioners to do their job as quickly as possible in case there was any more trouble. He had prepared an elaborate, thrilling speech, but it seemed now that he could only give it after the captives' heads were chopped off.

Such was exactly the situation in the Great Iron Plants, where desolation and chaos coexisted contradictorily like an ocean before a storm or a volcano that was about to have an eruption.

Everything was observed clearly by the ambushers who were hiding in an inconspicuous mine on the southwest side of the Great Iron Plants.

The locations of the new firing points in the Great Iron Plants, as well as the number and patrolling routes of the elites of the Imperial Guards and the devil huntresses, turned into waterfall-like data and glittering maps that popped up on the crystal processors next to the ambushers.

The young man with a headful of golden hair, who was carrying a heavy anti-shield sniper rifle, locked onto one firing point after another and set up the corresponding shooting parameters. With dense sweat leaking out of his forehead, he asked anxiously, "Sister Long, Grand Guardian Xu and the rest of them are about to be executed by the Immortal Cultivators. How much longer do we have to wait before rescuing them?"

"I have told you countless times on the way here, and I will say it one last time. We are not here to rescue but to kill. If you want to rescue anyone, go down and do it on your own!"

Long Yangjun had piled and knitted her long black hair on the back of her head. Biting a bluish dagger in her mouth, her face was as cold as iron. "There are so many elites of the Imperial Guards and the devil huntresses that are supervised by Yue Wushuang, the leader of the Tribunal of Extraterrestrial Devils. How do you propose to rescue them?

"Don't ever underestimate Yue Wushuang. Although she has never been the most famous expert in the Divinity Transformation Stage over the past decades, it is mostly because of the feebleness of the Tribunal of Extraterrestrial Devils.

"Judging from her battle performance in the past few months, she is certainly a mysterious, unpredictable, and brutal warrior. The achievements of the Tribunal of Extraterrestrial Devils on the studies of tenebrum energy in the past hundreds of years have all been focused on her.

"How can you rescue so many workers and Nepenthe believers right under her nose?

"It will be the greatest miracle if we can find an opportunity to kill her. As to whether or not the Immortal Cultivators' control over the Great Iron Plants will collapse, and how many of the workers and Nepenthe believers can escape, it will be up to themselves!"

Li Jialing said in a hurry, "But—"

"No but!"

Long Yangjun glared at the young man. "Do you think that I like coming back to this place? Do you think that I've returned to the Great Iron Plants that is heavily guarded by the Immortal Cultivators because I have nothing better to do or that I have lost my mind?

"It's only because I'm heavily wounded and running out of supplies and with the two experts in the Divinity Transformation Stage tailing us closely that I have to come back here to try my luck!

"Listen up, we barely have any ammunition, fuel, and spiritual energy left right now. The best they can support is a quick battle. We absolutely can't afford a war of attrition with the Immortal Cultivators.

"Right now, only the Great Iron Plants has stored enough resources that can refill us effectively. Also, nobody expects that we are bold enough to come back here. So, this is actually the safest place. That is the reason why we are here. The only reason!

"Over the past few days, we did everything we could to slightly get rid of the pursuit of the two experts in the Divinity Transformation Stage and get us the precious opportunity, but the distractions we deployed cannot deceive them for long. Once it is seen through, they will immediately realize what is going on and come back to the Great Iron Plants with reinforcement as quickly as possible. We have only six hours at best. In the worst-case scenario, they will march out of our back in only five minutes!

"I stand no chance at all in a one-versus-three battle. So, we have to take down 'Moon Devil' Yue Wushuang before the other two experts in the Divinity Transformation Stage show up. Then, we will have enough supplies to prepare ourselves for the new enemies.

"After taking down Yue Wushuang, there will be chances for me to deal with and even defeat the two experts in the Divinity Transformation Stage. Otherwise, we can just hang ourselves now!

"All in all, now is not the time for petty kindness. Remember your mission and pray that I can kill Yue Wushuang smoothly. In that case, some of the people down there might still survive, but if you are still absentminded and hesitant, you will save none of them!"

"I-I understand. I'm sorry, Sister Long."

Li Jialing lowered his head in embarrassment. After a while, he asked earnestly, "Sister Long, how are your wounds?"

"Not enough to kill me."

Her face pale and expressionless, Long Yangjun said, one word after another, with dense coldness beaming out of her eyes, "Long Yangjun is certainly not someone that any random person can kill. Three of them are far from enough. Let it be ten!"

"I wonder where Brother Yao went exactly."

Hesitating for a moment, Li Jialing said in a low voice, "It would be great if he were here."

"Don't mention the name of the monster!"

Long Yangjun immediately burst into fury. "Had I not been… deceived by his fancy words, how would I have fallen into this damn swirl that is even drowning me now?

"At first, I kindly asked him to escape, but he stubbornly refused it and decided to fight. Then, after we were all involved in this, it was he who ran off stealthily first, wasn't he? What a scoundrel!

"Let me tell you, the greatest mistake of my life is getting to know your brother and even mistaking him for a good friend for a while, and the second greatest mistake was that I was idiotic enough to believe him! Damn it, I truly feel ashamed now that I think of it. What exactly got into me back then and made me exactly like him?"

"Well…"

Li Jialing thought for a moment and said, "It was not exactly Sister Long's fault, but mostly because Brother Yao was too cunning and maudlin."

"So, if he ever shows up again—"

Long Yangjun declared angrily, "I will never give him any chance to talk but will simply slash him into pieces. Then, the world will be in peace!"

Not knowing how to respond, Li Jialing could only focus his attention on the scope.

On the scope, the executioner wearing iron masks had already activated their chainswords. The coldly-shining sawtooth was revolving quickly.

Xu Zhicheng and the other captives were pressed to the ground by the battle puppets. In the competition against iron, their backs were even straighter.

Li Jialing swallowed hard, and his palms were wet. Every muscle all over his body was tightened, and he couldn't hold back the urge of shooting at the foreheads of the executioners.

For some reason, he thought of the picture where Li Yao fought ten thousand on his own in blood again.

Did Brother Yao really flee in panic?

Was the miracle of a one-billionth chance not going to happen after all, and they could not rescue anyone?

The executioners raised the chainswords high and aimed at Grand Guardian Xu Zhicheng first.

Seeing Xu Zhicheng's smile on the scope, Li Jialing felt that his fingers were screaming and exploding.

"Do it now."

Right then, Long Yangjun patted his bottom softly. "I've already locked onto Yue Wushuang's position!"

Chapter 2411: Cut the Head of the Snake!

Li Jialing's heart turned from extremely scorching to extremely calm instantly.

Helical brilliance was slowly circling inside his eyes, eventually condensing into two brilliant stars that shined at the same pace with his breath and heartbeat.

On the scope, the veins on the arms of the executioner wearing a metal mask were already protruding high like snakes. He was obviously exerting his strength.

Under the saber, Xu Zhicheng closed his eyes and exhaled in great relief, facing the destiny that did not have any hope or miracles.

Li Jialing pulled the trigger.

The trigger had been modified to a very sensitive point. It was more like touching than pulling when the attack rune arrays inside the barrel of the gun were activated to the maximum. The tiny force field immediately expanded dozens of times, pushing out the explosive bullet that contained infinite fury!

The bullet that was thicker and longer than his finger, which almost looked like a mini cannonball, covered the few kilometers in between all of a sudden without leaving any sound, moving like a rushing ripple.

The bullet had been crafted by Li Yao in person. Not only did it boast the features of anti-shield, anti-equipment, and anti-interferences, but it also entailed certain stealth and anti-detection abilities. It was not sensed by the Immortal Cultivators until it broke into the defense rune array around the Great Iron Plants that had been temporarily established.

But there was no time!

Hiu!

Dazzling light spurted out of the rear of the bullet, further doubling the speed, when it turned into a flash and lunged at the executioner who was raising the saber high!

The Immortal Cultivators responsible for the security of the prison and the suppression of the workers were mostly Li Minghui's unworthy subordinates. Yue Wushuang's elites couldn't have cared less about the lives of the swine and the ants.

Therefore, when the bullet leaped out of the void and revealed the most aggressive appearance, nothing could possibly stop it from roaring.

BOOM!

The executioner meant to dodge, but there was no time. The bullet hit him right in the chest. Faced with the bullet that Li Yao had crafted carefully with super high-purity marrow crystals, the spiritual shield that he condensed in a hurry was no different from a piece of paper. After a blood-freezing explosion, the entire upper half body of the executioner was blown up into pieces, showering on all the captives and the onlookers with the broken flesh.

"This is—"

Dumbfounded, everybody watched the lower half body of the executioner cramping for a long time in disbelief, before it finally fell to the ground.

Xu Zhicheng was the first to jump up. The relief and desperation on his face were entirely gone, and unprecedented flames beamed out.

"Enemy incoming!"

Li Minghui's subordinates had just been through a cruel battle half a month ago and were just intimidated by Li Yao, Long Yangjun, and Li Jialing back then. They were still not entirely recovered from fear yet and screamed loudly.

Hiu! Hiu! Hiu! Hiu! Hiu!

Li Jialing did not intend to stop at all after he shot out the first bullet. When the bullet was still flying, he had opened fire at all the other targets that he had locked onto, shooting out all the nineteen bullets in the magazine.

Just now, he had aimed for a long time and determined all the shooting parameters. Also, the targets he chose were almost all sentry posts, fixed firing points, and immobile crystal tanks. Naturally, he could not miss them at all.

Hardly had the Immortal Cultivators been back to themselves from the shock of the executioner's horrible death when their torsos, heads, or at least arms were blown away. The bullets that Li Yao crafted in person were certainly not something that any random armor or spiritual shield could resist. Even the crystal tanks protected by the reactive armor was still left with a hole the size of a little finger. Then, the metal rays exploded radially inside the tank with an extremely high temperature. There was no place to hide at all, and all the Immortal Cultivators inside the tank were shot apart into pieces!

For a moment, the Immortal Cultivators under Li Minghui's command were all fleeing in panic, suppressed by only one sniper rifle.

BOOM! BOOM! BOOM! BOOM! BOOM! BOOM! BOOM!

The elites of the Imperium Guards and the devil huntresses brought by Yue Wushuang, however, were certainly incomparable for those people. Even though they might be at similar levels, their training, battle experiences, and mutual trust were much better than Li Minghui's subordinates. Almost at the same moment when the third bullet flew out of the barrel, a lot of the Imperial Guards and the devil huntresses had located Li Jialing. Hundreds of crystal cannons raised fiery snakes that drowned the rocks where Li Jialing and Long Yangjun were at like a torrential rain.

In terms of firepower coverage, even the most advanced sniper rifle could not be compared with a hundred cannons.

The forsaken mine where the two ambushers were hiding were soon reduced into a vulnerable beehive that was riddled with holes before it completely collapsed.

Dozens of experts of the Imperial Guards and the Tribunal of Extraterrestrial Devils, on the other hand, dashed at the forsaken mine at the earliest chance, blocking every possible entrance and exit of the cave.

Thankfully, the moment Li Jialing shot out all the bullets, Long Yangjun dragged him back, and the two of them hid in the deepest part of the cave.

The caves around the Great Iron Plants were complicated and interconnected. Nobody except for the old locals such as Xu Zhicheng could know the specific route.

When Long Yangjun arranged for the Nepenthe believers to evacuate a few days back, she had figured out every detail of the mines and tunnels around with the old locals. So, by the time the enemy came at their first shooting location, they had already moved on to the second spot several kilometers away.

BOOM! BOOM! BOOM! BOOM!

The first shooting location had naturally been planted with tremendous crystal bombs, whose heat and blast consumed quite a few Imperial Guards and devil huntresses that failed to dodge in time. Both Long Yangjun and Li Jialing had been hanging around with Li Yao long enough to pick up his bad habit of stuffing a few crystal bombs into the corners wherever they went.

The two of them had been setting their ambush outside of the Great Iron Plants for two hours. Before they shot out the first bullet, they had already made careful arrangements in tremendous mines outside.

At this moment, Long Yangjun activated the magical equipment they deployed in the mines previously, and abundant smoke grenades whose targets had been preset were immediately thrown at the Great Iron Plants like hails. In squealing sounds, the Great Iron Plants was immediately enshrouded in smoke, turning into a world of chaos where one could not see their own finger.

"Remember, your mission is to sabotage!"

Long Yangjun grabbed Li Jialing's ear and left a lot of heavy magical equipment for him. "Try your best to distract the Immortal Cultivators and create an opportunity for me to kill Yue Wushuang! Only by killing Yue Wushuang will we have a one percent opportunity of rescuing ourselves and the people here. Do not be a fool, and do not be blinded by petty kindness, or you won't be able to save anybody!"

"Got it!"

Li Jialing bellowed. Every cluster of golden hair was rising aggressively like sharp daggers, surrounded by cracking electric arcs that further added to the intimidating vibe. What happened in the previous half month made the well-talented young man grow significantly yet again. The strength of the Imperial Fire Pearl had been completely digested and absorbed by him, transforming him from a boy gradually into a real man.

Even if Brother Yao is not here, I still have to fight on!

Li Jialing thought to himself. Biting his lips hard, he activated the three-phase transformative crystal suit that Li Yao built for him, carried the heavy magical equipment that was even thicker than the crystal suit, and ran where the Imperial Guards and the devil huntresses were densest.

In a head-on clash, the brave ones were more likely to win. His damn fate had blessed him with so many things, and he would certainly not die in such a place.

He was Li Jialing. His legend had only just begun!

Hearing Li Jialing's roars coming from the smoke, mixed with the sounds of exploding bullets, spluttering shrapnel, twisted crystal suits, and screaming enemies, Long Yangjun set the crystal suit into the stealth mode and disappeared into the deepest part of the forsaken mine like a ghost.

She zigzagged in the mine and soon found a door covered in dust.

Plucking the crude wood plate, she found a small tunnel back there that had been excavated in secret.

Through the secret tunnel, it was possible to enter the Great Iron Plants directly.

The secret tunnel had been excavated by the workers for smuggling purposes originally. Thorough examinations were performed every day before and after the work to make sure that the workers did not steal the assets of the factories.

However, the days of the workers were really difficult. When they could not make ends meet, they would smuggle some faulty products and the leftovers and sell to the merchants in exchange for the agricultural products that the merchants brought from deeper underground. It was sort of an improvement for their poor life.

There were a lot of such secret tunnels in the factories of the Great Iron Plants. Generally speaking, as long as the production missions were accomplished in time, the Immortal Cultivators never bothered to care about such trivia. They even intentionally indulged the workers to do that as some sort of "relief valve" to reduce the tension.

As the Grand Guardian of Nepenthe and a miner with decades of experience, Xu Zhicheng naturally knew most of the secret tunnels.

When Long Yangjun craned her head out of the exit of the secret tunnel, she was only one kilometer away from the office where Yue Wushuang was commanding the battle.

It was exactly the moment when the smoke in the Great Iron Plants was densest.

The workers had already fallen into a mess. Some were running about like headless flies, and some emboldened themselves to rescue Xu Zhicheng and the rest of them inside the smoke. Their shackles were ringing aloud, adding to the anxiety in the atmosphere.

Li Minghui's subordinates were more or less overwhelmed, too. Although they did not suffer new attacks after the first round of shooting, they only felt even more pressure, as if a god of death could emerge out of the dark smoke any moment and claim their lives that should've been lost half a month ago.

It was indeed possible for Long Yangjun to take down all of Li Minghui's subordinates in half a minute.

But she did not do that. Instead, she floated toward Yue Wushuang's office like a weightless ghost, determined to catch the enemies' leader first!

Chapter 2412: Long Yangjun against Yue Wushuang!

At this moment, Yue Wushuang was observing the chaotic situation under the protection of three crystal tanks and a dozen devil huntresses.

She was still wearing a huge cloak, but behind the cloak was a fine crystal suit. Her mouth, nose, ears, and eyes had been covered carefully too, without leaving any weakness.

Having been the leader of the Tribunal of Extraterrestrial Devils for decades, and with her prowess in stealth, ambush, and assassination, Yue Wushuang certainly would not do such an idiotic thing as to enter the dangerous tunnels or the mines recklessly.

The dozen devil huntresses around her would not be separated from her in any case, either.

Realizing that it was impossible to wait for a perfect opportunity, Long Yangjun had no choice except to attack!

Shua!

Her skin turned clear all of a sudden, as if she were made of translucent glass that was absorbing the light around, melting herself into the environment.

Like a block of ice without the slightest impurities that had been thrown into the water, it was impossible to distinguish her at all without careful sensing.

Even her spiritual waves had been concealed to almost nothing. It was barely perceivable in the scorching fire and the intense smoke.

Her feet pressed the ground. The air seemed to be seawater that had a high density for her, as she turned into a fast and agile mermaid, cutting into the crowd through the gaps of the elite Imperial Guards and devil huntresses.

Quite a few devil huntresses were observing the surroundings in full wariness only one foot away from her. Some people had even activated high-sensitive detectors, scanning the environment again and again. But none of them noticed Long Yangjun.

There was only the last fifty meters between Long Yangjun and Yue Wushuang.

She opened her fingers, and the fingernails grew quickly like crystals, soon into ten thin but sharp daggers.

Yue Wushuang had been staring at Li Jialing's direction all the time. It was not until this moment that she finally sensed an uncanny wave and glanced at where Long Yangjun was at.

After only a quick glance, Yue Wushuang's pupils were reduced to the size of needle tips. Without any sign, she fell backward straightly, stabbing her feet into the rocks and raising thousands of cobbles, before she shattered them into burning powder with her spiritual energy and sprayed them forward. In the meantime, she glided underneath the chassis of a crystal tank with the counterforce and hid behind the crystal tank!

Before any of the other devil huntresses realized what was going on, a daunting shadow had already appeared in the cobbles and burning powder that Yue Wushuang raised with her feet.

It all happened too fast. With unstoppable auras condensed in the darkness, Long Yangjun jumped to the front of the crystal tank where Yue Wushuang was hiding. In ten dreamy, glittering curves, her fingers ripped the crystal tank, which weighed dozens of tons, into pieces more than easily. The elite Imperial Guards inside ended up in pieces before they had the opportunity to even scream.

Hu!

The sloppy black robe that Yue Wushuang had been wearing was tossed out to Long Yangjun like a wriggling cloud. After a bam, colorful, corrosive gas was flowing at Long Yangjun's crystal suit.

Long Yangjun's body immediately turned into a dozen broken crystals, and the broken crystals then turned into rushing streaks of brilliance. Without meeting the least hindrance, they dodged at Yue Wushuang's black robe and venomous fog and attacked Yue Wushuang's eyes, throat, heart, and abdomen.

Clink! Clink! Clink! Clink! Clink!

Dangerous yet exceptionally glamorous sparks were bursting out between the daggers of the two heads of special agents who were equally adept at stealth, ambush, and assassination.

Yue Wushuang had been on the losing side almost since the first second. After persisting for only three seconds, her left wrist had a hole from being stabbed by Long Yangjun, and the dagger that looked like a crescent moon fell to the ground.

Long Yangjun turned around and kicked at Yue Wushuang's throat, with bluish brilliance spurting out of her heel.

Yue Wushuang's loyal and fearless devil huntresses, however, lunged at her and forced her to slightly adjust the direction. The sharp brightness that should've cut Yue Wushuang's throat left intimidating arrows of blood when it wandered among the critical parts of the many devil huntresses like a dragon.

"You've been hiding your strength!"

Yue Wushuang stepped back into the protection of the devil huntresses, covering her throat with both hands, her face pale.

She was not surprised at Long Yangjun's arrival, but she was truly taken aback by Long Yangjun's sudden increased strength. "In the last two battles, you hid your strength and did not carry out your highest combat ability at all. You even got wounded on purpose… Why?"

"Idiot. If I had not been wounded on purpose, would you have been stupid enough to think that two guys in the Divinity Transformation Stage were enough to hunt me down?"

Long Yangjun grinned hideously and blinked past a few devil huntresses like a transparent octopus in a deep ocean.

The few demon huntresses were all frozen as if they had been spellbound. Very soon, their heads were all folded backward weirdly, and blood rose to the sky like blossoming flowers.

Long Yangjun shook the bloodstains off her fingernails and bent, evading the crossfire of two crystal tanks before she dashed to the top of one of the crystal tanks from the blind angle.

No matter how the crystal tank shifted the turret, it was impossible to lock on Long Yangjun. In the end, Long Yangjun stomped on the reactive armor of the crystal armor heavily, gaining a higher speed by the explosive force of the reactive armor. While kicking the crystal tank to almost a hundred meters away, she was able to dash to Yue Wushuang's front like a lightning.

Yue Wushuang raised the seemingly stupid question only to buy herself more time. She was actually retrieving the Colossus in her cosmos ring in an inconspicuous gesture.

Long Yangjun, however, had foreseen her plan. Three sharpest streaks of brilliance were snapped out from her fingertips and slashed at Yue Wushuang's right hand brutally. In the meantime, she kicked Yue Wushuang right in the chest, forcing Yue Wushuang to abort the retrieval and defend herself. Even so, she was still kicked to almost fifty meters back, and her pace was completely disrupted.

Long Yangjun was in the level where she could fight Blackstar the Great in a head-on battle for hundreds of rounds. Had it not been for her concerns that she was now by herself and it would be troublesome if she were heavily wounded or ran out of supplies, even three experts in the Divinity Transformation Stage together wouldn't necessarily be enough to defeat her.

Right now, she was determined to fight a bloody way of survival, and her enemy was only one person in the Divinity Transformation Stage. Naturally, she was unstoppable.

"You are not Dongfang Mingyue!"

Yue Wushuang's bones were almost all broken because of her kick. She vomited blood crazily and changed her face color. Even her voice became extremely panicked as she said, "Neither the Dongfang family nor the Association of Demon Hunters can raise such a monster like you. Who are you exactly?"

Long Yangjun sniffed, not bothering to talk to Yue Wushuang anymore. Cracking noises echoed from her elbows, and two bone blades extended out of the gaps of her crystal suit. The same bone blades also stabbed out of her toes and the back of her heels. She seemed to become an incarnation of the God of Death made of blades, and nothing could possibly stop her at all. All the Imperial Guards and devil huntresses standing in her way fell like the wheat that was being blown by the wind!

Without any hesitation, Yue Wushuang ran to where the crowd was densest in the Great Iron Plants.

In the obscure smoke, she was like a moon blocked by the clouds, hiding herself exactly like Long Yangjun did just now, and not even caring about the lives of her subordinates.

She seemed to be cruel and heartless and running shamefully, but Long Yangjun felt that her heart was heavier and heavier.

Yue Wushuang truly deserved to be one of the best special agents of the Imperium of True Human Beings. She would not make any lowly mistake or give Long Yangjun any chance.

She had realized that the greatest disadvantage of Long Yangjun and Li Jialing was that they were outnumbered. As long as she was not decapitated by them, it was absolutely impossible for the two of them to kill all of the Imperial Guards and the devil huntresses.

Long Yangjun's level was not overwhelmingly higher than Yue Wushuang's. It was one thing to defeat Yue Wushuang, but it was a whole other thing to kill her. The latter was ten times more difficult than the former!

Therefore, while she seemed to be slaughtering the Imperial Guards and the devil huntresses without any trouble, the cost was that her spiritual energy and ammunitions were being consumed quickly. Also, incessant enemies were coming at her. When one of them was down, two more would take his place.

The Imperial Guards and the devil huntresses had all been trained by Blackstar the Great. Their loyalty and determination were far better than that of common Immortal Cultivators.

With the precious moment of rest, Yue Wushuang would be able to summon her Colossus.

She had failed to kill Yue Wushuang in round one. If the enemy successfully summoned her Colossus, it would be major trouble!

Ice cones were almost erupting out of Long Yangjun's eyes as she stared at Yue Wushuang's back that was about to disappear and pursued it.

Whoever dared to stand in her way would immediately be chopped into halves together with their armor.

Such a way of fighting had a high demand for spiritual energy. She soon sensed that she was breathing heavily, and the crystal processor was beeping in alarms too.

She did not give Yue Wushuang an opportunity to retrieve the Colossus at the cost of such great consumptions, and she was about to catch the target at the risk of being bombarded by crossfire when Yue Wushuang suddenly seized something that was moving vigorously from the darkness and tossed it at her!

It was a human!

Long Yangjun meant to do the same and cut the guy into halves.

The person who was tossed over by Yue Wushuang, however, was unexpectedly agile. He screamed but dodged her fatal attack, apparently much more capable than the Imperial Guards and the devil huntresses!

He was Li Minghui, the proprietor of the Great Iron Plants and an expert in the Nascent Soul Stage!

It was truly a bad day for Li Minghui. Having witnessed Long Yangjun's toughness half a month ago when he was almost killed by her, Li Minghui did not intend to stand in Long Yangjun's way at all this time. He simply held himself back and pretended that he was commanding the soldiers and maintaining the order.

But as it turned out, Yue Wushuang sensed where he was and blinked to him, using him as a human shield.

The expert in the Nascent Soul Stage, who was unparalleled in the Great Iron Plants, was naturally nothing in front of the aggressive Long Yangjun. It was already his best to have avoided the first attack, and he could not stop Long Yangjun from tearing apart his breastplate and his bones and crumbling his heart with brute force!

The CEO of the Black Iron Group died just like that, like a dog that could not bark anymore.

However, during the moment when Long Yangjun was blocked by Li Minghui, Yue Wushuang's Cosmos Ring was finally able to spurt out the dazzling brilliance, and the Colossus "Moon Devil" crawled out from the ripples of the void!

Chapter 2413: Heavenly Crystal Against Moon Devil!

"Damn it!"

What flooded out of the Cosmos Ring together with the components of the Colossus were more than twenty balls made of strengthened porcelains. Under the boost of Yue Wushuang's spiritual energy, they exploded and unleashed an overwhelming blast that was turned into a visible twisting barrier, blocking Long Yangjun's attack.

Seizing the opportunity, Yue Wushuang crawled into the spiritual residence of the Colossus and integrated with the "Moon Devil"!

The deep blue eyes of Moon Devil immediately glittered, and creepy laughter echoed from its throat that was as narrow as a snake.

The Moon Devil was a rarely-seen Colossus in the shape of a beast. It looked like a gigantic lizard, with two short forelegs and a thick, strong, long tail that had the shape of a shocking sawtooth. The tail could even be folded and curled all the way to the head, turning the entire Colossus into an enormous metal wheel.

Now that the enemy had retrieved the Colossus, Long Yangjun would surely fail if she were to fight on like this. She had no choice except to blink into the crowd of the Imperial Guards and the devil huntresses who were in a mess. Jumping quickly and unleashing the dazzling brilliance, she swallowed the lives of dozens of Immortal Cultivators like an ocean, before she summoned the "Heavenly Crystal", her own Colossus, under the cover of the broken limbs and the stinky storm!

Almost at the same moment when she crawled into the spiritual residence of the Heavenly Crystal, Yue Wushuang's Moon Devil had already crouched into a sharp wheel and crashed at her destructively.

BOOM!

The whole Great Iron Plants was shaking because of the collision of the two Colossus. The deafening explosions ripped apart the eardrums of everybody around. The overwhelming blast even blew away quite a few crystal tanks, which did not collapse heavily and explode into giant fireballs until they rolled dozens of rounds in the midair.

Long Yangjun's Cultivation and the level of her Colossus were higher than the enemy's after all. Even though Yue Wushuang had attacked vehemently and she only defended herself in a hurry, she was still not hurt.

However, her Colossus was at least five floors high now that it had assumed the complete form. No matter how she covered it and dodged, it still became the center of everyone's attention.

After all, the underground space where the Great Iron Plants was at was limited, however vast it might be. Also, complicated factories, high heaps of components, and dilapidated buildings were everywhere. It really wasn't the best place for a Colossus' performance.

It was true that Colossi normally boasted advanced technologies in interference. But since the distance was too short, the enemy did not need the assistance of any ancillary magical equipment and could aim at her only with their naked eye.

Under the instructions of the gloating Yue Wushuang, all the Imperial Guards and devil huntresses aimed at Long Yangjun's Colossi. Explosive bullets, mini flying swords, crystal cannons, heal cannons, machine guns, destructive cannons, fiery ballistae… Thousands of fiery lines were raised from them and rained upon the Heavenly Crystal overwhelmingly, riddling the spiritual shield of the Colossus with holes. Ripples were spreading out nonstop, and it looked like a pond in a storm.

Although most of the Immortal Cultivators were much lower than Long Yangjun in terms of level, and the gap between common crystal suits and a Colossus was even greater, the changes in quantity could still lead to a change in quality when there were enough of them.

One attack from a crystal suit to a Colossus was like stinging someone with a needle softly.

But if ten thousand needles were stabbing someone brutally at the same time, nobody would be able to take it.

It was exactly the reason why Long Yangjun did not summon the Colossus sooner. The object was too huge, and there was no place to hide at all. She could only grit her teeth through the stormy attacks.

Getting to the bottom of it, no magical equipment was "invincible", and they all had their most suitable environment. The Colossus was an ultimate weapon developed for universal battles. In space that was boundless and frictionless, a Colossus could easily be made into a behemoth dozens of meters tall as long as the soul and spiritual energy of the user could bear it.

In a space battle, the units lower than ten meters tall, dozens of meters tall, and hundreds of meters tall did not have much difference in terms of the odds of being hit by the enemy. Taking factors such as ammunition load, fighting duration, and momentary combat ability output into consideration, the Colossi ranging between fifteen and fifty meters tall had the highest survival abilities on the battlefield.

Even though they were occasionally caught in starships that had limited room, they could fight a bloody way out unstoppably with their destructive power. It was often the case that the Colossus was not damaged at all when the starship it snuck in had exploded.

Fighting below the ground, on the other hand, was completely different.

The enormous body size of the Colossus became a fatal disadvantage.

The Heavenly Crystal and the Moon Devil were like two rhinos, with eyes bloodshot in fury, that had been locked in a narrow cage, without any room to retreat to.

The problem was that the rhino that was "Moon Devil" also had the help of countless agile rats that had sharp teeth. How could she fight the battle?

With everything coming to the point, Long Yangjun had no choice except to carry out the decapitation strategy to the end.

Yue Wushuang, on the other hand, couldn't have been more "cowardly". She obviously had the ability to fight a head-on battle with Long Yangjun now, but she never did it again after the first attack when she rolled at the enemy like a wheel. Instead, she simply moved on the wall outside of the Great Iron Plants. The enormous Colossus moved quickly like a gyro that had grown legs, dodging most of Long Yangjun's attacks swiftly.

Her Colossus was apparently very suitable in the cubic battle environment here. Clinging to the rocks with four legs, the Colossus could still attack with the tail that was dozens of meters long, which made it more like a hybrid of a lizard and a scorpion. The features of the Colossus must have been one of the reasons why Blackstar the Great had sent her for the underground hunting.

Harassed by the Imperial Guards and the devil huntresses, Long Yangjun could not aim and lock onto the enemy effectively at all. Although she had always been calm in battles, she was still more or less anxious when faced with the nibbling of the "rats".

But whenever she planned to turn around and take care of the "rats" first, Yue Wushuang would jump down from the wall and lunge from her vulnerable flank, disrupting her attacks that had just taken shape.

After five minutes of predicament, Long Yangjun only managed to kill and wound a hundred Imperial Guards and devil huntresses. She was unable to deal any fatal damage to Yue Wushuang's Colossus. In fact, it was her own Colossus that had been added with a few new, shocking damage. At the same time, the fuel and the ammunition stock fell below the emergent line, and earsplitting alarms were ringing nonstop.

Her Colossus had been heavily damaged in the battle against Blackstar the Great, and it should've been sent for major maintenance.

But her base deep below the ground did not have the ability to maintain a Colossus comprehensively. So, she could only bring it to battle again after simple maintenance.

That was why she was heavily wounded in the battle against the three experts in the Divinity Transformation Stage last time.

After the battle, she had been fleeing in a hurry while setting up decoys without a single second of rest. She did not even have the time or conditions for simple maintenance again before she returned to the Great Iron Plants.

Beneath the seemingly brilliant arm, there were actually dense cracks, and noises were echoing in every slightly violent activity.

The alarms of the mainframe crystal processor had long been turned off by Long Yangjun; otherwise, the shrieks would not stop at all. This Colossus had really reached the limits of fatigue and could not fight anymore!

BOOM! BOOM! BOOM! BOOM! BOOM! BOOM! BOOM!

The enemy's fire was still pouring on her mercilessly. The colorful fireballs covered her entire body like abnormal tumors, eclipsing the previous brilliance of the Colossus.

Chi!

A noise echoed from the right knee of the Heavenly Crystal. The Colossus slipped rightward and staggered backward, as if the axis that supported the leg had serious problems, and she had to retreat right now.

The Moon Devil obviously hesitated, as if Yue Wushuang was considering whether Long Yangjun was running for real or it was just a trap. A moment later, she still could not resist the temptations of capturing Long Yangjun alive. The Moon Devil turned from a fleeing gecko into a ferocious, venomous lizard and pounced at the enemy from high above!

Crack!

The right knee of the Heavenly Crystal echoed again. The entire support and turning unit completely exploded, and the right calf was entirely gone. The Colossus was immediately much shorter!

Even though Yue Wushuang had foreseen that it might be a trap, she still did not expect that Long Yangjun could bear the loss of a calf just for an opportunity of attack.

Her attack was higher than it should've been now.

Pu!

The tail that stabbed at the Heavenly Crystal's chest pierced into the Heavenly Crystal's shoulder in the end.

If it had pierced into the chest, there would've been a chance to penetrate through the "spiritual residence" and mince the user inside.

But if it only stabbed through the shoulder, the best it could achieve was to make the enemy lose an arm.

What was even more critical was that Long Yangjun took the opportunity to shorten the distance between them. Intentionally giving away the arm, she focused her right hand into a sharp blade and stabbed into the abdomen of the Moon Devil!

Chi! Chi! Chi! Chi! Chi!

The Heavenly Crystal's hand was immediately heated to thousands of degrees, pressing forward unstoppably in the Moon Devil's abdomen.

Yue Wushuang was greatly shocked, not expecting Long Yangjun's combat ability to be as tough.

She intended to withdraw the tail and get away from Long Yangjun, but Long Yangjun simply held on to her without letting her go. The two Colossus clung to each other and rolled on the ground, like two skyscrapers collapsing at the same time.

"Die now!"

Long Yangjun's eyes that were filled with ice almost exploded. Not bothering the fact that her right arm was about to be cut off by the enemy's tail, she shouted hoarsely.

Crap!

The cold sweat on Yue Wushuang's forehead was frozen, and desperation spread out of her previously calm eyes.

Right at the most critical moment, a sword aura that was as fast as a flash of lightning but did not have any sound darted close and pierced through the Heavenly Crystal's left leg, which had been still intact. Long Yangjun's well-prepared attack was deviated from the course.

Two shadows that were as magnificent as gods appeared at the edge of the battlefield at the same time.

The other two experts in the Divinity Transformation Stage, "Devilish Abyss" Li Jianyi and "Star Breaker" Song Huanzhen, had finally come back to the Great Iron Plants for reinforcement with their Colossi!

Chapter 2414: The Last Hope!

Long Yangjun's attack could've directly cut apart the crystalized spine of the Moon Devil, broke the wires and tubes that connected the upper and lower half bodies, depriving the Colossus of at least 70% of its combat ability.

However, the unexpected attack from the back made her only able to wave the sharp dagger sideways and leave shocking damage on the plate armor of the Moon Devil.

The damage seemed long and brutal, but it was immediately blocked by the sticky liquid metals secreted from the body. After meeting the air, the liquid metals quickly consolidated into a gray shell. The enemy did not suffer any fatal damage at all!

Before Long Yangjun realized what was going on, a cold and gloomy mental attack had crawled into her brain, displaying erratic, terrifying illusions. It was exactly performed by "Devilish Abyss" Li Jianyi, a top expert of the Meditation Healers!

Through the enhancement of the Colossus, Li Jianyi had boosted his brainwaves to the maximum and slapped them at the Heavenly Crystal like a ship!

It was a critical attack supported by his hundred years of Cultivation. If it had a regular expert in the Nascent Soul Stage, the victims could've been directly whipped into a retard.

Despite the toughness of Long Yangjun's soul, she was still more or less slowed down. During the half second of delay, "Star Breaker" Song Huanzhen's second wave of swords had already come. Long Yangjun was forced to cope with the attacks with the Heavenly Crystal, which only had one leg and one arm functioning right now.

After resisting Song Huanzhen's stormy, continual attacks for almost ten thousand years, she failed to dodge Yue Wushuang's ambush from the back. In an earsplitting noise, the heavy, powerful tail ripped apart the air and slapped the most vulnerable waist of the Heavenly Crystal in a streak of dazzling brilliance, blowing the Heavenly Crystal away into an iron forest. It could barely stand on its feet again.

"A*shole!"

Long Yangjun was in such excruciating pain that the ice spurting out of her eyes almost turned blood red. She felt that her body seemed to be minced apart, and her left arm and legs had been swallowed by numbness.

The sensors of the Colossus and the nervous system of the user were highly integrated. While the keenness of the Colossus could be improved, all the external wounds it suffered would be fed back to the user without any buffer. In the worst-case scenario, even the nerves and brain cells of the user would be burnt.

What Long Yangjun had been worried about most came true after all.

The traps that she improvised did not stall "Devilish Abyss" Li Jianyi and "Star Breaker" Song Huanzhen for long. They had even inferred her real plan and reinforced the Great Iron Plants without any delay, blocking her before she escaped again!

One ragged Colossus that had almost run out of supplies against three Colossi that were basically intact and supported by hundreds of elites and even more regular Immortal Cultivators and battle puppets. It was an impossible task!

Should I run?

Long Yangjun thought quickly and browsed through the maps of all the mines and tunnels in the hundred kilometers around.

However, the three Colossi did not give her any chance of running at all but surrounded her in a triangle. Hundreds of malicious eyes had also been focused on every gap around her like haunting ghosts.

"Monster Li, I'm really screwed up because of you this time!"

Long Yangjun vomited a mouthful of black blood and grinned miserably. Reviewing everything that had happened over the past half month, even she was extremely astonished at what she had done. What exactly had she been doing? Had she lost her mind? Was she still Lead Eunuch Wang Xi, one of the Four Rapscallions in the Ancient Sages Sector who brought disasters to the world and did not care about anybody?

When did she go through the changes that even she did not realize?

Was it the time when she got in touch with the secrets of the Nuwa warship and activated the primeval memories that had been sealed for hundreds of thousands of years?

Was it when she went to the Star Glory Federation together with Li Yao and witnessed a whole different lifestyle and social form, where human beings did not have to live like self-brutalizing swine in a pigsty or puppets at other people's mercy?

Was it in the illusionary world created by Extraterrestrial Devil Mo Xuan, where she listened to countless people of the federation singing the national anthem together and breaking everything that restrained them with their unity, making her realize for the first time that such great power resided in the souls of human beings that nothing in the entire universe could be compared to?

Or was it in the fierce battle between the federal fleet and the Black Wind Fleet, when so many soldiers of the federation, including "Rabid Dog" Helian Lie, charged forward relentlessly, sacrificing themselves to illuminate the dark universe and the entire federation behind them?

Did she leave the federation for the Imperium so impatiently really because she couldn't wait to unravel all the secrets? Or was it because subconsciously, she was scared that she would gradually be assimilated by Monster Li, by the Cultivators, and by the people of the federation, if she were to stay with them in the federation longer?

But she still did not expect that Monster Li would still catch up to her like a haunting ghost when she had run to somewhere so far away from the Star Glory Federation or that she would be infected with his characteristic idiocy!

"One wrong step leads to a thousand regrets!"

Long Yangjun couldn't have regretted more.

However…

She could clearly sense the sincere gratitude and trust of the refugees of the Great Iron Plants when she led them to evacuate into the deeper parts below the ground.

Their expressions were something that she had never seen when she played "Lead Eunuch Wang Xi". She had barely seen it when she played "Serenity Seraph" half for fun either. At that time, the Nepenthe believers who had been brainwashed by the Serenity Arts could only watch her with numb and indifferent eyes.

Theirs were not the real eyes, but only the blinking indicators on a spiritual puppet.

Human beings were a gregarious animal after all. They had to fight for something or someone, didn't they?

Was she… a human being?

Long Yangjun shook her head, and the black hair that had been curled to the back of her head dispersed like crazy flames. Mixed with the eccentric brilliance in her eyes, she looked ten times more intimidating than before, like an ancient goddess that had time traveled from the primeval battlefields!

"Sister Long!"

Li Jialing was also trapped in the battle against a hundred elite Imperial guards and devil huntresses. Under the enemy's stormy, consecutive attacks, the shortcoming that the three-phase transformative crystal suit was too vulnerable had been infinitely enlarged. His crystal suit had long been blown apart into pieces. In many parts, his golden skin had been directly exposed, only to be covered in red blood very soon.

To be honest, if Blackstar the Great hadn't strictly ordered the hunting troops to keep Li Jialing alive in any case through the mouth of Queen Li Linghai, he would've been ripped into shreds by the Imperial Guards and the devil huntresses a long time ago.

Death might've been spared, but torture was unavoidable. The enemies' every attack had been targeted at his limbs, almost tearing his arms and legs apart. Li Jialing was blown to his knees again and again, only to struggle to his feet every time and slash the dented vibration saber into the enemies' chest brutally.

There was only one belief that supported him to continue fighting.

As long as he distracted enough firepower, Long Yangjun would certainly be able to kill Yue Wushuang!

But the arrival of "Devilish Abyss" Li Jianyi and "Star Breaker" Song Huanzhen cast his heart into the ice at the bottom of a ten-thousand-meter-high valley.

All the strength in his body was pumped away, and he could not feel a single drop of his blood or marrow in his body anymore. On one of his knees, Li Jialing supported his body with the saber that had been blunted, with the world swirling before his eyes. His ears were humming, and he could not get back to his feet again.

"Serenity Seraph!"

Xu Zhicheng and other Nepenthe believers were all rescued by the workers and hiding in the safe area at the edge of the Great Iron Plants.

It was a mess right now, and they were all "important assets" of the Great Iron Plants. The Immortal Cultivators did not want those skilled workers to be stomped on into pieces by the Colossi and therefore did not stop them from running.

They all knew Long Yangjun's Colossus and that the "Serenity Seraph" had come back to rescue them without bothering anything.

Also, it did not require abundant battle experience for them to tell that Long Yangjun was in absolute disadvantage right now, and she had been heavily besieged and suppressed by the enemies.

All the workers, including Xu Zhicheng, were anxious with hot tears, but they did not know what else they could do except for clenching their fists and praying in silence.

Praying, on the other hand, had been proved to be the most useless thing they had done in the past ten thousand years.

The underground locals had prayed for ten thousand years in darkness, wishing vague brightness to appear in their worlds and change their lives.

But their prayer never worked, and all the changes only made their lives worse.

Right now, it was the darkest moment ever. Would new changes ever come again?

The underground locals were almost shedding tears of blood. They prayed in silence with their life. Once. Even if it is only once in ten thousand years, let the tiny hope arrive!

The Colossi of the three Immortal Cultivators gradually tightened the triangle of siege as they pressed near.

"A very brilliant trap."

"Devilish Abyss" Li Jianyi's faint chuckle echoed from the abnormally expanded head of his Colossus. "We were almost fooled!"

"One against three, and she is still capable of launching such a counterattack?"

"Star Breaker" Song Huanzhen also chuckled coldly. "I didn't know that Dongfang Wang had been keeping such experts like you to his side. Is Dongfang Mingyue really your name?"

"She is not Dongfang Mingyue!"

Yue Wushuang shrieked, "When I fought her just now, I sensed very familiar but weird waves. It's tenebrum energy! She is greatly related to the extraterrestrial devils. Perhaps she is a real devil. You must be careful, fellow Cultivators!"

"Extraterrestrial devils?"

Li Jianyi and Song Huanzhen looked at each other and grinned hideously. "It seems that we have caught a big fish by accident!"

Long Yangjun sniffed, and clusters of brilliant crystals grew all over her body again. Deep inside every cluster of crystals, objects that looked like gene helices were floating and breaking apart under the boost of her mind!

Chapter 2415: The Song of Infinite Fire!

With every broken gene strand, Long Yangjun seemed to be given a piece of new strength. The humanity in her eyes gradually faded, and the nonchalance and solemnity of the gods were surfacing drop by drop!

"Is this what you want? Then, come on and get a taste of the primeval horror!"

She mumbled in inhuman voices as the last part of her black hair turned into brilliant clusters of crystals.

Her Colossus that had lost both legs and one arm floated in the midair under the support of the anti-gravity rune arrays. Sharp thorns of crystals were also protruding while it was trembling weirdly. At the sharp tips of the crystals, weird brilliance was dazzling and stirring the air around into colorful swirls.

The Colossi of the three Immortal Cultivators also opened the protective plates of the power units to increase the power output. The exhaust flames were scorching and boiling the air around them, making them look somewhat unreal. The ground below their feet was falling apart in cracking noises as it could not bear the unparalleled pressure that they blew out anymore.

The four Colossi were all ready for a final battle.

At this moment, the whole air in the Great Iron Plants seemed to have been burnt up by the four Colossi. Everybody felt that their mouth was dry, their nose was stifled, and their upper lip was wet. When they touched their upper lips, they found that the wetness was caused by their nosebleed!

Right then—

"Devilish Abyss" Li Jianyi was standing at the northwest corner of the triangular siege. Not far away behind him, it was the magma well of the geothermal factory.

The well that directly led to the fiery mother lodes was the source of power for the entire Great Iron Plants. Before, it had been sealed and suppressed tightly by rune arrays to ensure that the spiritual energy could be output steadily and consecutively.

But at this moment, the roars of primeval beasts that had just been woken up were echoing from the magma well. Dozens of streams of magma redder than blood had broken the rune arrays and the seals, erupting skyward unstoppably!

"What?!"
