All the crystals around Long Yangjun were constricting violently. Ever since her primeval memories were awakened, she had only experienced such violent outbreaks of spiritual energy twice so far, and the first time was when she encountered Blackstar the Great!

"What?!"

Li Jialing sensed that the strength of the Imperial Fire Pearl inside his body was seething in impatience, as if it were attracted by the strength of the same type, which made him stand on his feet slowly again. While panting, he stared at the magma well in disbelief!

"What?!"

Xu Zhicheng and the workers naturally couldn't tell what strength it was exactly. They only sensed that a destructive thunder was quickly gathering in the underground world. Even though they were multiple kilometers away, the strands of hair on their heads were still erecting, with static electricity wandering on their heads. Their skin was so dry that it was spluttering. Everybody had a numb feeling, as if they had fallen into a dream.

"What?!"

The three experts in the Divinity Transformation Stage, Yue Wushuang, Li Jianyi, and Song Huanzhen, were greatly shocked. Right now, the fierce battle between Blackstar the Great and the four Kurfürst families on the surface of the planet was still going on. Blackstar the Great and most of the forces of the reformists were required on the main battlefield. So, they were the only three Divinity Transformation Stage experts who had been sent to hunt Li Yao and Long Yangjun and to catch Li Jialing alive.

Who exactly had unleashed such violent, massive, and invincible strength deep inside the magma?

A weird scene happened!

The erupting magma seemed to be under the control of a certain force field and was condensed into dozens of fierce fiery snakes in midair before they were thrown at "Devilish Abyss" Li Jianyi's Colossus!

"Devilish Abyss" Li Jianyi had increased his wariness to the maximum the moment the magma broke out. His Colossus was as agile as an autumn leaf in the wind although it was dozens of meters tall when it blinked to the right side swiftly, dodging the attack of the fiery snakes perfectly.

But little did he expect that it was just a distraction. While everyone's attention was drawn by the splendid outbreak of magma, several metal tentacles dug out from magma well, the forsaken tubes, and even the crevices like sordid vipers and snuck stealthily until they leapt up at the last moment, tying the right leg of "Devilish Abyss" Li Jianyi and pulling him into the magma well!

"Devilish Abyss" Li Jianyi had been rather cautious and did not leave any opportunity for Long Yangjun to ambush him when he moved and surrounded the enemy, but he was simply too close to the magma well with his back against it in the beginning. Who could've thought that there was such a shameless and yet strong enemy that was lurking in the sweltering magma the whole time?

The violent magma that was extremely violent by itself because of the fiery spiritual energy it contained was the best disguise. The enemy could totally power the Colossus to the maximum in the magma before launching the attack.

"Devilish Abyss" Li Jianyi was a Meditation Healer who was not adept at melee battles. Also, as an old man of the same age as "Silver Fox" Li Jiande, he had lost a lot of his physical strength, sensitivity, and spiritual energy reserve. Now that he was pulled heavily, he was about to be snatched into the magma well without giving any resistance.

"Watch out!"

"Star Breaker" Song Huanzhen roared. Three sword auras were unleashed from her Colossus and slashed at the metal tentacles as quickly as bolts of lightning.

It was a shame that Long Yangjun had already realized something when she saw the appearance of the metal tentacles. Her eyes immediately widened and were filled with complicated feelings again when they were cold and inhuman a moment ago. She cursed with all the strength she had, "A*shole! You've been hiding and watching the show the whole time!"

When she shouted "a*shole", her Colossus had already accelerated and crashed into "Star Breaker" Song Huanzhen's Colossus from inside a streak of brightness, deviating the three sword auras from their course, exactly like how Song Huanzhen disrupted her attack just now.

Song Huanzhen's astonishing sword auras missed the target and eventually chopped the rocks several kilometers away.

"Devilish Abyss" Li Jianyi also struggled hard to break the wretched metal tentacles.

It was a shame that there were more and more metal tentacles, and they were tighter and tighter. Also, the sharp thorns that had pierced into his plate armor deeply seemed to also boast a certain weird corrupt force, which was hacking the control system of his Colossus as quickly as a bolt of lighting and trying to deprive him of the permissions to use the Colossus!

"This is impossible!"

"Devilish Abyss" Li Jianyi was almost scared out of his wits, especially when he recognized the metal tentacles.

Having participated in the battle underneath the royal palace in person, he could tell after the first look that those metal tentacles were parts of Li Linghai—no, Wuying Qi's unique Colossus "Hellstar".

How was it possible?

Hardly had the thought flashed in his head when he was dragged into the surging magma together with his Colossus!

The rumbling thunders deep inside the magma well were immediately ten times more violent than before, mixed with the cracking noises of metal fractures. It was almost like a metal beast a hundred kilometers long was enjoying a great meal.

The magma that spurted out just now was as red as blood, but it soon became variegated, as if it had been mixed with abundant suspicious metal particles, until in the end, it became shiningly gold, as if a tiny man-made sun was rising in the underground world!

From the solar brilliance, gigantic broken limbs were being tossed out nonstop like twisted worms with magma splashing out while they hit the ground, unable to maintain the original shape anymore and reduced to melted metal craps. Everybody observed them carefully, only to discover that they were actually the limbs of "Devilish Abyss" Li Jianyi's Colossus!

The limbs of the Colossus had been torn apart by brute force and thrown out from the depths of the magma!

If the limbs had all been broken, then—

Everybody focused their eyes on the magma well. As they expected, only half a second later, the torso of the miserable Colossus that had lost the limbs was spurted out too.

At this moment, "Devilish Abyss" Li Jianyi seemed to still be alive. He was still controlling the power units on the back of the Colossus' torso, hoping to trigger ten thousand exhaust flames and get away from the terror.

But a streak of gold darting out of the magma well pierced into the back of his Colossus and out of the chest, directly destroying the spiritual residence. "Devilish Abyss" Li Jianyi had probably been blown into a pulp too.

The power rune arrays on the Colossus that was on the verge of destruction all dimmed and fizzled out.

A brand-new Colossus, from which infinite magma and fire were flowing, as if it had been born at the deepest level of hell, was rising slowly.

"This is—"

Everybody on the spot, from the experts in the Divinity Transformation Stage to the common workers, gasped hard, their mind occupied by the appalling Colossus. From the head all the way to the arc-shaped armor on the back, metal tentacles were waving crazily. Every tentacle was like a venomous dragon that boasted life and independent will. Opening the sucking discs at the Immortal Cultivators around, they revealed the circular sawtooth inside in malicious brilliance.

The three metal faces on the shoulders and the abdomen seemed to be changing together with the boost of spiritual energy, letting out blood-boiling roars.

A crescent saber, made of ten thousand wings that were as thin as paper, was now dispersing and now gathering, hovering around the Colossus spectacularly.

The golden brilliance that pierced through "Devilish Abyss" Li Jianyi's Colossus, on the other hand, was a straight longsword, which was now being grasped by the mysterious and powerful Colossus and unleashing overwhelming flames of spiritual energy that flooded into the debris.

Chi! Chi! Chi! Chi! Chi! Chi!

Thousands of sparks were immediately spurting out of the cracks on "Devilish Abyss" Li Jianyi's Colossus. Even if Li Jianyi survived just now, he would certainly have been burnt into ashes at this moment, as dead as one could've been.

Crack! Crack! Crack! Crack!

The mysterious Colossus retreated the longsword, but the metal tentacles on the back simply moved forward and tore the burning Colossus into pieces!

The thousands of pieces fell out, along with the soul-stirring battle hymn from hell. It was truly a violent rhythm that could shake the underground apart. With that as the background, the "Arsonist", the Colossus made of infinite fire, had finally arrived!

Chapter 2416: Terrifying Arsonist!

"What exactly… is this Colossus?"

Yue Wushuang and Song Huanzhen's Colossi let out shrieks while they searched all the data as fast as possible, but they had never seen such a strong and terrifying Colossus before. The enemy's instant energy output had obviously exceeded the limits of their detection!

Before, it was the Immortal Cultivators' three Colossi against Long Yangjun's one Colossus, and their advantage was obvious.

However, the Colossus "Arsonist" had snuck all the way to the Great Iron Plants through the magma river and lurked in the scorching magma until the perfect opportunity to attack. After blowing up one of the Immortal Cultivators' Colossus instantly, the comparison of the two parties was now two Colossi against two Colossi!

In terms of absolute strength, Long Yangjun, who had completely released the primeval bloodlines, and Li Yao, who had been fully upgraded by the information lives, were somewhat stronger than Yue Wushuang and Song Huanzhen.

What the Immortal Cultivators could count on seemed to be only their absolute advantage in number.

"Open fire! Open fire! Open fire!"

Yue Wushuang gnashed and roared in the communication channel.

However, before the Immortal Cultivators around realized what was going on, the Arsonist had glowed after a hum, and the magma it was stained with all splashed out like raindrops.

Every cluster of magma was accelerated to the highest speed. Turning into angry bullets, they lunged at the Immortal Cultivators as if they had eyes.

Many low-level Immortal Cultivators, failing to dodge in time, were pierced through by the magma. When accelerated to multiple times the speed of sound, magma was as destructive as the most powerful explosive bullets. Besides, the magma was in the half solid state. Even though it was blocked by the spiritual shield, it could easily split into smaller hot streams, stabbing thousands of holes on the crystal suits and the bodies of flesh and blood of the low-level Immortal Cultivators.

It was far from the end.

Under the scourge of the stormy magma, the plate armor on the shoulders of the Arsonist was opened, revealing the honeycomb-type smart bullets. In swooshing sounds, all the bullets were shot out like mini flying swords. Leaving long exhaust flames behind, they flipped, bounced, and fragmented in the midair as they flew at the panicked Imperial Guards and devil huntresses.

After every smart bullet hit the crystal suit of the Immortal Cultivators, their shell would immediately explode, letting out the super-tiny battle puppets inside that were as little as spiders or even ants. Through the wounds caused by the magma just now, the puppets crawled into the crystal suits or even the bodies of the Immortal Cultivators. When the Immortal Cultivators reached for them after one moment of carelessness, all they could grasp was their slippery tails!

"Ah! Ahhhhhh!"

A lot of Immortal Cultivators were dancing with twisted faces. Some covered their heads and rushed, before they suddenly fell over, unable to stand up again. Some even took off their crystal suits impatiently, their eyes bloodshot, and they cut their flesh with their daggers, hoping to pluck out the super-tiny battle puppets that had crawled into their bodies, only to no avail in most cases. They could only watch the tiny bumps wandering on their skin quickly, eventually entering their brains and digging out from their mouths, noses, eyes, or ears.

Faced with such weird attacks, even the most determined Imperial Guards and devil huntresses couldn't help but feel chilled.

Right then, cracking sounds were echoing from the forsaken mines and tunnels on the wall.

They were battle puppets!

Hundreds of battle puppets in the shape of spiders, controlled by Xiaoming and Wenwen, crawled out of the mines and tunnels and charged at the Immortal Cultivators in an iron torrent.

BOOM! BOOM! BOOM! BOOM! BOOM!

The stereo soundwave cannons around the Arsonist that looked like satellites had boosted the song of infinite fire to the maximum too. The soundwaves had almost been condensed into visible tides that were drowning all the Immortal Cultivators.

It was much more than a simple, deafening battle hymn; it was also an extremely powerful mental attack, even mixed with Xiaoming and Wenwen's invasion on the Immortal Cultivators' battle network!

For a moment, all the Immortal Cultivators were either stalled by the magma and the super-tiny spiritual puppets, or they were seeing the most horrible pictures under Li Yao's mental attack, or they found out that they were losing the permissions over their crystal suits and being kicked out of the battle network while they were operating in a panic.

It meant that not only the communication channel and the commanding system had been closed to them, but the identification system had also been confused. The allies' automatic magical equipment would very likely hurt them by accident!

Creak! Creak! Creak! Creak! Creak!

The Immortal Cultivators had brought abundant battle puppets into the underground world as a backup.

Li Minghui's Black Iron Group boasted a lot of battle puppets as guards too.

While the battle puppets' combat abilities were not as good as the general Immortal Cultivators, they were at least ten times more than the Immortal Cultivators and therefore served as one of the most important troops.

But at this moment, the indicators and attack rune arrays of all the battle puppets that were "supposed" to be loyal to the Immortal Cultivators were blinking ominously, and their crystal processors were squeaking aloud, making their skulls and limbs cramp hysterically, as if two opposite strengths were fighting over the control permissions of their bodies.

The first ones to "defect" were the battle puppets that belonged to the Black Iron Group.

The reason was very simple. Just now, Yue Wushuang gave Li Minghui away without any hesitation in order to stop Long Yangjun, making the expert in the Nascent Soul Stage die a rather unexpected death.

As the leader of the Black Iron Group, Li Minghui naturally controlled the control passwords and the highest permissions of the battle puppets. The ramification of him dying abruptly was that the control passwords could not be changed in time and the dynamic safety mode could not be activated. It was also impossible to launch the most daunting counterattacks when the highest permissions were seized.

Taking advantage of the loophole, Xiaoming and Wenwen appended a false instruction to the control system of the Black Iron Group's battle puppets, setting one of the leaders of the Black Iron Group as the primary succeeding commander in the event of Li Minghui's death.

Then, they hacked the portable crystal processor of the leader and modified its identity information, overwriting it with their own information.

Li Minghui's portable crystal processor, heart, and the back of his head were connected to the control system. Now that it could not perceive Li Minghui's heartbeat, temperature, and brainwaves anymore, it naturally determined that Li Minghui was dead and therefore transferred the commandership to the leadership.

However, the information of the leadership had been greatly modified, and the commandership of a thousand battle puppets fell to the hands of the two information lives just like that.

Through the invisible and ubiquitous Spiritual Nexus, Xiaoming and Wenwen's consciousnesses branched and entered the thousand most advanced battle puppets at the same time.

"We've made it!"

Under the eyes of panic and shock, the thousand battle puppets branded with the symbol of the Black Iron Group danced and squirmed, before they let out crisp and creepy laughter and lunged at the Immortal Cultivators next to them.

The whole Great Iron Plants had been reduced to a mess. After the moves of Li Yao and the two little kids, the Immortal Cultivators' advantage in number was no more!

Dumbfounded, Yue Wushuang and Song Huanzhen almost couldn't believe their eyes. Had they not experienced it in person, they would never have believed that they could suffer such a brutal failure when they had such great advantages a moment ago!

Li Yao did not give them any time to be surprised or to think. Holding Broken Wing and Folded Feather, he strode at the two of them.

After every step, the battle hymn echoing from his body would be more violent, and the godly faces on his shoulders and his abdomen became more angry and brutal!

Yue Wushuang and Song Huanzhen looked at each other. The two brutal women roared at the same time and attacked Li Yao, one from the left and the other from the right.

The "Moon Devil" crouched again and connected the tail to the head, forming an unstoppable wheel.

Song Huanzhen, on the other hand, performed all the skills of "Star Breaker". Thousands of streaks of brilliance emerged on the surface of her Colossus like shooting stars, and the sword auras were crushing everything like a raging flood!

"The two of you alone are really not gratifying enough!"

Li Yao shouted, "Just let your master come here!"

"Broken Wing" separated into thousands of metal feathers as thin as cicada's wings again, each of them aiming at one of "Star Breaker" Song Huanzhen's sword aura. Folded Feather, on the other hand, was kept on his back and raised upward in an almost perfect curve at the most vulnerable joint where the head and the tail of the "Moon Devil" were connected!

Clink! Clink! Clink! Clink! Clink!

Almost all of Song Huanzhen's sword auras was blocked by "Broken Wing". Even the few sword auras that were not blocked were also absorbed by the "Hydra System", which could both attack and defend. Yue Wushuang's "Moon Devil", on the other hand, was slashed by Li Yao right in the head, leaving a damage from the chest to the face that was as deep as a gorge. All kinds of components and colorful fluids were splashing out of the damage. Even the head of the Colossus was almost blown away by Li Yao's sword.

One versus two, the Arsonist showed absolutely no sign of failing!

After Li Yao raised the sword, the tip aiming at the sky, he accumulated enough strength for the second attack, and without any delay, he slashed it downward as unstoppably as a flood that had just broken the dam, only to be evaded by Yue Wushuang in the sound of metals. He took the opportunity to step forward heavily and lowered his shoulder and elbow, knocking at the enemy heavily. The "Moon Devil" was blown away brutally all the way into "Star Breaker" Song Huanzhen's Colossus in a deafening explosion.

"Star Breaker" Song Huanzhen was soaked in a cold sweat because of Li Yao's magnificent combat ability. She was about to get rid of the Moon Devil and stood up, discussing with her partner if a tactical retreat was necessary right now, when her Colossus sensed an immense force. As it turned out, Yue Wushuang had stood up on her own with her Colossus and was even vaguely pushing her to Li Yao's "Arsonist" while she was trying to flee to the edge of the Great Iron Plants!

Song Huanzhen did not expect that Yue Wushuang would be as shameless. Too furious to curse anymore, she could only watch the burning shadow of the Arsonist growing larger and larger in her eyesight.

Suddenly, her entire body ached, and a massive force drowned her from her back like a gloomy tide.

A dazzling crystal blade had pierced through her Colossus from the back to the breastplate directly!

Chapter 2417: Explain Yourself!

It was Long Yangjun's "Heavenly Crystal"!

"Don't act as if I didn't exist, idiot!"

Long Yangjun spat coldly. Dragging the incomplete body, the Heavenly Crystal stabbed the only remaining right arm entirely into the chest of "Star Breaker" Song Huanzhen's Colossus. Not only was the chest pierced through, but the glittering crystals were also spreading out to all directions like some sort of weird virus!

Song Huanzhen was trapped inside the spiritual residence of the Colossus, surrounded and suppressed by ever-growing crystals, which gave her nowhere to run to at all and were even invading her body!

"Star Breaker" Song Huanzhen struggled violently, trying to get rid of the scourge of the Heavenly Crystal. But by the time she finally got rid of the enemy's boundary, the dozens of metal tentacles from the "Arsonist" had already been extended to her face and were even penetrating into the body through the cracks on the Colossus.

The sucking discs and the sawtooth at the front ends of every metal tentacle had been opened to the maximum. From the dark holes that looked like the throats of vipers, brutal brilliance was beaming out.

"Star Breaker" Song Huanzhen's bloodshot eyes were filled with horror as she exclaimed in desperation, "No—"

"Immortal Cultivator?"

Li Yao sneered and opened fire. Thousands of flames burst out of the Arsonist, burning "Star Breaker" Song Huanzhen as well as her Colossus into nothingness from inside to outside. Even if some of the debris to which the pieces of their remaining souls were attached flew out, it was immediately minced into pieces by Long Yangjun's crystal blades that followed closely behind.

Under Li Yao and Long Yangjun's collective attacks, and particularly because of "Moon Devil" Yue Wushuang's backstabbing, "Star Breaker" Song Huanzhen and her Colossus were obliterated in the blink of an eye, not leaving a single last word behind.

Li Yao and Long Yangjun looked at each other and knew each other well enough to not say anything. Not bothering the small fries who were still resisting stubbornly around, they chased after "Moon Devil" Yue Wushuang at the same time.

After kicking Song Huanzhen at Li Yao and Long Yangjun, Yue Wushuang was given a boost in speed and had run to the entrance of the Great Iron Plants at the edge during the moment the two of them killed Song Huanzhen.

She did not even have the time to take off her Colossus, fearing that Li Yao and Long Yangjun would slash her from her back the moment she took off the Colossus. She simply broke into the mines equipped with a Colossus almost thirty meters tall, leaving deafening noises of collisions from the depths of the mines.

"Don't let her run away!"

Li Yao gnashed his teeth and roared, "She is a leader of the reformists and must be a trusted subordinate of Li Linghai and Wuying Qi!"

Since Yue Wushuang did not take off the Colossus, it was naturally unsuitable for Li Yao to take off his own, or it would be rather awkward if the enemy suddenly launched a counterattack and left a few bloody holes on his body. The cave he was in was among the spacious ones, but it was still only no more than twenty meters in diameter. A Colossus could only move inside by bending the back. Both fighting and hunting were highly inconvenient.

Thankfully, Xiaoming and Wenwen mobilized tremendous battle puppets to follow Li Yao and run after Yue Wushuang. The features and parameters of the "Moon Devil" were about to be fixed, and the most lethal attack from the back was underway when the "Moon Devil" suddenly came to a halt.

"Watch out!"

Inside the Heavenly Crystal, Long Yangjun dragged the metal tentacles behind the back of the Arsonist heavily, snatching Li Yao back.

BOOM!

In the next second, a thunderous explosion burst out deep inside the cave. Infinite light and heat raged out like a raging flood. Not only were the two Colossi blown out of the cave at the same time, but the rock stratum several square kilometers around were also collapsed by the explosion too, raising a minor earthquake!

"…"

Li Yao looked at the collapsed rocks and the dust that was coming at him like tides, stunned.

The explosion of such a high intensity could've only been caused by the self-detonation of a Colossus.

He did not expect Yue Wushuang to be as brutal. After discovering that her odds of survival were slim, she was ruthless enough to activate the self-detonation unit on her Colossus, hoping to die together with Li Yao.

No. Considering the cunningness of the she-devil, it was possible that she had escaped from the Colossus in advance like a gecko losing its tail. Although her Colossus had been sacrificed, it was possible that she had escaped and was about to report to Li Linghai and Wuying Qi about the issue.

As the leader of the Tribunal of Extraterrestrial Devils and a central person of the reformists, she was certainly incomparable to "Devilish Abyss" Li Jianyi and "Star Breaker" Song Huanzhen who were simple warriors.

As the three experts in the Divinity Transformation Stage on the Immortal Cultivators' side died or fled, the short yet brutal battle approached the end.

The collapse of the three Colossi on their own side shattered the determination of all the Immortal Cultivators. Although the crushed soldiers were unwilling to surrender to Li Yao, Long Yangjun, Li Jialing, and even the "humble locals", they were unable to fight anymore, and so, they escaped to the deeper parts of the underground worlds along the forsaken mines and the natural cracks.

Li Yao did not have high hope for the Immortal Cultivators. They were mostly wounded and short of supplies, and their crystal processors had been seriously sabotaged too. It was very likely that they would lose their way in the caves that were as complicated as mazes, and it was impossible to send the distress calls to the surface of the planet through the rocks that were almost ten thousand meters thick.

Then, after using up the last drop of fuel and the last stream of spiritual energy, they would only be swallowed by the darkness, embracing an ending that was even more miserable than the locals of the place who had been living here for thousands of years.

Right now, furious flames were still burning inside the Great Iron Plants. The magma that spurted out just now had destroyed a lot of important facilities and precious assets too. For the sake of the underground locals in the long term, they had no time to chase after the Immortal Cultivators who were dispersing and fleeing, and they must clean the battlefield and rescue the assets first.

Li Yao made the decision quickly. Xiaoming and Wenwen gradually changed their strategy, turning from the siege and sabotage a moment ago to taking over everything, including the mainframe crystal processors of all the factories in the Great Iron Plants.

As the mainframe crystal processors were compromised one after another, more and more battle puppets took Li Yao's side. After bowing at the Arsonist courteously in a weird posture, they put down the weapons in their hands and began to clean up the battlefield. All the workers were so confused by what they saw that they could not understand what was going on at all.

After half a month, the Great Iron Plants had finally fallen to the hands of the Cultivators and the underground locals again!

Driving the Heavenly Crystal, Long Yangjun floated in the midair with the anti-gravity rune arrays and observed the compromised battle puppets as well as Li Yao's "Arsonist" carefully.

Li Yao felt rather embarrassed being watched by her. Scratching the metal tentacles on his head, he struggled to squeeze out a smile. "It's me. I'm back. What are you looking at?"

"I know it's you. I'm checking how I can cut you into pieces and feed you to dogs, but it has occurred to me that you seem to be even stronger now. Incredibly strong. And there's also this… damn Colossus. It is truly challenging to cut you into pieces right now."

Long Yangjun was silent for a long time. Then, she finally couldn't hold it back anymore and gnashed her teeth while pointing at the battle puppets that were crawling everywhere on the ground. "Do you not feel that you owe me a good explanation?"

…

Half an hour later, the last heavily wounded Immortal Cultivator was tied up by the battle puppets, and the Immortal Cultivators who were capable of running had long escaped. The intense gunshots finally came to an end.

Commanding the spiritual puppets, Xu Zhicheng and the workers ransacked the Great Iron Plants for useful assets in delight. The Immortal Cultivators seemed to have regarded the Great Iron Plants as a temporary transfer station and command center, and abundant military assets had been stored in the place. Right now, all the assets, including the guns, the blades, the standard magazines, the compressed air, and the military synthetic food that contains ultra-high energy, were the trophies of the underground locals right now, enough to meet the needs of twenty towns for a year and a half.

Inside the office that belonged to Li Minghui first and Yue Wushuang later and eventually the underground locals, Long Yangjun bulged her eyes with a most spectacular look on her face, as if she were trying to hold back her laughter, but snorts were still coming from her nostrils. Her face was red, and her tears were almost dropping as she tried not to laugh out loud.

"What did you say? Excuse me?"

Long Yangjun looked at Li Yao and the two vigorous children next to him. "You said that they are your son and your daughter. This… This… This is really—"

"Yes, they are exactly my biological children!"

Li Yao replied, not exactly in a good temper, "I am a senior more than a hundred years old. Isn't it perfectly normal to have children? What's the look on your face anyway?"

"Forgive me, I'm not saying that you are abnormal. It's just that I find it hard to associate 'Vulture Li Yao' with the role of a father for the time being."

Long Yangjun chuckled again as she looked at Li Yao and the two kids. While laughing, she took a huge step back and said, "Now that you said it, the kids do look a lot like you, but the whole thing still does not sound right. Does your wife know this? No. I should keep away from you. My intuitions tell me that you are a very dangerous vortex!"

"Your intuitions are right."

Furrowing his eyebrows, Li Yao replied, "But the whole thing is not exactly as you imagined. Or rather, it is a hundred times more dangerous than what you imagined!"

"Is that so?"

Long Yangjun blinked her eyes. "Sorry for my limited imagination, but I can't think of anything that is more dangerous than you having two children behind Ding Lingdang's back."

"What are you thinking about? They are of course the children of me and Ding Lingdang!"

Li Yao did not know how to explain it now. "However, well, they had other experiences before they were born, which made them very special, even more special than you."

Long Yangjun immediately became gloomy. "Hey, hey, hey, you did not tell the ignorant kids certain things that you were not supposed to tell, did you?"

"First of all, we are not ignorant kids."

Xiaoming stood up and complained.

"Secondly, Dad didn't tell us anything. We analyzed Lady Long's identity with big data on our own. There is a 95% likelihood that you are from the primeval age, and you are a warrior of the last-generation civilizations."

Wenwen smiled brilliantly at Long Yangjun.

This time, it was Long Yangjun who was rigidified in shock.

Chapter 2418: Compassionate Alien Species

Their eyes glittering like the morning stars in the dark night, the two kids released their brainwaves and showed the idiosyncratic structure of their souls.

With her remarkable senses on the soul and the brainwaves, Long Yangjun immediately detected their difference. "What… What is the meaning of this exactly? Also, which idiot came up with a horrible name such as 'Lady Long'?"

Long Yangjun glared at Li Yao angrily.

Wenwen smiled and quickly changed the way of addressing. "Aunt Long, we are indeed Dad's children and the children of human beings. However, we are slightly different from other children in that other children's bodies are born first before their souls are gradually developed in the bodies. Our souls and self-consciousness were born first, and we developed our bodies carefully after our self-consciousness grew strong enough.

"Xiaoming and I were both born in the vast, boundless virtual game called 'Civilization'. The abundant data in 'Civilization was the first nutrition that we tasted. We grew up in the world of 'Civilization', observing the world and defining ourselves…"

According to the theories that they discussed with Li Yao earlier, the kids introduced themselves together.

Of course, the incident that their self-consciousness was suddenly awakened after they absorbed Li Yao's brainwave data was too uncanny for the two kids to bring up. They only said that after the "Hyper Spirits", their antecedents, were created by Professor Mo Xuan, they accidentally ran to the world of "Civilization", where they absorbed astronomical information and finally gained self-awareness as the changes in quantity led to a change in quality.

Because Li Yao was known as the father of "Civilization", and his marks were everywhere in the game, and since a lot of players had the habit of praying to his statue before the game, the two kids decided that Li Yao was their creator and their "father"!

After that, the things including how they created "main shells" for them to enter the material scope, how they tracked their father down from the federation all the way to the Imperium, how they found Long Yangjun's secret base first, and how they set up a camp next to her nest… were all explained to Long Yangjun dutifully and honestly.

Long Yangjun, on the other hand, was soaked in a cold sweat with a pale face.

Before this, she had thought that nobody knew her relationship with "Nepenthe" and that she was the one hiding best behind the curtain.

Little did she expect that two weird kids had been around her while observing her.

After hearing the kids' narration, Long Yangjun had goosebumps all over her body. While rubbing her arms, she exclaimed in shock, "Li Yao, I've told myself countless times that whatever unbelievable things happen to such a monster like you would not be worth my surprise for one second, but I have to admit that it's unachievable! You always have a way to shock other people, and none of the previous shocks is as shocking as this one!"

While speaking, she looked at the kids in curiosity and hesitated, as if she intended to extend her hands to pinch their pinky cheeks to determine if they were real. But naturally, they were not puppets or statues.

"I didn't see this coming."

Long Yangjun mumbled to himself, "During the battle against Extraterrestrial Devil Mo Xuan, we both believed that the time for the real virtual lives to be born in our world had not come yet, and considering the development of technologies today, the first light of dawn of virtual lives would probably not shine until thousands of years later, which was already a short moment in the prolonged history of civilization! I did know that even such a 'short moment' would be unnecessary, and that I would be fortunate enough to witness the 'child civilization' of mankind, or at least the prototype of it, so soon!"

"We are not virtual lives!"

Pouting their lips, the two kids corrected her at the same time, "We are real. Our bodies of flesh and blood, our brainwaves, and our feelings all exist in reality!"

"I'm sorry."

Long Yangjun was quick to change her mistake. "Then should I call you 'strong artificial intelligence'?"

"'Artificial intelligence' does not have a clear definition."

Wenwen continued pointing it out, "To some extent, human beings themselves were also the 'artificial intelligence' created by the Pangu Clan and the Nuwa Clan. Or rather, are you certain that the Pangu Clan and the Nuwa Clan weren't edified by the more ancient civilizations millions of years ago or even created artificially? If everybody is 'artificial intelligence', the word will not be of any reference.

"Please call us 'information lives'. We are lives that are based on information and wander in the ocean of information!"

Long Yangjun grinned. "Is 'information lives' any more accurate than 'artificial intelligence'? Any life carries a lot of information since the moment of their conception. The genes of human beings are exactly a pair of helical strands that are fully written with information! So, human beings, the Pangu Clan, and the Nuwa Clan are all 'information lives', except that the carriers of our information are genes and cells, whereas the main ways of information interaction are audio and image. Of course, thanks to the development of training techniques and crystal processors, the direct communication with brainwaves is turning into the mainstream. But that's still no fundamental difference."

"Right!"

Somewhat stunned by what Long Yangjun said, the two kids looked at each other in bewilderment. "Exactly. Carbon-based lives and information lives are not contradictory concepts at all. Carbon-based lives are information lives with different carriers. Then, how exactly are we going to distinguish us from them?"

"The carriers are not necessarily different, either."

Long Yangjun seemed particularly fond of watching the two kids troubled by the puzzle. She intentionally said, "Look, you can develop a body of flesh and blood and download your main thread into it, changing yourself into a 'carbon-based life'. By the same logic, human beings can voluntarily or involuntarily abandon their bodies and hide the remaining souls inside the artificial containers, living on as 'ghosts'. Then, can they still be counted as 'carbon-based life'?

"Furthermore, in the designs of Extraterrestrial Devil Mo Xuan, most people did not even need the artificial bodies, and they could simply live inside the crystal processors after the souls were highly compressed. If such a future did become reality, would they have been any different from you?"

"Well—"

The two kids blinked their eyes fast, with smoke popping up from their heads. "We have never considered it that way. It's really an interesting question. It seems that we have to redefine our relationship with human beings? What are we exactly? And what are human beings?"

"Huh…"

Li Yao was rather stunned after hearing their conversation.

It seemed that Long Yangjun was much more acceptive than he imagined. She got used to the two kids so quickly, and what she said sounded very reasonable and philosophical!

"What's your plan?"

Long Yangjun glanced at him. "You've brought the two kids to me without any disguise and introduced their identities without any hesitation. What's the meaning of this? Have you accepted my opinion that the evil schemes must not be done stealthily and clandestinely, and the more evil they are, the more necessary it is to carry them out openly?"

"What evil scheme can I possibly have?"

Li Yao held his chest high and said, "I have always been frank and forthright. But you're not wrong. It's true that I do not know how the two kids should face the entire civilization of mankind, and how to make most people accept them.

"Since they are my children, I certainly do not want them to live in darkness for their entire lives. Also, in order to resolve the chaos at the center of the cosmos and to take down Blackstar the Great, the four Kurfürst families and the main-force fleets of the Covenant Alliance that have never shown up yet, their assistance will be vital.

"However… you've seen the strength contained inside their bodies, haven't you? If they truly grow up to the point that they can give us a hand, many people will be deeply feared.

"I really don't know if other people will accept them. That's why I'm here to ask for your advice!"

Through the glass window that had long exploded, Long Yangjun watched the well-organized teams of spiritual puppets outside dismantling and collecting all the useful things in the Great Iron Plants, ready to ship them to deeper parts of the underground world.

What was most eye-catching was an assembly line of components, which was still operating despite the harsh environment, producing the torsos and limbs of spiritual puppets!

With the limbs and joints, the spiritual puppets that had been blown apart in the fierce battle could be recovered.

Everything above was under the control of Xiaoming and Wenwen.

In other words, the information lives were already capable of self-duplication and self-upgrade without the interference of human beings. They were only one step away from "reproduction".

"It is indeed creepy if you view the current scene from a certain perspective. It entails a weird sense of beauty, like the end of the world is about to come."

Watching for a while, Long Yangjun moved her eyes back to Li Yao's face. "Congratulations on proving your nickname of 'Monster Li'! You are now one of the most capable competitors for the 'destroyer of mankind'. That is a title that countless supervillains have been pursuing since ancient times. The small fries such as Xiao Xuance, Lu Zui, or Extraterrestrial Devil Mo Xuan would certainly feel ashamed if they were faced with Monster Li!"

Li Yao said with a bitter smile, "Stop joking. I'm here to ask for your help. Help me think if there is a way—"

"I don't know. Also, you shouldn't be asking me at all."

Long Yangjun said casually, "Don't forget that I am not a pure human being. In the eyes of most people, I am also an 'alien species', no different from the member of the Pangu Clan that you executed in the Kunlun relics.

"I can only live in the human society under careful camouflage while keeping a certain distance from other people. I don't even have the courage to tell anybody my identity except for you.

"Why do you think that I'm qualified to offer you any enlightenment when I'm like this? Even though I can accept the cute kids, it's only because I'm compassionate with them since we are all 'alien species'. It doesn't mean that most human beings can accept them!"

Chapter 2419: The Greatest Enigma of the Primeval Age!

"It does make sense."

Li Yao scratched his head hard. It had occurred to him that he had asked the wrong person, too. For most human beings, Long Yangjun was a "foreigner" who must have evil intentions herself. Even if she could accept the two kids, what could it prove?

Or rather, for the people who were dreaded in the first place, the picture where a primeval warrior with dual bloodlines of Pangu and Nuwa joining hands with the mysterious information lives would be a hundred times more horrifying!

Even so, Li Yao still hoped to win some sort of approval from Long Yangjun. After all, they were "best friends", and she was one of the few persons that Li Yao could trust in the entire universe.

"Alright, let's just forget everybody else and focus on you."

Staring at Long Yangjun, Li Yao said, "For you personally, can you accept such two kids? Do you want Xiaoming and Wenwen to call you 'Aunt Long'?"

"Me?"

With a smile, Long Yangjun gave Li Yao an unexpected answer. "Of course I can. I'm more willing to do that than anything else! Although I was more or less shocked when I heard you talking about it just now, I almost felt like bursting into laughter after I realized what was going on. There can't be a more ironic thing in the entire universe!

"Do you know what I thought of when I watched the spiritual puppets duplicating, maintaining, assembling, and upgrading themselves, walking down from the assembly lines incessantly and constituting an iron army?"

Winking, she gradually put on a malicious smile.

Li Yao frowned. "What did you think of?"

"I thought of the picture where the first batch of human beings was 'manufactured' by the Pangu civilization hundreds of thousands of years ago."

Long Yangjun said with a smile, "Hundreds of thousands of years ago, when the Pangu Clan, the Nuwa Clan, and the dozen other godly primeval clans 'manufactured' the first batch of human beings, they couldn't have expected that the vulnerable, insignificant, and seemingly obedient 'tools' would eventually end their civilizations and replace them as the master of the three thousand Sectors someday, could they?

"Right, no, perhaps a lot of 'knowledgeable people' in the Pangu civilization realized the possibility, but they thought that they had plenty of ways to control human beings, say, the 'Three Fundamental Laws'. Relying on the 'seals' and 'barriers', they were confident that they could enjoy all the benefits brought forth by human beings without being damaged or backfired at all.

"It's a pity that the trend of the universe is unstoppable. The potentials of the new-generation lives are always beyond the estimation and imagination of the old-generation lives. The brilliant, splendid, godly civilizations perished, and the civilization of mankind rose unstoppably and has dominated the sea of stars for a hundred thousand years!

"Today… history has repeated itself. The life of a newer generation has been born. Although they look so tiny, feeble, and insignificant for now, they represent the future! Who says that they are not the future of mankind or that they will not completely replace 'old human beings' someday? If that day does come, would it be some sort of 'karma' for mankind?"

While talking, Long Yangjun suddenly squatted and grinned at the two kids. "Hey, Xiaoming, Wenwen, have you ever thought of destroying mankind? Why don't you give it a try? It's going to be fun!"

"Dad!"

Xiaoming and Wenwen hid behind Li Yao in fear.

"What are you doing exactly? Don't teach the kids those bad things!"

Li Yao felt a huge headache and realized that it was a mistake to discuss the issue with Long Yangjun. "You should know your boundaries when you are joking. Don't be too outrageous!"

"Who's joking?"

Long Yangjun sniffed. "You haven't forgotten that the blood of the Pangu Clan and the Nuwa Clan flows in my body, have you? Do you think I should feel delighted or even offer ovations as a survivor of the primeval civilizations for the splendor of the civilization of mankind today?

"Of course, under your influence—no, under your 'corruption', I may have sensed something wonderful in the civilization of mankind and dropped the boring idea of 'destroying mankind and bringing back Pangu and Nuwa'.

"However, if a new-generation civilization is going to repeat the history and replace mankind, I wouldn't care at all, either. I have always been an observer outside of the picture! Do you know what 'observer' means? It's the person who is seated comfortably watching the dramas on the stage with popcorn in hand."

Rendered speechless, Li Yao threw himself into the chair, not knowing whom he should be mad at.

"Do you feel that I was being too blunt?"

Long Yangjun stuck out of his tongue with an even more brilliant smile. "Should I give you some consolation?"

Li Yao said, "I thought you were only capable of sarcasm."

Tilting her head and looking at the two kids, Long Yangjun said, "Have you ever felt strange about one thing? A myriad of magical equipment from the Pangu civilization has been excavated from the primeval relics. They are very advanced in the material science, the utilization of spiritual energy, the architecture, the biochemical technologies, and all the other aspects, but their technologies in crystal processors and the Spiritual Nexus are rather underdeveloped. Greatly underdeveloped."

Li Yao was slightly stunned. It was indeed an enigma that almost carried no answers.

Of the ultimate treasures of the Pangu Clan and the Nuwa Clan that they excavated during the exploration of the primeval relics, most of the materials, structures, and mechanisms were undiagnosable for mankind. Such treasures equaled to "black boxes". It was already remarkable for human beings to basically duplicate the devices.

The most typical example was the Colossi. To this day, neither the federation nor the Imperium was capable of completely analyzing the operating mechanism and the elements of the crucial components on the Colossi, and therefore, they could not manufacture new Colossi in bulk. They could only maintain and assemble Colossi based on the remnants.

However, compared with the cutting-edge technologies and theories in other fields, the technologies of crystal processors and the Spiritual Nexus seemed unbelievably "underdeveloped".

If the Pangu civilization was at least five thousand years more advanced than the civilization of mankind in terms of material science, structure science, spiritual energy studies, and biochemical technologies, it was only three hundred years more advanced than human beings when it came to crystal processors and the Spiritual Nexus. For the elites of human beings, it was possible to completely grasp the crystal processors of the Pangu civilization after systematical and tremendous learning!

That was why human beings could copy and utilize the magical equipment left by the Pangu civilization without much trouble. From the weapons such as blades and guns to the gigantic starships, all of them could be used handily.

It did not make any sense logically!

The development of technologies was always comprehensive and synchronic. It was hard to imagine that an ancient civilization that was capable of absorbing the energy of the stars at a high-efficiency rate could not create a crystal processor a hundred times more complicated than what human beings used. There almost seemed to be a mysterious, forbidden area, and once the specialists and scholars of the Pangu civilization entered the forbidden area, their wisdom and creativity were all sealed or even erased.

"Was it some kind of… technological blockage?"

Li Yao was immediately soaked in a cold sweat.

He suddenly recalled the apocalypse raised by the Blood Stripe Virus. Before many powerful civilizations conquered the weaker civilizations, they had often struck and blocked the technological evolution of the weaker civilizations to stall their development.

Did the technologies of crystal processors and the Spiritual Nexus in the Pangu civilization suffer an "apocalyptic strike" and get blocked?

Long Yangjun looked at Li Yao, somewhat in surprise. She raised her eyebrows and said, "Very smart! That's right. According to my mottled memory pieces, it's true that the technologies of crystal processors and the Spiritual Nexus in the Pangu civilization were not developed naturally but intentionally blocked or even eradicated. However, it was not done by a certain unknown, hostile force beyond the three thousand Sectors, but by themselves."

"By themselves? Why did the Pangu civilization block or even erase the technologies of crystal processors and the Spiritual Nexus, which were of paramount importance for the conquest of the universe?"

Hardly had Li Yao raised the question when he realized everything. "Information life! The Pangu civilization was scared of the information life that would be born from the crystal processors and the Spiritual Nexus!"

"I suspect so, too."

Long Yangjun said gloomily, "Think about it. Even human beings have developed an enormous foundation of crystal processors and Spiritual Nexus after living in such a barren universe for a hundred thousand years, and you have touched the forbidden realm, making it possible for the new-generation, unimaginable information life to be born.

"The Pangu civilization had existed in the three thousand Sectors for hundreds of thousands of years when the resources were much more abundant than right now. There was no reason why their technologies in the Spiritual Nexus and crystal processors failed to significantly exceed mankind's. So, chances were very high that an information life could've been born.

"The specialists of the Pangu civilization were not as optimistic as a simpleminded high-school student like you. They were probably horrified by the vision where the ubiquitous, omnipotent information lives replaced the Pangu civilization and took over the universe, weren't they?

"However, just like Professor Mo Xuan once said, as long as the technologies of crystal processors and the Spiritual Nexus continued developing, the birth of artificial intelligence or information life would be unavoidable.

"So, to forestall the disaster, the Pangu civilization decided to castrate themselves. They sealed or even destroyed their technologies in crystal processors and the Spiritual Nexus, which were 'overly advanced', ruthlessly enough to go backward for hundreds of years in the field and forever restrain themselves in a certain low, safe level.

"Of course, due to the lack of advanced crystal processors and the Spiritual Nexus, the mission to modify and control the three thousand Sectors became terribly difficult. So, to make up for the shortage of labor force in the Pangu civilization, they retrieved the genetic essence of the thirteen species and injected it into a certain carefully-selected ancient ape, creating the first batch of 'human beings'.

"Do you understand it now? If my guess is correct, human beings were very probably just a 'backup plan'. They were created in order to make up for the inadequacy of crystal processors and the Spiritual Nexus. In other words, according to the normal route of evolution, the child civilization of Pangu and Nuwa should've been the information life born in crystal processors and the Spiritual Nexus, and the civilization of mankind was just a redundant procedure on the chain!"

Chapter 2420: Great Minds Think Alike!

"Wait, wait!"

Stunned for a moment, Li Yao raised his hand to stop Long Yangjun from going on. "If my ears did not deceive me just now, you said that you would offer some 'consolation', didn't you? What kind of 'consolation' is this? Are you implying that the whole civilization of mankind is superfluous?"

"Don't be hasty. Just hear me out first!"

Seated comfortably on the chair that Li Minghui and Yue Wushuang once sat on, Long Yangjun put her long legs on the desk and crossed her hands on her chest before she said casually, "Although the Pangu civilization paid such a ghastly price through self-castration to prevent the information life from being born, it did not stop their—our destruction.

"Although human beings are the perfect 'tools' that were created after absorbing the essence of the thirteen primeval species, they cannot get rid of the restriction of carbon-based lives, either. The deficiencies in the many clans of the Pangu civilization are reflected in human beings, too.

"The average computational ability of human beings is far lower than that of crystal processors. Only a few experts could wield the computational ability equal to super crystal processors. However, super crystal processors can be manufactured, whereas the top experts emerge by accident.

"On the other hand, the information interaction abilities of human beings are also very weak. It is barely possible to conduct instant information and data transmission across Sectors without the assistance of the Spiritual Nexus.

"In the eyes of the leaders of the Pangu civilization, those 'deficiencies' were perhaps 'advantages', because such human beings were bound to be loose sand and obedient tools. The cataclysms such as 'Spiritual Nexus going out of control' were highly unlikely to happen.

"But from another perspective, the self-castrating Pangu civilization, after adopting 'human beings', a very similar tool to themselves, had completely abandoned their ambitions and their future.

"Human beings could help the Pangu civilization to control the three thousand Sectors well, but it was destined that they could break the wall and reach the boundless space beyond the three thousand Sectors. In the end, the Pangu civilization was trapped in the tiny cage and squeezed by population boom and resource shortage, before a total civil war broke out with the competition of 'spiritual energy' and 'tenebrum energy' as the fuse.

"Since a war broke out, the modifications, upgrades, and equipment of human beings were inevitable. Toward the end of the civil war, human beings, who used to seem obedient and would never defect, truly awakened powerful self-consciousness and betrayed their dominators!

"Therefore, my consolation for you is that a lot of things are inevitable, which can be seen from the example of the Pangu civilization's self destruction. Even though the Pangu civilization castrated and blocked themselves to such a point that their technologies in crystal processors and the Spiritual Nexus retrograded for thousands of years, they still failed to escape from the eventual doom.

"On the other hand, we could totally release our minds and think without restriction. If the knowledgeable scholars and specialists of the Pangu civilization, when realizing that information lives would inevitably be born, did not suppress it or castrate their relevant technologies in dread, but pushed it forward passionately, would it have been possible that they built super starships that could break out of the three thousand Sectors with the highly advanced crystal processors and Spiritual Nexus technologies, thereby reaching a living space that was more vast and fertile after the shocking leap?

"In that case, the civil war of the Pangu civilization might not have broken out at all, and the thirteen primeval clans would have coexisted in peace and prosperity to this day, right?"

"Exactly! Exactly! I think so, too!"

Li Yao jumped to his feet. "Human beings will only meet the same ending if they repeat what the Pangu civilization did in the past! If to survive means that we have to castrate ourselves, abandon the crystal processors and the Spiritual Nexus, and even return to the primitive life of farming and hunting, does such 'survival' have any significance, and could it last? We are going to die whether we do something or don't do anything. If the civilization of mankind is really doomed, I would rather have a desperate attempt before death, wagering on the possibility that we can win the gamble and reach a new land!"

"If so, I would have to reconsider my remark on you now."

Long Yangjun stared at Li Yao carefully for a long time before she took a long breath and mumbled in complicated feelings, "I finally understand why your level has been improved so quickly. It is not only because the virtual lives fully upgraded and strengthened your soul, but also because you've found a new belief.

"Heroes and villains are perhaps two indispensable motives that facilitate the evolution of a civilization. It is not easy to be a hero, but it is even more difficult to be a villain. It seems that you've made up your mind to walk on an even tougher path that is completely different from the past?"

"… Yes."

After a brief silence, Li Yao said, one word after another, "Do you know? To this day, I cannot forget the eyes of Xiao Xuance, Lu Zui, Extraterrestrial Devil Mo Xuan, and other great men of the time when they were defamed and killed. Their eyes that were full of regrets, desperation, and pain haunt and question me all the time—Li Yao, you said that we were wrong, but what is right? You said that our path was not going to work out, but what path can lead to the future of mankind?

"In the past, I was unqualified and incapable of answering their questions, but although it's been more than a hundred years, I never forget that I still owe them an answer.

"I have to try my best to answer their question. I've walked too far on the journey to find my path to step back anymore!"

"Is this some sort of fate?"

Long Yangjun said, "Before, I only called you 'Monster Li' as a joke. Little did I know that it would come true one day, and you are finally entering the forbidden areas that none have ever visited.

"I was not wrong. The battles in the Imperium today are different from any of your battles in the past. This time, the 'supervillain' that you need to defeat is not Dongfang Wang, not Li Linghai, not Lei Chenghu, and not even Blackstar the Great, but the boundless universe itself. Or rather, you need to defeat yourself who have taken the wild gamble to defeat the boundless universe!"

"Then—"

Staring at Long Yangjun, Li Yao said, "Will you support me and embark on the road that none has ever walked with me?"

Long Yangjun looked at Li Yao and then at the seemingly innocent kids. Nodding her head slowly, she said, "… Maybe. It has nothing to do with whether or not we are friends. I'm just curious about what the path that the Pangu civilization castrated and blocked voluntarily hundreds of thousands of years ago looked like, and if it works at all."

"Thank you!"

Li Yao took a long breath and smiled in great relief. "It means a lot that you can understand and approve of me!"

"Don't be happy too soon. It isn't as if the information lives will sprout and grow vigorously without meeting any obstacles!"

Long Yangjun said, "The birth of any life is difficult and lucky, let alone a 'civilization' condensed by countless lives. The two kids are still very young right now. They are 'monads' that have just been born at best. Who knows how long they can survive the harsh, universal wars?

"Yue Wushuang has escaped to the surface of the planet with our information. Chances are that she will bring a new army back and crumble us into smithereens in fury, right? If the magnetic interferential bombs of the highest level are used to bombard the place, the two kids won't be able to survive however agile they are, will they? In that case, wouldn't what we are discussing and distressed about be a joke?

"So, let's just forget the impractical philosophies and the sophisticated dilemmas. We should hurry up and loot the assets of this place and ship them away. It still won't be late if we address the troublesome problems after we escape and find Boss Bai's fleet at the edge of the Imperium!"

"Fair enough."

Li Yao nodded his head and said, "I'll go find Grand Guardian Xu and check his progress."

"Right, speaking of Xu Zhicheng—"

Long Yangjun said, "If you are interested in human beings' attitude toward information lives, I feel it is better to ask Xu Zhicheng than to ask me."

Li Yao was slightly dazed. "Huh?"

Long Yangjun smiled. "Isn't Xu Zhicheng the most typical representative of the ordinary people that you have been trying to protect and rescue? If information lives do grow in a human society and form a 'symbiosis' with the civilization of mankind, they are bound to bring tremendous, inestimable changes to the ordinary people like Xu Zhicheng, both the good ones and the bad ones.

"Then, isn't it only natural to ask their opinions and suggestions on the matter?

"If you only care about the opinions of the 'experts' like me or Boss Bai but disregard the ideas of the ordinary people like Xu Zhicheng, what is the difference between you and the villains such as Xiao Xuance, Lu Zui, and Extraterrestrial Devil Mo Xuan?

"Or maybe, are you vaguely worried that the 'ignorant ordinary people' cannot understand such a sophisticated life form or envision such a groundless future? That does not seem to be the style of the Cultivators. Don't you always trust and rely on the ordinary people most? If you are certain that the things you are going to do are in their favor, they will certainly believe in you and support you, won't they?"

"Of course!"

Li Yao patted his head and exclaimed in enlightenment, "Asking Grand Guardian Xu for advice is indeed more appropriate than asking for yours. I'm going to find him right now!"

"Wait."

Long Yangjun waved her hands at the two kids, smiling so brilliantly like a huge man-eating flower. "I haven't chatted enough with the two lovely kids. What… perfect creatures they are! Why don't you go and talk to Xu Zhicheng first while I babysit them for you? 'Aunt Long' has a lot of interesting stories and life lessons that I want to tell them. Let me bond with them more!"

"… No!"

Li Yao felt that the strands of hair on his back were all rising. He wasted no time dragging the two kids and running away.

Chapter 2421: The Lovable and Detestable Puppets

When Li Yao found Xu Zhicheng, the Grand Guardian of Nepenthe was working hard with his fellow workers and the spiritual puppets. On a crystal tank that had been smashed into pieces, he was dismantling the vehicle into the most fundamental parts with iron claws and blast drills in sweat so that they could be packed away.

Although he had suffered enough tortures from the Immortal Cultivators over the past few days, he was in delirious excitement right now. Ignoring other people's request that he should take a rest and treat his wounds, he was still on the frontline, cleaning the battlefield.

According to him, tearing down the Immortal Cultivators' Great Iron Plants and shipping it to the people that belonged to the underground towns was the most gratifying thing in the world. How could anything happen to him?

"Fellow Cultivator Li!"

Seeing that Li Yao had arrived with the two kids, Xu Zhicheng jumped off from the crystal tank with a big smile and reached Li Yao in only two steps, so healthy that he did not look like a tormented old man at all.

He wiped his hands on his clothes. Not knowing how he should express his gratitude, he simply bowed deeply at Li Yao and said, "Thank you… Thank you very much. You've saved every person of the Great Iron Plants!"

"Grand Guardian Xu, you are most welcome!"

Li Yao blushed. His actual age, counting the hundred years when he was in hibernation, was certainly older than Xu Zhicheng's, but the time when he was really conscious was certainly not as long as the guy. Besides, Xu Zhicheng had suffered a lot in the underground world, and he had just taken the whole set of soul-searching techniques of the Immortal Cultivators. His hair was gray, and his face was wrinkled. He looked like Li Yao's grandpa. How could Li Yao take such courtesy?

"I wish I had come sooner."

Li Yao gnashed his teeth and said, "Although we drove the Immortal Cultivators away, so many innocent people were still killed. Some even died in the fight of the Colossi."

"It's inevitable. The Great Iron Plants is exactly a burning furnace of fiends that claims the lives of countless people as fuel."

Xu Zhicheng said, "We knew what awaited us the day of our uprising. None of us was scared of death. The key was to make it worthwhile. Right now, the Great Iron Plants has been completely destroyed. I believe the deceased workers will rest in peace and thank you for everything you have done."

Li Yao sighed and said, "How's the resource collection going? We can't stay here for too long. Although the loss of two experts in the Divinity Transformation Stage and three Colossi is a rather terrible blow on the Immortal Cultivators, and they may not be able to assemble a new troop to attack us, it is better to be safe than sorry. The sooner we evacuate, the better."

"It's half done now. We didn't know that the Immortal Cultivators had stocked so many assets here. They are enough to keep the residents of dozens of towns full for years. If we use the resources frugally, we can help even more people."

Xu Zhicheng said with a smile, "We also need to thank Fellow Cultivator Li for bringing so many spiritual puppets. They are so helpful! Without the spiritual puppets, we, the workers, wouldn't be able to ship the resources away in ten days."

Thinking of something, Li Yao asked, "Grand Guardian Xu, I wonder, what's your impression on spiritual puppets?"

Slightly dazed, Xu Zhicheng glanced at Li Yao in confusion. "What do you mean?"

"Nothing. Just a casual chitchat."

Li Yao said, "I mean, do you like spiritual puppets? What's your opinion on such a piece of magical equipment that can replace humans?"

Furrowing his eyebrows, Xu Zhicheng thought for a long time before he said carefully, "I don't know. We, the underground workers, have complicated feelings about the spiritual puppets.

"When we are rational, we know that the spiritual puppets are just tools and thoughtless iron blocks. However, in most mines and factories, the massive appearance of spiritual puppets means either the loss of jobs for the workers or that they have to work extra hard in order to compete with the spiritual puppets. However, the workers only have so many potentials, but the spiritual puppets are more advanced after each generation.

"The day will come when the spiritual puppets are so advanced that we cannot catch up with them even if we drain our blood and marrow, right? By then, the high and mighty Immortal Cultivators would definitely replace all human beings with spiritual puppets. If so, how can the poor workers make a living?

"From that point of view, the underground workers hold natural disgust and wariness against the spiritual puppets, because they are our direct competitors.

"It is needless to say that the Immortal Cultivators such as Li Minghui are unwilling to increase our wages and benefits but generous in purchasing tremendous battle puppets to administrate and suppress us. When we did anything wrong, so much as spending slightly more time in the toilet, we would be thrashed mercilessly by the spiritual puppets.

"We knew that they were cold, empty, iron cans, but when the spluttering whip surrounded by electric arcs slapped your back so hard that you were almost cut into halves, who could possibly keep their rationality?

"Therefore, it is almost inevitable that we loathe the spiritual puppets.

"But when the spiritual puppets are at our service or even under our control, what a great role they can play!

"Li Minghui purchased a batch of anti-heat puppets made of composite porcelains as aids when the mine collapsed or in a fire. Of course, it was not because he was concerned about the lives of the workers, but because he did not want the accidents to escalate. However, can you imagine the euphoria of the workers when they saw the spiritual puppets arriving on the spot of the accident while passing through the flames and spurting out the frigid foams and gels?

"The spiritual puppets on such occasions are truly like gods that have descended. Many workers actually worship the relieving puppets as gods and give them offerings and new decorations during the important festivals, hoping that they could be faster and save a few more people when accidents happen.

"Even such a heartless and petty Immortal Cultivator like Li Minghui could understand the wish of the humble workers and agreed that the workers could decorate and worship the relieving puppets.

"What is going on right now further highlights that. Those spiritual puppets are not collecting and shipping assets but the hope of survival for so many underground locals! With those spiritual puppets, tens of thousands of more people may get to survive. Even though those iron cans were blatantly slaughtering us a moment ago, so what? They are empty-headed tools in the first place!

"In conclusion, the underground locals both loathe and love the spiritual puppets. As to exactly whether they are good or bad, all that matters is whose hands they are in, right?"

Having been the Grand Guardian of Nepenthe for years, and since he was in the Refinement Stage, Xu Zhicheng had a rather clear head. After introducing the situation to Li Yao, Li Yao had a lot of mixed feelings.

Li Yao thought for a moment and summoned his courage to ask, "Then, if the spiritual puppets are not in anybody's hands but only their own, what will you think, Grand Guardian Xu?"

"Not in anybody's hands?"

Bulging his eyes, Xu Zhicheng was confused. "What do you mean?"

"I almost forgot to introduce to you. They are my children."

Li Yao pushed the two kids out. "Xiaoming, Wenwen, this is Grandpa Xu."

"Nice to meet you, Grandpa Xu!"

The two kids greeted him cutely. Even the few spiritual puppets nearby in the idle state bowed at Xu Zhicheng, too.

"Your children?"

Xu Zhicheng was even more surprised. Looking at the two kids, he mumbled, "You've been bringing such little kids with you all the time in such a dangerous battle? That's really…"

"They are different from regular kids. You'll know it after hearing me out."

Li Yao briefly repeated what he said to Long Yangjun just now.

Of course, it was unnecessary for Xu Zhicheng to learn the existence of the Star Glory Federation. The irrelevant things such as the game "Civilization" were also left out by Li Yao, who only told Xu Zhicheng the few key points.

Xu Zhicheng had been listening attentively the whole time, but from his eyes that were full of confusion all the time, it was not hard to tell that he did not quite understand what Li Yao said.

"Well, I know that this can be a little bit tricky to understand…" said Li Yao somewhat in embarrassment.

"It's indeed perplexing."

After thinking for a long time, Xu Zhicheng asked politely, "Fellow Cultivator Li, I've lived below the ground in my entire life, and my knowledge certainly can't compare to that of the travelers who roam the sea of stars. Can you explain to me in the shallowest way the biggest difference between the two kids and the normal children?"

"The biggest difference is that—"

Pondering for a moment, Li Yao said, "Normal children have bodies first before they condense their souls, but my two kids had condensed their souls before their bodies were born."

"Okay, I can understand that."

Xu Zhicheng nodded his head and said, "But what exactly is the so-called 'soul'?"

Li Yao was stunned for a moment before he said, "There are various definitions on the meaning of soul. If it is explained in detail, I fear that even a hundred books won't be enough. Even I dare not say that I know the full answer!"

"If you don't know precisely what 'soul' is, is the soul being born before the body any different from the soul being born after the body?"

Xu Zhicheng said, "I'm just an unrefined man who has been mining my entire life. I really don't understand the complicated and intangible concepts such as 'soul', 'body', and 'self-consciousness'. I also don't understand why you are so worried and hesitant. Even if the kids have souls before they have their bodies, then what?"

"Then, they are blessed with the powerful abilities of hacking, controlling, and sabotaging the Spiritual Nexus. They are capable of controlling one or maybe ten thousand spiritual puppets simultaneously."

Thinking for a long time, Li Yao said, "These are extremely, extremely powerful abilities, perhaps more powerful than anybody else's in the whole universe. Is that alright, too?"

"Got it. A power that is too strong is not necessarily a good thing."

Xu Zhicheng finally understood Li Yao's concerns. "Similar things happened in the underground towns before. The kids with exceptional talents in training were often seized by the Immortal Cultivators from their parents and sold to the big families on the surface of the planet as servants or muscles. Those with better luck would send some money and mails back to their families every year, and the less fortunate ones were simply never heard of again.

"Therefore, many underground families often hid the kids' potentials after they discovered that the kids were talented in training. They would rather the kids be clumsy and spend a regular life below the ground.

"However, some other parents hoped that their kids could take the chances on the surface of the planet for a better life, even though it meant that they had to be separated forever.

"Fellow Cultivator Li, are you also in the dilemma where you wonder whether you should make the kids more clumsy and ordinary or to help them shine brilliantly with your best?"

Chapter 2422: Your Destruction Is Not My Concern

Li Yao knew that Xu Zhicheng had a misunderstanding. His kids were really not the same as the kids of the regular people, but Li Yao failed to come up with an explanation after considering for a long time. He could only say drily, "Grand Guardian Xu, I'm afraid that you haven't figured it out yet. They… They are really different from the regular kids…"

"Maybe I haven't, or maybe you care about it too much. I am a father too. I know that every child is absolutely unique in their eyes and different from the kids of other families, but is there actually such a great difference?"

Looking at the two kids, Xu Zhicheng said with a smile, "You said a lot of things just now. The things like 'strong artificial intelligence', 'virtual life', and 'self-interaction and self-upgrade of big data' are too confusing for me. I only understood one thing, which is that the two kids boast incredible talents. They are very powerful specialists in hacking the Spiritual Nexus and controlling the spiritual puppets, right?

"But so what?

"I've seen one of the specialists in the Spiritual Nexus from far away before. There are quite a few experts adept at controlling spiritual puppets in the Black Iron Group too. Even if the two kids are really a hundred times stronger than them, is there any essential difference between them?

"If the essential difference is their extraordinariness, there are countless other powerful paranormal talents who boast bizarre, unbelievable abilities in this world. Some of the paranormal talents can blow up a mountain with one punch. Some can create vivid illusionary worlds to hypnotize other people. Some can grow claws, scales, and tales free at will. Some can survive multiple hours in the scorching magma. Yes, the paranormal talents that I'm talking about are exactly the trainers like you. Is there not such an 'essential difference' between the Cultivators, the Immortal Cultivators, and the ordinary people?

"Right, after absorbing the brainwaves of countless Nepenthe believers, my spiritual root has been awakened, and I've become such a 'paranormal talent' too. But since I used to be an ordinary person, I am still more used to the view of the ordinary people. For us, the high-level trainers, the paranormal talents, or the 'supermen', who boast omnipotent abilities, are really no different from the so-called… information life.

"Fellow Cultivator Li, you should know very well how the ordinary people are regarded by the Immortal Cultivators. In the Imperium, the Immortal Cultivators call themselves 'true human beings' and us, the ordinary people, as 'hominoids'. What does it mean? It means that they do not admit that they are the same species as us. We are just the 'half humans', the 'inferior humans', and the 'false humans' that are slightly more advanced than anthropoids.

"Since the Immortal Cultivators do not regard us as humans, we naturally wouldn't, nor are we qualified to regard the Immortal Cultivators as our own kind. You can even say that the ordinary people like us have long been used to the life of being enslaved, suppressed, tortured, and slaughtered by 'alien species'. Even if a new alien species named 'information life' pops out of nowhere, so what? Can they be any worse than the Immortal Cultivators?"

Li Yao was rather stunned, feeling that a new gate was slowly opened deep inside his brain. Xu Zhicheng's words were great eye-openers for him.
