As for the obvious contradiction that the height of the tunnel was only no more than fifty meters while the working surface of the ultra-huge shield tunneling machines was a hundred meters in diameter, it was not really a problem.

The ultra-huge shield tunneling machines seemed to be magnificent iron beasts, but they could be extremely fragile on the other hand, and every second of their working time mattered. Unless it was absolutely necessary, it would be best to minimize their working duration.

Dismantling the ultra-huge shield tunneling machines into thousands of units, shipping them to the spot through heavy vehicles, and assembling them again before they were put to work was the most efficient way of using them.

Knowing that the man-made tunnel must be leading to Blackstar the Great's "trump card", they were not surprised to hear the deafening noises as if a hundred thousand waterfalls were pouring ahead of them and see the Imperial Guards who had been armed to the teeth after sneaking forward for another two hours.

Not just Li Yao and Long Yangjun, even Li Jialing who had just finished one strengthening treatment could've taken down the Imperial Guards more than easily.

But alarming them in such a way was unnecessary. They chose a safer approach.

While everybody lurked in a dark corner of the man-made tunnel, Li Yao drilled a hole the size of a fingernail on the wall with Neltharion, which directly led to the heated working spot up ahead. Then, Li Xiaoming and Li Wenwen controlled abundant mechanical spiders and crawled in through the hole, sending back the image and the waves that they had scanned so that everybody could share the vision of the mechanical spiders.

The pictures transmitted by Neltharion and the mechanical spiders made it hard for them to breathe, and they almost exclaimed in shock.

The end of the man-made tunnel was simply too huge and vast.

Li Yao had seen countless enormous underground spaces. He had even seen a silver "ocean" below the ground of Boneyard earlier.

But all the underground spaces combined were not as shocking as the void he was seeing right now.

The diameter and height of this underground space were at least ten times more than that of the Great Iron Plants. It could've accommodated dozens of underground towns and was like a tiny blackhole embedded inside the capital planet, absorbing the rocks nearby incessantly.

As far as the eyes could reach, stairs and platforms that led downward helically were everywhere. There were also giant, horizontal caves on the wall, like some sort of beehives and ant nests with complicated structures.

Some of the "ant nests" were the camps of the Imperial Guards and were surrounded by impenetrable bastions of crystal cannons and cubic defense systems, while some of the caves were factories where shield tunneling machines were assembled and maintained. They were all fairly large in scale.

Li Yao maneuvered Neltharion and carefully changed the direction to go downward. He saw that quite a few ultra-huge shield tunneling machines were excavating and shattering without any stop on the platform close to the bottom of the gigantic hollow. They were also followed by a hundred large, medium, and small shield tunneling machines. Their sizes were so obviously different that it was like several giant beasts going hunting with their children.

Those few shield tunneling machines were far from enough to establish such a vast underground space so quickly!

Thinking quickly, Li Yao was immediately amused by his silliness. Such a "boundless" space couldn't have been dug out by Blackstar the Great alone. It was probably created by the Supreme Emperor's or the Blood God's armies like Long Yangjun had said. Wuying Qi had only inherited the cause of the ancestors and continued digging downward.

BOOM!

BOOM! BOOM!

BOOM, BOOM, BOOM, BOOM!

Right then, they heard patterned explosions from the depths of the underground space.

"Are they blowing up the rocks?"

Long Yangjun asked Li Yao in a low voice.

"No."

Li Yao stuck his ear and cheek to the wall and sensed carefully for a moment before he shook his head and said, "They're scanning the structure of the deeper parts by detonating the crystal bombs and examining the feedback."

It was a regular approach in underground explorations to scan the environment with the feedback of waves.

Judging from the distribution of the wave sources, the Immortal Cultivators had dug out a relatively shallow tunnel in every direction radially and planted a group of special crystal bombs at the end of each tunnel so that they could scan the whole environment.

In other words, whatever Wuying Qi asked them to search for, they hadn't made any breakthroughs yet but only searching randomly.

Li Yao was more or less relieved after discovering that.

"At least a whole legion of the Imperial Guards has been deployed here. Also, judging from the new camps and fortresses being built, the scale of the garrison will further increase."

Narrowing his eyes, Li Yao analyzed the high-definition image that Neltharion sent back carefully. "No wonder Li Linghai paid so much attention to the Imperial Guards. I had been curious about that. Most of the Imperial Guards had little combat abilities and would be crushed the moment they encountered the enemies on a battlefield. They were not of much use however loyal they might be.

"As it turns out, Li Linghai or Wuying Qi didn't plan to ask the Imperial Guards to go to the battlefield at all but are using them as 'engineering soldiers'. This is rather an appropriate use."

Li Yao glanced around and did not notice any expert as far as he could see. Thinking of something, he chuckled and said, "What do you say we suddenly march down and strike on them?"

Chapter 2430: Insane Wuying Qi!

"I say it's a bad idea."

After carefully observing the configuration of troops and the defense system of the underground space, Long Yangjun rejected Li Yao's impulsive offer.

First of all, the underground space was guarded by at least one strengthened legion. Also, crystal cannons and fixed fortresses had been established on all the platforms from top to bottom, constituting an impenetrable web of the crossfire. The place was ten times better defended than the Great Iron Plants.

Even they could charge forward and go on a killing spree with their Colossi, they would certainly be absorbed by the web of the crossfire. Their fuel and ammunitions would be greatly consumed, and the Colossi would be riddled with holes too.

Of course, assuming that the enemy was not supervised by leaders who were above the Nascent Soul Stage and also equipped with a Colossus, it would be possible for them to crush the whole legion. The Imperial Guards were not the most determined warriors. It was possible that they would completely collapse the moment they saw that two Colossi showed up.

Then, so what?

To some extent, both the Imperial Guards and the shield tunneling machines were "consumable". Even if they were to annihilate all the Imperial Guards in this place and destroyed all the shield tunneling machines, Wuying Qi and Li Linghai, having occupied 99% of the resources of the Empyreal Terminus Sector, could easily devote more "consumables" to the place.

Even though the production and maintenance of the ultra-huge shield tunneling machines could be tricky, the regular shield tunneling machines could certainly be shipped in incessantly.

Their reckless attack would be entirely pointless except for slightly delaying Wuying Qi's excavation progress. Instead, it would only alarm the enemy, inform Wuying Qi and Li Linghai of where they were, and let the enemy know that their trump card had been exposed.

Once Wuying Qi discovered that his trump card had been exposed, he would certainly change the way of playing it. Under the stimulation, he might even sacrifice millions of people just in order to accelerate the excavation without bothering the consequences.

Right now, the greatest advantage of Li Yao and his team was that they were in the dark while the enemy was in the light. Wuying Qi and Li Linghai did not know the first thing about their latest action. Naturally, they would not jump to the enemy's face easily.

Unless, of course, they could destroy the underground space entirely.

But it was certainly not an easy task!

The underground space had been an empty cave in the first place. How exactly could it be destroyed? Even if they could blow up the rocks around and fill the space, the loose soil could easily be dug through by the shield tunneling machines and the excavators again.

What was even more critical was that this underground space was probably on an extremely vulnerable seismic belt. The exploration of the Supreme Emperor and the Blood God ten thousand years ago had already resulted in the drastic changes in the crust of the entire planet, leaving thousands of gorges on the vast continent. There was no telling how many people were killed in the catastrophe.

The capital today had multiple times more population than it did in the age of the Star Ocean Imperium. Even the giant gorges torn apart in the disaster were now full of underground towns where billions of people and even more were residing.

If they insisted on destroying the underground space, the catastrophe ten thousand years ago might be repeated. Countless towns would be swallowed by rocks, and too many people would fall into darkness forever. Billions of residents might be affected.

Blackstar the Great perhaps did not care about that. Billions of people were but a number for him.

But Li Yao certainly could not do that.

"Be careful. This area has been frequented by earthquakes for quite a few days."

Long Yangjun said in a low voice, "Do you still remember how I mediated in the war among the three wildling tribes, namely the Ning Wings, the Black Armors, and the Red Rings? The reason why the wildlings were fighting each other was that their habitats were destroyed in the consecutive minor earthquakes. Those earthquakes happened a lot over the past half year but were never heard of several years ago.

"I suspect that it was what Blackstar the Great did here that triggered the series of chain reactions in the thousand square kilometers around.

"He can be blatant and pay no attention to the prices, but can you?"

After a long silence, Li Yao mumbled, "What is Blackstar the Great trying to do exactly? Doing such crazy things on a highly-sensitive seismic belt can really lead to the collapse of the entire capital planet!

"Although 'blowing up a planet' might be an exaggeration, the possibilities that the plate movements become highly active, the continent sinks into the ocean, mountains rise from the ocean, and the whole ecological circle and even the atmosphere fall apart, turning the habitable planet into a desolate planet that's no longer habitable… are quite high!

"Is he crazy enough to abandon his own capital?"

"Don't forget Blackstar the Great's most remarkable battle on his rise."

Long Yangjun said, "When he was still a general of the border defense troops of the Star Ocean Republic, faced with the fleets of the Covenant Alliance, he had once abandoned 'Karan', where his command center was located, and installed powerful crystal bombs that could raise a chain reaction below the ground of the planet, alluring the Covenant Alliance to occupy Karan so that the planet would be blown up together with the enemy's fleets!

"So, this is apparently not the first time that he has destroyed a planet. Today, the four Kurfürst families are obviously stronger than him when combined. Who knows whether or not he will play the same trick if he wants to win quickly?"

Taking a deep breath, Li Yao said in a low voice, "In order to excavate the primeval treasures buried deep below the ground and to defeat the four Kurfürst families, Blackstar the Great has already made up his mind to sacrifice the hundred billion residents on the capital planet for the purpose? It's truly as expected of the ancestor of Immortal Cultivators!

"Right now, the Imperium has been controlled by him. We have to bring everything we've seen out of the capital and let anyone who objects to Blackstar the Great know. Only in such a way can we reveal his insanity!"

Long Yangjun said, "We have captured all the pictures."

Li Yao shook his head and said, "Shooting pictures from far away is not convincing enough. Such 3D videos can easily be fabricated. Besides, spying from such a long distance away can barely prove anything.

"We have to retrieve a working log of one of the ultra-huge shield tunneling machines. Let's see what we should do… Hey, if your 'clones' can approach the ultra-huge shield tunneling machines, how long will it take to copy the machine's log?"

Li Yao turned around and asked the kids.

"It depends on how long the log is and if it is encrypted and defended."

Li Wenwen said, "But I don't think it will take too long. After all, it is just a piece of clumsy, crude civil magical equipment. Blackstar the Great couldn't have installed the most advanced super crystal processors on every shield tunneling machine, right?"

"Ten seconds!"

Li Xiaoming, on the other hand, bashed his chest and guaranteed, "Ten seconds will be enough for us to copy a complete working log!"

"Very well. Then…"

Li Yao narrowed his eyes and observed for a while longer before he said, "Considering the erosion and the cooldown time, the No. 2 ultra-huge shield tunneling machine on the left side must've been used for the longest time, so its log must have the most abundant content!"

The team observed in silence for a long time to determine what the few ultra-huge shield tunneling machines were going to do next. Then, Li Yao activated Neltharion again and bypassed it from the flank before he planted a super-tiny crystal bomb on the most vulnerable point in the rocks that the No.2 ultra-huge shield tunneling machine was going to excavate.

When the No.2 ultra-huge shield tunneling machine's working surface was revolving at the highest speed and all the vibration sawtooth and blast drills were letting out deafening noises, Li Yao remotely controlled the tiny crystal bomb to explode, which raised a chain reaction in the rocks, collapsing the cave that the No.2 ultra-huge shield tunneling machine just dug out!

The explosion of the crystal bomb was completely dwarfed by the noises when the machine was working.

Also, during the excavation of the shield tunneling machines, such minor collapses were anything but unusual and could not hurt the machines at all.

All kinds of ambulances and spiritual puppets immediately ran close and injected liquid metals and buffer foams into the collapsed rocks to strengthen the structure, while more than ten heavy tractors dragged out the shield tunneling machine that had been buried in rocks.

Such accidents were almost happening on a daily basis. No Immortal Cultivators felt that it was strange at all.

The metal scraps left by the tiny crystal bomb after it exploded had also been mixed in the collapsed rocks, never to be found again.

In the meantime, during the several minutes when the ultra-huge shield tunneling machine was buried in the pebbles, countless tiny "ants" crawled into the gaps of the ultra-huge shield tunneling machine through the cracks of the rocks, hacking its mainframe crystal processor and scanning and copying tremendous information.

Before the heavy tractors were in position, all of Xiaoming and Wenwen's clones had retreated safely without leaving any traces behind.

The working log had been retrieved!

"Let's go!"

Li Yao raised his thumb at the two kids, who immediately smiled as brilliantly as blossoming flowers.

They were about to retreat when deafening noises echoed from the other side of the tunnel, and the whole ground began to shake. Another engineering team that was full of assets, tractors, and shield tunneling machines had come to support the project.

The team was so overwhelming that it took them half an hour before they all reached the base.

The crazy excavation in the underground space was even more heated than before.

Everybody looked at each other, all feeling that time was burning quickly like the fuse on a powder keg.

Li Yao thought of something and suddenly asked, "Where do you say is the start point of this tunnel? We are now twenty thousand meters below the surface of the planet. This tunnel can't possibly be extended upward for twenty thousand meters all the way to the ground, right?"

It did make sense.

If the tunnel had been extended upward for twenty thousand meters, considering the slant, the total length of the tunnel could've been more than a hundred thousand meters, which would've been too heavy a load for the project.

Li Yao and the team simply went back and searched for the start point of the tunnel.

Instead of a hundred thousand meters, they explored forward for only some ten kilometers before they reached the start point of the tunnel, which was another huge camp and advance base.

Standing at the center of the advance base, which was carved with complicated rune arrays and glamorous spiritual stripes that were glowing incessantly, was a massive portal.

Chapter 2431: Two Imperiums

The portal looked like an enormous palace in the shape of a pyramid and released pulsating, intense spiritual waves every five minutes. As the gold brilliance raised tiny ripples in the space, the crystal suit troops that had been armed to the teeth and the heavy tractors showed up. The half-enclosed cars of a lot of the heavy tractors were also carrying medium and small shield tunneling machines as well as the consumable parts of the large ones.

A few support troops were being gathered quickly and would probably take shape in a day or two before it was sent to the working stop more than ten kilometers away.

After observing in secret for a long time, Li Jialing couldn't help but say, "So, this is only a transfer station but not the original start point. It would be great if we can know where this giant, pyramid-shaped portal leads to exactly."

Li Yao whispered something to the kids. More than ten "tiny ants" immediately crawled to the advance base, but instead of approaching the portal, they simply gathered some stones, dust, and powder on the ground that had just been rolled over by the heavy tractors.

Li Yao divided the particles that they collected into two parts. One part was placed into a piece of magical equipment that could determine the elements of materials for tests, while he picked up the other part with his fingers and sniffed it carefully.

"Judging from the saltiness, humidity of the particles, and the metal elements they contain, they should be somewhere close to the temperate zone on the east coastline of the main continent of the capital planet. Also, there are quite a few mines of rare metals below the ground."

After saying that, Li Yao glanced at the result of the analysis of the magical equipment and grinned. "Yes, that is also what the test of the magical equipment says. The soil and dust that the tracks of the heavy tractors brought from the other side of the portal contain the elements of twenty-five rare metals. While the metals are not really precious, there are few places on the entire planet where all twenty-five metals can be found at the same time.

"Xiaoming, Wenwen, you must have the files of all the large mining and refining bases on the capital planet in your databases, right? Search and screen it with the following conditions.

"First of all, located on the east coastline of the main continent of the planet, thirty degrees, plus or minus five degrees, north latitude.

"Secondly, there are mines that contain the following twenty-five rare metals within a thousand kilometers or the massive magical equipment factories where such minerals are used. The mother lodes in the mines can't be too deep, or the metal particles wouldn't have floated to the air and dropped into the soil.

"Thirdly, I think the place can't be too far away from the few main garrisons of the Imperial Guards on the capital planet, but that may be questionable."

"Input the few conditions into the databases and see if there's any result now!"

Xiaoming and Wenwen both nodded their heads. While weird brilliance shot out of their eyes, their portal crystal processors projected interconnected rays of light that constituted a 3D light beam, displaying a narrow, long area on the main continent of the capital planet.

"This is…"

Li Yao felt that the area was rather familiar. Thinking quickly, he immediately realized where it was. "Isn't it where the royal palace and the Emperors' Mausoleum are at?"

There was a massive portal deep inside the royal palace or the Emperors' Mausoleum of the Imperium of True Human Beings, which directly led to a tunnel deep below the ground created by the Supreme Emperor or the Blood God in the past.

In the meantime, there was still no telling what kind of astounding secrets were hidden in the mysterious space at the end of the tunnel.

Such a discovery made Li Yao and the rest of them rather anxious.

All the intelligence that could be collected had been collected. The few of them were far from enough to compete with Blackstar the Great. It was time to jump out of the capital, gather all the forces that could be gathered, and reveal Wuying Qi's scheme before marching back unstoppably!

Li Yao and his companions retreated without a sound and returned to the narrow cracks next to the giant tunnel where they came from.

After they reached the place that the Immortal Cultivators couldn't sense them anymore, the two Colossi finally showed up again and moved all the way upward. Three days later, they saw the sunlight again!

At this moment, it had been more than a month since Li Yao and Long Yangjun's great battle against Blackstar the Great at the bottom of the Silver Moon Ocean.

In only one month, the sky of the capital had changed the color.

With his identity as "Blackstar the Great", Wuying Qi had won the support of the influential persons in the four Kurfürst families, including "Devilish Abyss" Li Jianyi and "Star Breaker" Song Huanzhen, and the loyalty of the young generals such as Dongfang Sheng again. As a result, the reformists were ten times more powerful than before.

On the four Kurfürst families' side, in only one day, they lost Prime Minister Dongfang Wang and "Silver Fox" Li Jiande. It was like the sky had half collapsed. They were showing the trend of division.

Wuying Qi conquered the coalition fleet that was sent to the Silver Moon Ocean to hunt Dongfang Wang through thunderous methods. After the fleet was mixed with his own Deep Sea Fleet, the brand-new central troops of the reformists were formed.

The Imperial Guards who were naturally on his side, based on the central troops, became the enormous skeleton and the flesh and blood. Even though the Imperial Guards might be too weak to fight the elite troops of the four Kurfürst families in space, they were more than enough for the battles within the atmosphere in their hometown.

Besides, Blackstar the Great was watching them in person!

Although the battles might not have been one-sided, the nobles who had lost two Kurfürsten consecutively were certainly not determined to fight on.

The capital was not their own base at all. Faced with the drastic changes, it seemed a better option for them to run back to their hometowns, gather the troops and resources, and even recall the expedition armies that were still in the frontline against the Covenant Alliance, before they made thorough plans to deal with the enemy.

Just like that, in only one month, the reformists became the new master of the capital.

Emperor Shenwu's demise, on the other hand, was also spread to the entire capital and the whole universe through the open and secret channels.

The reformists regarded the four Kurfürst families as the criminals who killed their monarch, swearing that they would get to the bottom of the truth and avenge His Majesty. In the unusual period when the truth was not out and revenge was not sought, the current Queen Li Linghai and the future Empress Dowager of the Imperium became the regent to clean, reestablish, and expand the Council of Elders.

The claim to the supreme throne could always be decided after a new Council of Elders that was just, bright, and clean was born.

"Five hundred years! The Imperium has been sinking in darkness for five hundred years! It is time to reform the Imperium and bring justice and light to all the people!"

On the official level, the reformists shouted such an ambiguous slogan.

But in private, a lot of zealous reformists, as well as the young officers and low-ranked Immortal Cultivators who used to be hopelessly suppressed, had already declared that the Kurfürst system should be abolished and all the glories should go to His Majesty and the Council of Elders.

In many half-official orders and announcements, the term "illegitimate Kurfürsten" had shown up a lot, too.

By the so-called "illegitimate Kurfürst", did they mean that the current Kurfürsten did not deserve their places and should be replaced by somebody else, or did they mean that the title of "Kurfürst" should not exist at all?

The result probably had to be decided by the starships, the Colossi, and the crystal suits of the two parties.

Faced with the aggressive reformists, the four Kurfürst families naturally did not give in but responded immediately.

However unfavorable the situation in the capital was, the territories of the Kurfürst families were basically intact even though the "Astounding Thunder Fleet" had been nailed to the heartland of the Li family.

They were the time-tested noble families that had controlled the Imperium for five hundred years after all. Despite all the problems they had, including the lack of leadership, the disagreement among the families, the outbreaks of strikes and student movements in their territories, the instability of the young officers and the low-level Immortal Cultivators… the four Kurfürst families had not suffered any critical hit yet.

Now that the reformists suddenly turned aggressive after part of the unstable factors were separated from them, the four Kurfürst families actually became more united than ever.

Naturally, they accused the reformists of regicide and even narrowed the target down to Li Linghai, the leader of the reformists. They claimed that Li Linghai was the real murderer and described the whole process of the murder so vividly as if they had seen it with their own eyes.

Very ironically, while the "proofs" of the four Kurfürst families were all fabricated, they were in fact not wrong.

Now that Li Linghai was guilty of regicide, she was certainly unqualified to be the queen of the Imperium or the Empress Dowager in the future.

The new emperor could not be selected from the princes that were born by Li Linghai or decided by the "illegitimate Council of Elders". Instead, the glorious tradition of the past five hundred years should be observed, and it should be the four Kurfürst families who made the call.

The nobles had found quite a few suitable candidates from the peripheral bloodlines of the royal family. They were still doing the final screening and exchange of interests. There was no doubt that a new emperor would soon claim the throne in a certain Sector outside of the capital under the support of the four Kurfürst families.

After that, the army of nobles loyal to the new emperor would naturally march at the rebels lurking in the capital thunderously and destructively, carrying out the righteous execution on the "illegitimate regent" Li Linghai who had committed the unpardonable crime of regicide.

As a result, there were two highest authorities of the Imperium of True Human Beings at the center of the cosmos, one being the "Reform Committee of the Council of Elders", and the other being the coalition army made of the traditional nobles centered at the four Kurfürst families.

Both parties claimed to be the rightful government of the Imperium of True Human Beings. While at loggerheads with each other, they also sent tremendous orders, appointments, and warrants to the Immortal Cultivators in the entire universe, hoping to win more Immortal Cultivators who were still wavering over to their own side.

Chapter 2432: In the Name of the Imperium!

When the two "highest authorities of the Imperium of True Human Beings" were about to collide, a lot of people of the Kurfürst families, as well as the notable nobles and great merchants who were deeply associated with them, were still lingering on the capital that was under the control of the reformists.

The capital was the heart of the Imperium after all. Any noble or sect that was slightly capable would deploy an office in the capital to claim their interests. There were also a lot of lobbying groups whose livelihood depended on the Council of Elders. Tremendous descendants of the noble families were studying in the hundred advanced academies, too.

Blackstar the Great took action simply too fast. In only three days, through "Devilish Abyss" Li Jianyi, "Star Breaker" Song Huanzhen, and other experts in the Divinity Transformation Stage, he had won over quite a few fleets of the four Kurfürst families and launched unexpected attacks on the armies of the nobles that were stationed in the space fortresses in the Empyreal Terminus Sector.

By the time the confused nobles realized what was going on, the fleets of the reformists had surrounded the atmosphere of the capital planet and began to separate and swallow the remaining troops in the vast area of the Empyreal Terminus Sector.

In the turbulent month, the most popular saying in the Imperium was "in the name of the Imperium".

For the big nobles as well as the magnates and merchants who were associated with them, those words were like a death sentence.

"In the name of the Imperium, the accounts as well as the assets of your sect in the capital will be frozen for military usages. Please rest assured. After the cause to reform the Imperium is completed, they will be returned to your sect with interests!"

"In the name of the Imperium, in the name of His Majesty, and in the name of all the people of the Imperium, we are going to carry out the sacred sentence on you, a shameless traitor who betrayed the Imperium and the civilization of mankind. Die now, traitor!"

"In the name of the Imperium, for the great cause to reform the Imperium, all the Immortal Cultivators in your families whose spiritual roots are awakened are now recruited for this sacred war. This is your ultimate glory. All the Immortal Cultivators on the list, please gather and come with us!"

The Imperial Guards, the low-level Immortal Cultivators, and the young officers congregated into a furious, unstoppable torrent that cleaned the forces of the big nobles in the Imperium ruthlessly. Similar orders were echoing everywhere in the capital every day.

All the assets in the noble families, which had a history of hundreds of years, were frozen and confiscated. Even the crystals, magical equipment, and Heavenly Materials and Earthly Treasures that the families kept in private were found without leaving out any. All the Immortal Cultivators were brought away and tied to the tank of the reformists after being dispersed.

For the nobles who were relatively "docile", the reformists would give them a document that had the seal of the "Reform Committee of the Council of Elders", solemnly swearing that their assets would be returned with benefits on the day of success.

Of course, considering that those Immortal Cultivators were all sent to the most dangerous battlefields, few might be able to survive a couple of months. By then, even if the Reform Committee of the Council of Elders really abided by the promise, it remained to be seen who would claim their heritages in the end.

The situation was certainly not in their favor. Many quick-witted and determined nobles insisted on refusing the paperwork of the Reform Committee of the Council of Elders but "donated" all their belongings to "the great cause to reform the Imperium" just so that the glory of the Imperium a thousand years ago could be restored sooner.

Those nobles immediately became the models that the reformists praised relentlessly. The descendants of their families were moved from the most dangerous, cannon-fodder troops to the relatively safe garrisons and logistical troops. Also, they were given another promise—in the war against the four Kurfürst families, once the enemy was completely crushed, they would have their fair share in the astronomical trophies, too.

The wealth they lost today would return in double by then.

It was a natural-born skill of the Immortal Cultivators to shift sides.

After a few examples were set, the reformists soon combined most of the strength of the nobles in the capital.

Of course, not all the nobles trapped in the capital were as wise and generous.

Many nobles could not bear to give up the wealth they had accumulated through hard work for hundreds of years, and some did not believe that the insignificant reformists could accomplish their purpose. They resisted the Reform Committee of the Council of Elders to the end.

Very soon, they got a taste of the iron fists of the reformists. Not only were they branded as traitors and their belongings confiscated, but their family members were all enlisted to the daring teams whose odds of survival were slim.

It remained to be seen if the four Kurfürst families could make a comeback, but even if they could, those families would have been razed to the ground without any dust left.

Under the crush of the merciless reformists, a lot of other nobles chose to flee together with the remaining troops of the four Kurfürst families.

The atmosphere had been tightly surrounded by the fleets of the reformists. The dozens of space fortresses that defended the capital had fallen to the hands of the reformists, too. Almost no starship could break out of the atmosphere by force.

Any starship that acted recklessly would be torn into shreds by the fierce firepower on the space fortresses soon after they broke out of the atmosphere.

Even though they were not torn into shreds, they could barely perform a space jump when their shell was broken, if they did not intend to be obliterated into nothingness after being involved in the storms in the four-dimensional space.

With that in mind, there was only one way to escape from the capital.

First of all, they would be teleported to the edge of the Empyreal Terminus Sector in a random jump through the super teleportation arrays on the surface of the planet, leaping over the fleets and the space fortresses that surrounded the planet, before they activated the space jump systems of the starships and ran away.

In terms of space jump, the Imperium of True Human Beings was a hundred years more advanced than the Star Glory Federation.

In the Heaven's Origin Sector, there was only one super teleportation array, "Heaven's Artillery", and the Blood Demon Sector only had "Eye of Blood Demon". But at the capital and the heart of the Imperium, more than ten super teleportation arrays with astonishing capacities had been established.

The ten or so super teleportation arrays were the most precious emergence exits and had helped countless nobles and troops to escape from the capital over the past month.

Of course, such locations were the primary targets of the reformists, too. After a month of fierce battles, they had mostly fallen into the hands of the reformists.

To this day, there was only one last super teleportation array that was still in the hands of the four Kurfürst families. It was known as the "Netherwind Channel".

It was a super teleportation array with a rather unique structure. Most of the framework was established inside a continuous, highly-rising mountain. A helical tunnel had been dug out inside the mountain, and when a starship entered the mountain from the bottom of it and got accelerated on the rail of the helical tunnel, it would eventually rush out from the enormous cave at the peak and rip the void, arriving at any random point at the vast space at the edge of the Empyreal Terminus Sector.

The highly-rising mountain was named the Netherwind Mountain. It was how the "Netherwind Channel" got its name.

It could be easily seen from the structure that was hidden deep inside the mountain that the super teleportation array had been designed as a military teleportation array of the highest defense when it was established.

Based on the entire Netherwind Mountain, too many fixed firing points, fortresses, underground arsenals, and anti-air cannons had been constructed. The mountain had also been strengthened by countless defense rune arrays. Even if the whole mountain was reduced to the ground by blanket bombardments, the super teleportation array inside might not be damaged at all.

After hundreds of years of development, a major city named "Netherwind City" had been established at the foot of the mountain, too. The inhabitants of the city were mostly the people of the four Kurfürst families who were sent to the capital. It was not hard to imagine their attitudes toward the so-called "Reform Committee of the Council of Elders".

When all the strongholds in the capital had fallen into the hands of Blackstar the Great, the Netherwind Channel became the only island of hope in the tsunami raised by the reformists.

All the nobles who craved to escape from the capital had been gathered here, putting up resistance while being desperate to board on starships, crawl into the channel, and flee.

The army of the reformists followed them closely. They had surrounded Netherwind City and the Netherwind Channel from the sky, on the ground and below the ground, determined to block the last way of escape of the nobles and remove their last banner in the capital.

However, the battle did not go as well as planned.

It was partly because of the special territory of the Netherwind Channel and the residents of Netherwind City. But more important, it was because too many nobles had been gathered here.

The fairly-small city was perhaps the place with the highest density of Immortal Cultivators in the entire capital, or even the entire Imperium.

The weapons of the traditional nobles were aiming at the sky like quills on the hedgehog, making the reformists rather hesitant about where to attack.

It was a common sense in military arts to leave some hope for the enemy. At this moment, even if the reformists were capable of blocking the Netherwind Channel, they would not dare to do it recklessly, fearing that the hopeless nobles would launch crazy counterattacks in their desperation, which might result in heavy casualties. If so, the losses would outweigh the gains.

For the reformists, they were also thinking about something else. The "Netherwind Channel" could only teleport a limited number of starships every day. Of a hundred nobles, few could successfully escape. The nobles left behind were all valuable talents, and it would be a shame to kill all of them. If the reformists could make them surrender, the comparison between the two parties would change quickly. That would certainly be for the best.

Therefore, the battle in the Netherwind Channel had been protracted to a whole month without being ended. By the time Li Yao and his companions finally reached the Netherwind Mountain, the whole mountain was still enshrouded in the smoke of war!

Chapter 2433: The City of Fire!

"It's like… the end of the world!"

Hiding in a trench on the Netherwind Mountain, Li Yao looked at the bleeding, hellish battlefield far away and observed in amazement.

Come to think of it, it was the first time he had witnessed a ground siege of the highest intensity in the Imperium of True Human Beings.

The battles on the surface of a planet within the atmosphere were vastly different from the space battles in the vacuum.

Even though the scale and intensity of the space battles were a hundred times higher, since sounds could not be transmitted in the vacuum, and the distance between each other was too far apart, it was often the case that when a starship turned into an enormous fireball in silence, the fireball would perish very soon, bringing away the lives of thousands of people.

The deaths in the vacuum were greatly diluted by silence and distance. Lives were nothing but cold and hollow numbers. Those who were less imaginative could hardly understand how cruel the battles were.

But the ground battles before his eyes were entirely different.

The air was hot, the earth was shaking, and the moans and screams of human beings, accompanied by the explosions of cannons and collapses of fortresses, brushed everyone's eardrums and hearts like stinky tides.

The whole process where vigorous lives were shattered, decayed, and obliterated was displayed in front of everyone in the most vivid scene. The indescribable sensational stimulation was a hundred times more than that in space battles and could really drive people mad.

Netherwind City was surrounded by mountains on three sides. The mountains rose high into the sky and were very steep. It was very hard for the soldiers to be deployed.

In the meantime, tremendous anti-air bases had been established based on the mountains, where all the ammunitions and fuel of the four Kurfürst families that were still lingering in the capital were stored. Every second, ten thousand fiery snakes were dancing in the sky crazily. It was impossible for the low-altitude warships of the reformists to break in at all.

In terms of remote firepower, the reformists had deployed a thousand giant cannons in the places on the mountains where it was relatively smooth, shooting tons of heavy crystal cannonballs at Netherwind City incessantly.

However, Netherwind City boasted a comprehensive, multilayer defense system. The whole city, as well as the border of the Netherwind Channel, was covered in translucent, hexagonal spiritual shields that were glowing.

Outside of the spiritual shields were the twisting force fields and the detonation force fields, which twisted the air into giant vortexes.

When the heavy crystal cannons of the reformists were shot over, they would be detonated in advance while they were flying. Then, all the shrapnel would be blocked or deviated by the twisting force fields. Even though some of the pieces or blasts managed to leak in, they would still be offset by the spiritual shields.

Despite the deafening explosions and the grand earthquakes, they would not deal too much damage to the city and the super teleportation array.

There were also dozens of secret bastions of heavy cannons at the heartland of the Netherwind Mountain. All the critical locations within five hundred kilometers had been calculated in advance, and the shooting parameters had been set, allowing them to lock onto the positions of the reformists easily and launch determined counterattacks.

The positions of the reformists were all established temporarily. Naturally, they did not have a solid, multilayer defense array as Netherwind City did, and they suffered heavy losses in the mutual barrages of heavy cannons. After quite a few positions were destroyed in a row, they did not dare to bombard on a large scale but hurried to move on to somewhere else.

The rugged mountain roads were unsuitable for the heavy crystal tanks to push forward. Therefore, the reformists could only send out the crystal suit legions and push forward inch by inch in head-on collisions.

The coalition army of nobles, which was having their dying struggle, had also deployed the troops on the long, treacherous defense lines. As far as one's eyes could reach, there was an ocean of armors and blades. The modern war of crystal suits was somewhat like a classical war of cold weapons in the ancient times.

However, no matter how sturdy the defense of Netherwind City and the Netherwind Channel was, it was a lone island in a vast ocean after all. Everybody knew that it could not be defended for long.

Therefore, all the nobles were fleeing in a hurry, stomping on each other while they tried to board on starships and jump out. They had even violated the operation regulations of the super teleportation arrays and significantly reduced the gap between two jumps. It was often the case that a second starship had already been launched out when the ripples of the first starship hadn't dispersed yet.

It was a highly dangerous and unprofessional approach.

Space ripples were like reefs on a sailing route that would cause critical damage to ships.

It was especially the case when the anti-fire network and the warships of the two parties had burnt the sky into an ocean of fire, and a lot of space-based magical equipment was bombarding the ground from beyond the atmosphere. The tiny space ripples had already become terrifying "icebergs".

When Li Yao and the rest of them observed the highest peak of the Netherwind Mountain, they happened to witness such an accident.

Only one minute after the first starship ripped apart the void, the second starship had rushed out without bothering anything and was almost torn into helical "noodles" by the ripples. But it narrowly escaped and disappeared into nothingness.

However, the third starship that closely followed behind was not as lucky. It waited too shortly and soared to the sky only half a minute after the second starship was launched. As a result, it hit the fuzzy, twisted void vortex precisely, which was the "mass trap" left by the abrupt disappearance of the second starship.

Water was fluid. When a huge mass of water was lost in an ocean and a giant hollow appeared, the water around would certainly flow in crazily to fill the hole.

In the process, surging currents would be raised, and highly-dangerous swirls might be formed. Whoever sailed near such swirls would probably not end well.

The same applied for space, which was also fluid and continuous.

When two starships disappeared from the stable, fluid, and continuous space in a row, they temporarily disrupted the stability, fluidity, and continuity of the space.

The ripples of the four-dimensional space continued dropping to the three-dimensional space to fill the gap. A lot of "turbulence" and "swirls" would be raised in the process, too.

The third starship, on the other hand, had accelerated to the highest speed and dashed into such "turbulences" and "swirls", hoping to rip a new crack in the unstable space so that they could crawl through in one piece.

How was it possible?

In the eyes of the observers including Li Yao, the starship that had just crawled out of the exit of the Netherwind Channel was longer and longer like a glittering, translucent noodle, gradually turning from a "noodle" into a "thread" while it tried to pass through a needle hole in the midair.

However, right when the "noodle" was stretched to the maximum, it suddenly collapsed because of the entanglement of space ripples!

Every block of the collapsed "noodles" had lost the previous "elasticity". When they were bounced back, they became blocks of the broken starship.

Many of the nobles on the debris had not died yet but were waving their hands and legs crazily in the buffer gel.

Part of the debris and passengers had been absorbed by the shining needle hole in the sky. Nobody was willing to imagine what they would encounter and become after that.

Another part of the debris exploded in the midair, and the raging fire consumed each and every passenger. Fortunately, most of the passengers had already been ripped and killed by the space ripples before that.

The gory destruction was displayed everywhere above the hellish battlefield below the sky of flames.

But nobody was interested in the destruction at all. The escape of the Netherwind Channel was only disrupted by the accident for twenty minutes before the fourth starship soared out against the overwhelming barrage and the space ripples that had not entirely dispersed.

"They are crazy."

His blood frozen, Li Yao mumbled, "Considering cooldown, buffer, maintenance, refill of the fuel, and all the other factors, such super teleportation arrays can be launched every five minutes at best. By the limits of the limits, it may be launched every two minutes. But in order to escape, those nobles are insane enough to launch them once every minute or even every half minute?"

That was not an escape but a suicide!

It was also a sign that the last stronghold of the four Kurfürst families in the capital was about to be lost.

Today was probably the day the city would be conquered. Otherwise, the nobles who cherished their lives more than anything else would never have escaped so hastily.

"The defense line of the coalition army of the nobles is still rather steady without major flaws, but I estimate that something must've happened in the city. They can't persist for another twenty-four hours."

Li Yao said to Long Yangjun and the rest of them, "In order to be safe, we will sneak into Netherwind City in twelve hours and board on a starship to get out of here.

"Xiaoming, Wenwen, have you made it?"

"Yes!"

The kids who were busy working on crystal suits clapped their hands and exclaimed at the same time.

Then, they shouted at each other, unwilling to give in, "I made it first!"

"No, it was me, it was me!"

What the two kids hacked just now were the few standard crystal suits of the Imperium that Li Yao and Long Yangjun stole just now. They had the latest emblem of the reformists engraved on the breastplate, which was the traditional lightning trident with an additional furious fire below it.

"Renew the entire Imperium with iron, blood, and fire!"

It was the most determined roar of the troops of the reformists who believed that they were the rightful army of the Imperium.

Naturally, it was not challenging at all for Li Yao and Long Yangjun to steal a few standard crystal suits from the reformists' troops.

The troops that were attacking the Netherwind Channel together were also complicated and made of a lot of units. The Deep Sea Fleet, the Imperial Guards, the surrendering nobles, and even the special squads made of civilian experts—all kinds of people could be found there.

The army was organized in a haste, and they were not familiar with each other. After more than half a month of fierce battles, many troops had also been separated, which gave Li Yao and the rest of them a precious opportunity.

The only problem was the identification system based on the soldiers' personal information and the dynamic passwords that changed every day.

That problem was resolved after the kids hacked the control center of a certain troop of the reformists and cracked the database of personal information and the dynamic password generation system.

"Come on, let's go and break into the city!"

Li Yao put on a standard crystal suit of the Imperium and spoke to his partners behind him.

Chapter 2434: Psychological Attack

The dull and explosive drumming that was full of self-contradiction echoed again. It was a sign that the reformists were about to launch another wave of attack.

Li Yao and the team snuck in through the intense smoke and blended into the army of the reformists.

After Li Yao's decoration, their crystal suits were now rusty, riddled with holes, and stained with blood and mud, as if they had been fighting on the battlefield for half a month.

The identification system had been compromised by the two kids, who fabricated new identities for them. Unless their crystal suits were taken off, it was absolutely impossible to tell who they were on the messy battlefield.

Li Yao, Long Yangjun, and Li Jialing were all experts in using crystal suits. Naturally, there was not a problem for them to put on the standard crystal suits of the Imperium.

Li Xiaoming and Li Wenwen's situation were trickier, but the two kids boasted the naturally-endowed technique of controlling spiritual puppets. Li Yao had specifically chosen two particularly short and exquisite medical crystal suits for them and modified the crystal suits by stuffing a lot of control units to help the limb movements. Then, the kids were able to keep up with the three of them.

BOOM! BOOM! BOOM! BOOM!

Before the troops attacked, the cannons of the two parties began to have a dialogue again.

To cover the charge of the infantrymen, the reformists had no time to bother with the weak defense of their cannon positions but revealed all their firing points to compete with the heavy cannons of the Kurfürst families.

The warships in the sky were also descending relentlessly against the stormy anti-air firepower, searching for the heavy cannons of the Kurfürst families.

Even though they could not annihilate the enemy, they still needed to disrupt the roars of the enemy's cannons so that the soldiers on their side could march into the enemy's defense line quickly, turning the battle into an inseparable mess. That way, the tragedies where half a legion was wiped out by a few heavy cannons would be impossible.

During the five minutes, at least ten thousand cannonballs left terrifying arcs in the midair.

The cannonballs were in such high density that many of them even collided into each other in the sky, resulting in colorful fireballs. The blasts spread out to all directions like storms.

The soldiers under the storm of cannonballs, despite the protection of their crystal suits, were still shuddering in fear like ants.

In both the armies of the reformists and that of the Kurfürst families, most of the soldiers and bottom-level officers were low-level Immortal Cultivators in the Refinement Stage at best. So much as one or two cannonballs were to explode next to them, it would be the same as the full-strength strike of an expert in the Core Formation Stage or even the Nascent Soul Stage, which was too much for them.

In better cases, their internal organs were shattered, and their faces bled hard.

In worse cases, their crystal suits were torn apart, and they died the most miserable death.

Some of the least fortunate ones, who were at the landing point of the cannonballs, were directly vaporized without leaving the slightest residue.

Li Yao and Long Yangjun had calculated the trajectories and landing points of most cannonballs with their astonishing computational abilities and avoided all the destructive explosions, but they could barely keep themselves steady in the blasts. Their skin was stinging, and they felt bitterness below their tongue.

Xiaoming and Wenwen followed the adults, both confused and excited. Their eyes that were as clear as water and mirrors absorbed every detail on the hellish battlefield.

The self-brutalization of human beings, the marvelous dance of the broken limbs, the mix of blood and guts, the red mist that had been burnt up by the fierce fire, the screams, the moans, the cries, and the devastating roars… Everything they perceived turned into the special curves in their databases, shaping their souls and making them grow more or less like human beings.

Under the cover of the warships and the heavy cannons, the crystal suit legions of the reformists finally marched into the frontal positions of the coalition army of nobles again.

Tu! Tu! Tu! Tu! Tu! Tu!

The previously silent positions immediately turned into a burning forest. The crossfire outlined complicated mazes of death where countless vigorous lives were lost and obliterated. Their bodies that used to be brimming with hot blood fell like weeds blown by wild winds.

The first wave of troops that marched forward was crushed back, but behind them were the supervising team made of the veterans of the Deep Sea Fleet, who shattered the hope of escape of the defeated soldiers with a firepower a hundred times stronger, forcing them to turn around and march at the swirl of death again.

"The firepower of the coalition army of nobles is too fierce!"

Li Jialing couldn't take it anymore. His body was barely wounded, but his nerves were almost breaking down. He was the first to shout, "The commander on the reformists' side is so useless that he doesn't know any way other than marching forward simply? What can it achieve except for reaping the lives of their own soldiers? What commander has Blackstar the Great chosen?"

"He is not useless. This is just a field training."

Narrowing his eyes, Li Yao analyzed the trajectories of the cannonballs in the midair, the firing point configuration of the defense line of the nobles' army, and the movement of the reformists' troops by his side. With extreme calmness beaming out of his eyes, he said, "Haven't you discovered that the troops of the reformists are mainly the regular legions of the Imperial Guards, mixed with tremendous private troops of the local sects and nobles who have surrendered?

"Except for the few elites, the regular legions of the Imperial Guards are just mobs. The private troops of the local sects and nobles perhaps have some combat abilities, but their cohesion and loyalty cannot be guaranteed. It is impossible to train such troops into best warriors through regular approaches in a year.

"The greatest disadvantage of Blackstar the Great is the lack of soldiers. Other than the Deep Sea Fleet and the Astounding Thunder Fleet, he only has the few elite troops of the Imperial Guards who are capable of fighting. They are barely enough to defend the Empyreal Terminus Sector, let alone occupying countless planets in the vast space.

"How can you harvest most elites in the shortest amount of time? Through the bloody battles like what is going on here!

"The qualities of the bodies of the Imperial Guards are not bad. They belong to the royal family with the surname of Wuying after all. No matter how they are short of training resources, their conditions must be a hundred times better than the ordinary people's. If you command a hundred of them to charge forward, not caring whether they will win or fail, as long as ten of them survive in the end, the survivors will be the fervent elites who will be both crazy and stubborn in the next battle.

"I finally understand why Blackstar the Great is slow to remove the enemy's last base when he has controlled the entire capital. He regards this hellish battlefield as the best training ground and is asking the Kurfürsten's troops to whet the Imperial Guards and wake up their brutalities that have been sealed for five hundred years for him!"

Li Jialing was rather shocked. "A hundred soldiers move forward, and only ten return alive… Is that an acceptable price?"

"Why not? You wouldn't think that Blackstar the Great cares about his descendants whose last name is also Wuying, would you?"

Li Yao said, "I assume that in Wuying Qi's eyes, his offspring are no more valuable than a few sperms. He may even be glad that some of the worthless descendants no longer get to waste his precious resources now!

"A well-trained army is better than a large army. The war against the Kurfürst families' army is going to happen soon. He has to get a well-trained army as quickly as possible. Also, I may have exaggerated just now. The sky and the earth are both under Wuying Qi's control right now. So, he can totally control the intensity of the battle. The casualty rate can't be as high as 90%. It's thirty percent to forty percent at most."

Humans were not machines. At least, the humans of the federation and the Imperium were not. Even the most determined troop had to withdraw from the battle with a casualty rate of twenty to thirty percent.

However, with Wuying Qi's brutality and his commanding arts, it was quite possible that he was really training the troops in such cruel ways.

Li Yao and his team, however, found their journey much trickier. They had to maximize their senses and computational abilities to look for the momentary windows in the impassable fire lines of the nobles' army. Most critically of all, they had to conceal themselves well and prevent the two parties at war from noticing their weirdness. The best they could afford was that they were regarded as lucky dogs who survived disasters again and again.

Otherwise, if both parties in the war aimed at them simultaneously, it would be quite awkward.

The reformists launched several charges but failed to break in. They gained nothing after dropping thousands of bodies that were twisted with their crystal suits.

But it did not mean that they did not have more powerful weapons.

Under the cover of the few consecutive charges, weirdly-shaped crystal tanks were finally shipped to the frontline.

The turrets of the crystal tanks looked like silver pans. What they unleashed was not fatal heat or mystic rays, but soundwaves that entailed strong mental interferences.

"Brothers of the illegitimate Kurfürsten's army, stop fighting for your master who is about to be crushed!

"Just look at the highest peak of the Netherwind Mountain behind you. While you are here fighting like caged beasts, your masters whom you pledge your loyalty to and who swear to bring you away with them, those shameless and heartless nobles, have already left with the wealth of the people that they have exploited during the past hundreds of years!

"They would rather load more Heavenly Materials and Earthly Treasures on the starships or even bring a few more pets than take you away with them! Please think about it. Is it really worth it to sell your lives for such masters?

"Look! One starship, another starship, a third starship! Every starship is taking away the high and mighty nobles, who can escape back to their nests and continue their extravagant and unaffected lives. But what about you? Are you going to die for the nobles when you have never enjoyed any benefits that the nobles promised?

"Wake up, brothers of the illegitimate Kurfürsten's army, my dearest countrymen, and fellow Cultivators! We believe that most of you are the real Immortal Cultivators and the most loyal patriots of the Imperium of True Human Beings. You were just deceived by the unpardonable nobles!

"The door of the Reform Committee of the Council of Elders is always open to all the Immortal Cultivators and patriots. There's still time to embrace brightness and justice. Your action will be regarded as 'battlefield uprising'. But if you hesitate, your precious lives will really turn into meaningless dust!"

Chapter 2435: Total Collapse

BOOM! BOOM! BOOM! BOOM! BOOM!

Before the reformists' shouting came to an end, dozens of flying swords had already darted out of the positions of the Kurfürsten's army and shot at the heavy tractors that had been boarded with gigantic broadcast rune arrays.

The blast raised by the flying swords turned into visible helical pillars of air as they rushed close like burning stars.

Thankfully, the tractors all carried sturdy defense systems, which condensed glowing spiritual shields in front of the line in time. The powerful airwaves they unleashed voluntarily minced the flying swords into pieces or deviated them from the course.

In the next second, a hoarse voice echoed from the position of the Kurfürsten's army, too. The man cried like a pig about to be butchered, "Brothers, don't believe the bulls*it of those rebels. As long as we persist for another couple of days, five days at most, the main forces of the four Kurfürsten will certainly arrive in our reinforcement and slay all the worthless rebels!

"Hang in there! Just hang in there! Whoever persists in fighting without retreating for a couple of days—no, for only twenty-four hours, will be rewarded with a hundred kilograms of crystals of the highest quality. Their citizen ranks will also be increased by three levels. Three levels!"

After the shouting of the hoarse voice, the slightly damaged morale of the Kurfürsten's army behind the forest of blades was more or less improved again.

Somebody sneered behind the positions of the reformists. His chuckle spread out across the entire battlefield through the broadcast rune arrays. "What 'Kurfürsten'? They are all traitors who stole the places that they did not deserve. The Reform Committee of the Council of Elders represents the highest authority of the Imperium of True Human Beings. We will never admit any knighthood, military rank, or citizen level granted by the old Council of Elders and the four Kurfürst families. Not just an increment of three levels, even if you are improved by thirty levels, you will lose all your rights and end up as slaves and corpses if you are dogged enough to resist us!"

"Bulls*it! Bulls*it! Bulls*it!"

The hoarse voice burst into fury and roared, "What 'Reform Committee of the Council of Elders'? You are just a bunch of regicidal, hypocritical rats. When the main forces of the four Kurfürsten arrive, all the rebels will be executed with their entire families. We will see who is being dogged and who will turn into slaves and corpses by then!

"Brothers, hold your position. This whole mountain has been armed to the teeth. Behind every rock is a crystal cannon and a firing point on our side. Those rebels are all untrained rookies. They haven't broken in during the past half month, and they will not break in in another half month!

"The reinforcements of the four Kurfürsten are already on the way. The prodigious crystals, the rarely-seen Heavenly Materials and Earthly Treasures, and the powerful magical equipment—the reinforcements are bringing them to you, as long as you can persist for another twenty-four hours!"

The reformists' soundwave tractors were silent for a while, as if they were rendered speechless by what the commander of the Kurfürsten's army said.

But only three minutes later, another voice that was full of mockery and anger echoed again. While it was not as loud as the previous voice, it was well-oriented and very aggressive. "Song Hongchang, stop your bragging. You are still bulls*itting and trying to deceive more people to die for you when things have gone so far?

"Brothers of the Kurfürsten's army, your commander in this battle area is Song Hongchang, the leader of the 'Furious Tide Sect' and a direct descendant of the Song family, right? Let me tell you, you've all been tricked by him! Very badly!

"My name is Zhao Guanglong. I used to be a family warrior of the Song family and the deputy director of the Furious Tide Sector. Many brothers here probably know me. If you don't, ask those around you, and some of them must've heard of my name before. You'll know that Zhao Guanglong is a real person, not someone invented by the reformists!

"I swear that in the past week, I saw with my own eyes that Song Hongchang pushed the disciples of the Furious Tide Sector, as well as the brothers here, to the frontline to be killed, while he secretly sent his family to the starships to escape from this place. Not just his wife and his sons, even his mistress has been sent away by him!

"Did those people leave emptyhandedly? Of course not! They brought all kinds of crystals, magical equipment, and Heavenly Materials and Earthly Treasures. Some of them were earned through scams in the past decades, and some were looted by him during the past month when other people were in trouble. The worth of his wealth was astronomical, and his wife, his children, and his mistress brought all of it away without leaving a single crystal for you!

"He claims that the reinforcements of the Kurfürsten will arrive if you persist for a couple of days? Hahahaha. That's bulls*it! There won't be any reinforcements, at least not in the next month! Right now, the four Kurfürsten are busy nominating a 'new emperor'. They are bickering with each other hard. Who cares about you?

"Song Hongchang clearly had crystals and Heavenly Materials and Earthly Treasures in his pocket, but he refused to award you and lied to you that reinforcements would arrive with tremendous assets in a couple of days? The truth is that he is just deceiving you into persisting for another twenty-four hours because he will find an excuse to slip away in twelve hours at most!

"By then, he will continue his luxury life at the edge of the Imperium with the wealth that he exploited from you. He may continue serving as a noble or a commander under the so-called 'new emperor'. But the poor cannon fodder like you would have been rotten into nothingness by then!

"Brothers, I used to be a family warrior and a trusted subordinate of the Song family, which is why I know all the clandestine things he has done. I could've totally left with Song Hongchang, but it would be too unbrotherly. I would not deserve the brothers here and the real Imperium!

"That's why I abandoned the darkness and joined the reformists, siding with justice and loyalty, to reveal the hideous face of Song Hongchang for you.

"The corrupt scoundrel Song Hongchang is a representative of all the big nobles. Brothers, what are you thinking exactly? Are you really going to die for those insatiable, selfish animals?"

The few words were as powerful as three hundred crystal bombs. The frontline of the Kurfürsten's army was seething, and the morale that had just been condensed collapsed again.

The hoarse voice was panicked and did not reply until after a long daze, "Zhao Guanglong, you traitor, spy, shameless scoundrel! This is shameless slander!

"It was you who intended to escape from this place by slipping on a starship after you stole tremendous military assets if you were not caught by me.

"You kneeled before me and cried hard, claiming that you did a stupid thing in your confusion. Considering that you were a family warrior that had been following me since little, I spared you and hoped that you could correct your mistake by making greater contributions.

"I did not know that you would be so shameless as to join the regicidal rebels during the chaos or to come up with such asinine, flawed lies!

"Brothers, don't be fooled by him. It is absolutely untrue and ungrounded! I swear to live and die with the position. I swear to fight side by side with the brothers here until the end!"

"Is that so? Then, it's simple!"

Zhang Guanglong chuckled on the reformists' side and shrieked, "We'll know if it is true when you let your wife and your sons stand before us! Song Hongchang, your eldest son is 27 years old and an Immortal Cultivator in the Building Foundation Stage. Right now, everybody is fighting fiercely in the frontline, and countless fellow Cultivators have died miserable deaths, but where is your eldest son? Is he having a great time somewhere else?

"Come on, let your eldest son stand out. As long as he appears in the position here and now, I'll cut my head off and give it to you!"

After hearing that, the positions of the Kurfürsten's army fell into dead silence.

The hoarse voice was silent for a long time before the man shouted in fury and helplessness, "Zhang Guanglong, you were a family warrior of the Song family. How disloyal and unfaithful you are!"

"Damn you!"

Hardly had he concluded his sentence when Zhang Guanglong on the reformists' side spat hard and interrupted his former master without any mercy. "I am a valid Immortal Cultivator and a defender of the Imperium of True Human Beings and the civilization of mankind. I am only loyal to the Imperium, to the queen, to the real His Majesty, and to the Reform Committee of the Council of Elders. Who is your family warrior? Are you even qualified to demand the loyalty and faith of an honorable warrior of the Imperium?

"Brothers of the illegitimate Kurfürsten's army, have you seen the ugly faces of the big nobles? However hard you work and fight, and however loyal you are to them, you are just a dog, a servant, or a family warrior at best in their eyes!

"Is that what you want? Is that the fate of your life that you are unwilling to change? I truly feel sorry for you!"

The specialist of mental attacks on the reformists' side who spoke first chimed in, "All the brothers of the illegitimate Kurfürsten's army, now is the last opportunity for your 'battlefield uprising'. Rising up will be different from being captured and even more different from being killed!

"Wake up and resist. See clearly the real brightness and justice and join the great cause to renew the Imperium. Aim your weapons and blades at the nobles and the lords, especially Song Hongchang.

"Don't let Song Hongchang get away!

"Don't let Song Hongchang escape with the wealth that is supposed to be yours!

"Besides Song Hongchang, there are still countless other wretched nobles and lords in the city who have not escaped yet with their tremendous wealth. Come on and march into the city with us and keep the trophies to ourselves!"

The voices that were full of temptation and instigation found their ways into the ears of every soldier and every bottom-level officer in the Kurfürsten's army like all-pervasive venom although the battlefield was mired in deafening noises, raising the most primitive hatred, the most scorching desires, and the darkest jealousy in the hearts of the low-level Immortal Cultivators.

Why could the big nobles escape on the starships openly, while they had to die in the damp trenches and the stinky fortresses like rats and cockroaches?

It remained to be seen whether or not the reformists could achieve what they wanted, but they would probably kill all the enemies on their way in their fury.

Everything would be gone after death!

Just like that, the positions that the Kurfürsten's army had defended for more than half a month finally had thousands of cracks and collapsed beyond control.

Chapter 2436: Loss of Unity!

The defense area where Song Hongchang, the leader of the "Furious Tide Sector", was in charge was only one point on the prolonged defense line.

The reformists had long talked Zhang Guanglong, the deputy leader under his command, into surrender. They knew that the place was the weakest point on the defense line and therefore had deployed tremendous elite troops here.

The few rounds of charges just now were just disguises. At this moment, after Song Hongchang's subordinates, particularly the disciples of the Furious Tide Sect, were swayed by the psychological attacks, and a lot of people were really having "battlefield uprisings" in their anger, the elites of the reformists leaped out, and launched the most determined assault!

The elites in the assault were mostly the pillars of the Deep Sea Fleet and the few veterans in the Imperial Guards, or the unaffiliated Immortal Cultivators who had bloody grudges against the four Kurfürst families. They were certainly incomparable for the rookie troops that attacked just now.

Like a burning wolf fang, they pierced through the defense line immediately.

The Kurfürsten's army that had been penetrated through completely lost the fighting will. Collapses and chaos were spreading out like a chain reaction in a crystal bomb.

Under the leadership of the bottom-level officers, countless regular soldiers in the Kurfürsten's army were really aiming their blades and barrels at the middle- and high-level commanders.

Song Hongchang, the highest commander in the defense area, immediately turned into a rat that everyone stomped on from a magnificent general a moment ago.

"Song Hongchang is caught! Song Hongchang is caught!"

Cheers that might or might not be true echoed on the battlefield. The flags of the four Kurfürst families' old Imperium were shaking under the bombardment, only to be torn into shreds and burnt into ashes a moment later.

A new flag of the reformists' Imperium replaced them and rose on the battlefield. On the flag, the furious flames were licking the sky and the earth!

Li Yao and his companions certainly would not let go of such a great opportunity. Mixed in the overwhelming "avalanche", they marched in and passed through the defense area of the Kurfürsten's army.

The whole defense line of the Kurfürsten's army was almost a hundred kilometers long and dozens of kilometers thick. Song Hongchang was only responsible for one section on it. Even though it was collapsing, the process had not been completed yet.

In many trenches and fortresses, face-to-face shooting or melee combats were still going on, but the battle was apparently one-sided.

The reformists were pushing forward unstoppably, and the warriors were like invincible lunatics. The Kurfürsten's army, on the other hand, had been devastated. Many troops were dispersing or kneeling to surrender the moment they were engaged with the enemy. Some of them even pointed their blades and weapons at their commander the moment they saw the flag of the reformists, having a "battlefield uprising".

Moving forward quickly and observing the battle, Li Yao had a lot of mixed feelings.

The minor city battle in front of him was exactly the epitome of the big picture of the Imperium today.

In terms of comprehensive capability, the number of soldiers, and the average quality of soldiers, the Kurfürsten's army was apparently higher than the reformists. The Imperial Guards, as the main force of the reformists, had never been through any bloody and fierce battles for hundreds of years. They did not have the combat abilities that deserved their equipment.

However, the biggest difference between the two parties was their "belief".

Belief was something that everybody always talked about but might not be able to explain if pursued. It was just an intangible, philosophical concept.

In Li Yao's eyes, the belief was very simple. It was just the ideology that one had full faith in.

Only when a trainer had belief could there be a direction for their strength to advance. In the meantime, an army made of countless trainers could only carry out 100% of their combat ability if they had a solid belief.

The Kurfürsten's army, however strong on paper and however magnificent and splendid it looked, was essentially a traditional, underdeveloped, and corrupt army.

The leaders of the army were selfish, insatiable, and devoid of shame and morals. They could sell their country for their families, or sell their families for themselves. Belief, ideology, and faith were nothing but a skin they used to earn personal interests. Nobody took it seriously.

As for the underclass, they were either deceived by their "skin", bought over with petty benefits and conspired with the upper class, or were coerced into working for the upper class by violence against their own wish.

In such an obsolete army, no matter how strong the experts were, how vast the territory was, and how many starships and soldiers they had, it was just a tray of loose sand where everybody was rejective of each other!

The reformists, on the other hand, were different.

No matter how Li Yao despised Blackstar the Great's character and hated his schemes, Li Yao had to admit that Wuying Qi, Li Linghai, and all the other reformists had their beliefs and were devoted to them.

At least 50% of the reformists were not fighting for their personal interests but for something of a higher level, for the new Imperium that they believed in.

This was a new army with beliefs. When encountering an old army without beliefs like the Kurfürsten's army, it was inevitable that they would crush the enemy like rolling wheels.

No matter what high level one person was at and however strong their combat ability was, there had to be something that they believed in.

The Kurfürsten's army was absolutely no match for the reformists. Perhaps only the federal army that had beliefs, and possibly even more firm ones, was capable of competing with the "new army" under Wuying Qi's command.
