A defeated army was like a falling mountain. At this moment, the whole defense line was like a mountain that was having an avalanche. It was slowly retreating in deafening noises.

Countless soldiers in the Kurfürsten's army had chosen to kneel and surrender or have a "battlefield uprising", but many other people who owed bloody debts or who had tremendous interests in their familial territories chose to flee back to the downtown of Netherwind City, hoping to grip a slim chance of survival and escape with a starship.

The soldiers and the bottom-level officers of the two parties in the fierce battle were all equipped with the standard crystal suits of the Imperium, except that the breastplate of the reformists had an additional emblem. Li Yao and his companions took off the emblem in secret and altered the mode of the identification system. After they were mixed in the messy, crushed soldiers who were all busy running, none of them paid any attention to the strangers at all.

Half an hour later, they entered the downtown of Netherwind City successfully.

This place, however, was another living hell that was probably even more bloody and horrible.

The news of the collapse of the defense line had been delivered. The order in the city that had been maintained by the big nobles through tyranny completely fell out. Everybody ran toward the Netherwind Channel desperately.

Their eyes bloodshot, they cried and waved their hands crazily, like hybrids of zombies and animals, fighting for the last opportunity of survival.

A lot of people who were thin and knew that they could never cram in a starship gave up their hope. They wandered on the street or squatted, looking at the sky and the surroundings in confusion, as if they were too overwhelmed to know why they were here or even who they were.

Li Yao saw countless such persons with his own eyes. Most of them were graceful, gorgeous women in fancy clothes, and some were old men who were obviously in power but had lost most of their strength due to old age.

Xiaoming and Wenwen even recognized a few very famous superstars through facial scanning.

"The few persons standing next to the street like zombies are in fact very awesome culture-type Immortal Cultivators. They are the big stars that every household of the family knows!"

Xiaoming and Wenwen said, "We didn't know that even they wouldn't have access to the starships."

"Culture-type, admin-type, research-type, and creation-type people can only play their roles when society is in order."

Looking at the pale, desperate stars, Li Yao sighed. "When the order collapses and the society is back to the bloody, dog-eat-dog jungle, the simplest violence will be the only strength!"

As he said, some of the big stars or powerful admin-type Immortal Cultivators, through sexual temptations, bribery, or heartfelt begging, pleaded with the battle-type Immortal Cultivators to lead them forward to the Netherwind Channel.

But the Immortal Cultivators simply kicked them away without any mercy. No matter how the faces were in the past and how the hands used to sign the great contracts in the past, they were absolutely useless right now.

Some of the weaker battle-type Immortal Cultivators, knowing that they certainly would not have a chance to board on a starship to evacuate from the place, simply made up their minds and joined each other into a mob, raiding and burning the city down while they roared the slogans of the reformists.

Now that they had made up their minds to not leave, the superstars and the big bosses who carried tremendous wealth with them naturally became their best prey. Before the troops of the reformists really entered Netherwind City, the whole city was already on fire and enshrouded in smoke. Screams, laughter, and the sounds of flesh being torn apart were echoing nonstop.

"I finally understand why Wuying Qi has spared Netherwind City for such a long time."

Li Yao and Long Yangjun slew a team of mobsters who were bold enough to rob them. He said, "It is partly to train the troops, and partly because he hoped that Netherwind City would fall into a mess on its own.

"The spontaneous chaos in Netherwind City foreshadows what will happen in the territories of the four Kurfürst families. When one big noble retreats from the place easily, ninety-nine middle and low-level Immortal Cultivators will be left behind. Those people who have been 'abandoned', stirred by intense jealousy and hatred, will join the arms of the reformists without any hesitation and become the most hardcore reformists.

"Furthermore, when everything that happens here is broadcast to the entire universe, especially the territories of the four Kurfürst families, by Wuying Qi and Li Linghai in various ways, what will the middle- and low-level Immortal Cultivators in the territory think?

"A team can barely be called a team without unity. Wuying Qi surrounded Netherwind City for a whole month without attack just in order to strike the 'unity' of the four Kurfürst families!"

"But right now, the city has been conquered."

Long Yangjun turned around and looked at the defense line at the edge of the city that had been rippled with holes. "We have to speed up. Otherwise, if a lot of nobles are gathered in the Netherwind Channel, only to discover that the starships are far from enough, they may destroy the starships if they do not have any hope of leaving!"

Chapter 2437: We'll Go First!

As a matter of fact, it was what was going on right now.

After killing, burning, and looting for a while, a lot of the mobsters congregated into destructive torrents and flooded at the Netherwind Channel.

Perhaps it was because the big nobles were all gathered in the Netherwind Channel, and they intended to use the heads of the nobles as the stairs for them to rise on the reformists' side.

Or maybe, they simply did not want the big nobles to escape but want them to end as miserably as themselves.

The order in the city was entirely gone. Li Yao and his companions struggled to move forward in blood, screams, flames, and explosions. They countered countless mobsters who were on a killing spree. Their eyes were all bloodshot; their faces twisted so hard that they were foaming as they killed and sabotaged everything that they saw.

Those people were no different from animals.

No, even the most brutal animals did not have the evilness that surrounded them.

"The 'ecological equilibrium' of the billions of devils deep inside their souls has been completely broken."

Long Yangjun said, "In other words, those people have been devilized. In the meantime, the violent brainwaves that are being released by their souls that have completely gone out of control are likely to raise quakes and resonations, resulting in the loss of control of even more devils in people's heads. This is an unexpected but very typical 'arrival of devils'.

"Now you should understand that I did not lie to you, right? How can there be so many devils that come from the extraterrestrial lands? Most of the devils live inside the heads and hearts of human beings in the first place."

"Mankind, our father, is truly incomprehensible."

With the slaughters and brutality reflected in their eyes, Li Xiaoming and Li Wenwen sighed in confusion. "If the purpose is to preserve the whole as much as possible, shouldn't some people sacrifice themselves in order for the rest of the people to evacuate in order?

"If the city falls into chaos before the enemy comes, and everybody has a war against everybody else around them, how many of them will be able to escape in the end?

"It's just like all the threads under our control are built for the general purpose. As long as it is beneficial for the completion of the task, any thread can be sacrificed without any hesitation or resistance. They will never act on their own."

"Perhaps that is the feature of mankind or any life."

Lost for words, Li Yao thought for a long time and could only say, "If you accept that a brilliant, ever-evolving civilization can only be born if everybody has independent self-awareness, you must also accept that some of the people are selfish and not to be communicated with. It is the inseparable bright and dark sides of the civilization of mankind.

"If everybody is turned into something like a machine or a thread, they will certainly sacrifice themselves without any hesitation or even any complaint under such circumstances… But if you think about it more carefully, that may be an even more horrible picture!"

"You do have a point, Dad."

The kids thought carefully for a while. "Keeping the variation of the individuals so that they can view and modify the material world in hundreds of different modes of thinking is perhaps beneficial for the whole civilization to survive in the cruel universe, but keeping the independence of the individuals will inevitably lead to what is happening right now. It is a price that has to be paid. We… We need to consider carefully before we find the solution to the dilemma."

The consideration was for the future. Right now, they had to seize every second to arrive at the Netherwind Channel.

After taking care of quite a few teams of ferocious mobsters, everybody was stained with blood or even fresh human tissues on their crystal suits, which became the best warning. Few other mobsters were bold enough to take advantage of the team that seemed rather small in number and kept a distance from them while they marched to the entrance of the Netherwind Channel.

When they passed by the largest hospital in town, Li Yao slightly slowed down.

The hospital had been fully inhabited—no, crammed with heavily wounded soldiers who were covered in bandages.

Most of the wounded soldiers couldn't have basic treatment. Not only were the medical cabins filled with nutrition drugs few, but even their bandages hadn't been replaced for days even though the bandages were dirty and filled with blood.

Had the soldiers not been strong, healthy fighters or low-level Immortal Cultivators, who boasted a high vitality, they would've kicked the bucket a long time ago.

But however vigorous they used to be, they were dying right now and did not seem to have much time left.

They had all suffered heavy wounds on the city defense line a few days back when they covered the nobles to retreat.

The moment they were no longer of any value, they were immediately abandoned by the nobles and piled up here like decaying, salty fish.

Even though Li Yao was a Cultivator, he couldn't help but feel sympathetic toward the poor and pathetic Immortal Cultivators.

The only comfort was that Li Yao knew the army of the reformists would soon break into the downtown area and rescue the dying soldiers.

It was not exactly because Li Yao had high hopes for the moral standard of the reformists, but because the wounded soldiers were the best materials for propaganda. For nothing other than to reveal the ungratefulness and heartlessness of the big nobles, the reformists would take good care of the wounded soldiers and use them as the most powerful weapon in the psychological war, exactly like how they used Zhang Guanglong, the deputy leader of the Furious Tide Sect.

Soon after they left the hospital, the road ahead of them was completely jam packed with people. It was impossible to pass.

Further ahead, based on the highly-rising mountain was a splendid alloy gate that was dozens of meters in both length and width with a weight of almost ten thousand tons. Behind the gate, it was the body of the mountain that had been hollowed out and the main part of the Netherwind Channel.

It was a gate of survival and hope.

But right now, it had become a gate of death and destruction.

A hundred burning, exploding crystal tanks were heaped before the gate, blocking the road to the gate completely. A team of Exos with extremely fierce firepower was hiding on the fortresses on the two sides of the gate, raining flying swords, cannonballs, and scorching metal particles upon their partners a moment ago crazily.

The terrain here was extremely tricky. On the two sides of the road were high, steep precipices. No matter how many people had come from the downtown area, they had to cram forward through the narrow road in the middle. Therefore, all that it took was a few elites and enough firepower to claim countless lives. The place was barely conquerable.

When the mobsters marched forward, they were immediately blown back by the stormy forward. After more than ten futile charges, their morale hit the rock bottom.

As mobsters or even "devilized people" who had lost control of their souls in the first place, they were not determined to conquer the place. Instead of charging at the Netherwind Channel, they might as well go back to the downtown area to burn and kill without restraint!

But right at the moment, the ten-thousand-ton gate behind the burning tanks and the fixed firing points were slowly closed under the pull of the giant gears and hinges.

It seemed that the escape on the Netherwind Channel had reached the last moment, and few starships were available inside.

Should the gate be closed, it would be impossible for the people outside to squeeze in.

As a result, the soldiers and the bottom-level officers who lingered in the firing points to buy more time for the big nobles were immediately dumbfounded.

They had just killed thousands of their partners in the old days. The freshest blood was on their hands. Even if other people could all surrender to the reformists, how could they be accepted?

No, before they met the reformists, they would've been torn into shreds by the crazy beasts outside!

Realizing that, the soldiers and the bottom-level officers in all the fortresses and crystal tanks howled in fury and desperation, abandoning their positions and running toward the gap of the gate that hadn't been entirely closed.

BOOM! BOOM! BOOM! BOOM!

Some people even turned around the crystal cannons and fired at the gears and hinges at the edge of the gate, trying to destroy the locking system of the gate.

They all turned into crazy mobsters from accomplices of the nobles in half a second.

The mobsters who were about to retreat in their low morale were greatly encouraged, too. They shouted and passed the burning tanks, charging at the undefended gate.

Li Yao and his companions were naturally at the vanguard. Boosting the power rune arrays, they turned into five streaks of brightness and crawled in at the moment before the gate was closed.

Shua! Shua! Shua! Shua!

By the time everybody landed, almost twenty sections of broken arms had landed next to them, which all belonged to the guards who attempted to stop them from breaking in.

Like the gods of killing, Li Yao and Long Yangjun pushed forward in a storm of blades. Not any guard could resist their one attack. Even the crystal tanks were easily chopped and torn apart before they exploded in spluttering electric arcs.

After Li Yao gripped a crystal bomb that flew at him with only one hand and threw it back to where it came from, nobody had the courage to stand in the way of the mysterious yet strong squad.

Li Yao's team pressed forward and reached the departure point of the Netherwind Channel without meeting any obstacle. It was a secret dock deep inside the mountain.

Tens of thousands of nobles and their squires were waiting for their starships to refill the fuel and prepare for the jump anxiously inside the dock. The news that the gate had been lost made them even more nervous. They hurried to ask their subordinates to work faster.

The Netherwind Channel was designed to accommodate one starship and accelerate it to a high speed to break into the four-dimensional space for a jump at a time. Also, the gap between two consecutive jumps was at least two minutes.

Almost eight starships were still waiting, which meant that it would take at least twenty minutes for everybody to evacuate.

But it was a big problem whether or not the mobsters who had broken into the gate would give them another twenty minutes.

"Let me go first!"

"I'll go first. Just give me your terms!"

"Bulls*it! If you don't let me go first, nobody will be leaving!"

Dozens of nobles who seemed to be CEOs, leaders, and captains, without their usual manners, opened their arms like crabs and argued with each other vehemently.

Suddenly, dozens of flying swords, in fatal brilliance, were focused on the most awe-inspiring noble of all of them, blowing up his spiritual shield as well the protective magical equipment. Then, his half body and his head were completely ripped apart, burnt up, and obliterated!

Before anyone realized what was going on, the cocky noble a moment ago had only half of his body that had been blackened left, which fell to the ground into powder!

"There's no need to fight."

Li Yao strode to the nobles and released the daunting vibe of the Divinity Transformation Stage as he casually said, "Get out of the way. We'll go first!"

Chapter 2438: The Birth of a New Imperium!

The overwhelming vibe of the Divinity Transformation Stage was like a collapsing mountain, whose billions of invisible rocks were falling on everyone's head.

Besides, Li Yao was not the only person in the Divinity Transformation Stage. Long Yangjun was also releasing the coldest killing intent behind him, locking onto the heart of every noble.

Li Jialing also grimaced and unleashed the fighting will of a lion.

While those nobles belonged to the four Kurfürst families, the members of the Kurfürst families had different ranks as well. The people who had lingered to the last moment naturally couldn't have been the central persons of their families. Their capabilities were quite limited. The few best ones of them were only in the Nascent Soul Stage.

Under the covetous eyes of Li Yao and Long Yangjun, two ferocious animals in the Divinity Transformation Stage, those nobles absolutely couldn't resist.

"Senior!"

"Elder!"

"Master!"

Seeing that the noble who was most aggressive, intimidating, and actually strongest had been blown up by Li Yao and Long Yangjun in half a second, the remaining nobles all turned pale and put on an obsequious smile between their wrinkles and their cold sweat.

Although they were cursing aloud in their hearts about how two a*sholes in the Divinity Transformation Stage had jumped out of nowhere, they couldn't have appeared more humble. They looked so enthusiastic and welcoming that they seemed willing to kick their parents off the starship and spare the seats for Li Yao and his team if their parents were on board.

Li Yao glanced at the starship on the dock that was about to enter the rail quickly and pointed at the third starship to the left. "Whose starship is that?"

It was a "Blue Bird"-level light, half-armed merchant ship. Although the loading capability was not high, and the turrets and the spiritual shield it boasted were limited, it had remarkable power units and could be quite agile in turning and dodging, which made it most suitable to evade the enemy's bombardment or pass in the complicated meteoroid belt. It was a rather classic "smuggler's starship".

Many people were fond of wandering in the gray area doing the clandestine businesses with the starships of such a model.

Li Yao did not have a high requirement for speed and defense ability. After all, should he encounter any danger, he and Long Yangjun could always march out with their Colossi. The key was that the "Blue Bird"-level starships were easier to control and highly automatic. Under the cruise state, more than 95% of the operations could be completed by the mainframe crystal processor.

It was a very classic starship that many people were familiar with. There were no secrets about its control system. Xiaoming and Wenwen happened to know the ways to maneuver the starships of such a model.

In other words, even without the cooperation of the crew, they were still confident to get the starship to the edge of the Imperium.

"It's… It's mine."

A fatty with wrinkles all over his greasy face stood out, shuddering. "If I may ask…"

"This starship will go first!"

Li Yao did not bother to chitchat with him. With his eyes glowing brighter than the sun, he said to the other nobles, "It will take twenty minutes at most for all the starships to evacuate. Everybody will be able to leave if you stay patient and obedient! Otherwise, we will blow up all the other starships first and kill each and every one of you lot, and there will still be time for us to leave!"

His words made all the nobles pale and flabbergasted. They hurried to shake their heads, announcing that they daren't violate the order of the almighty experts.

Li Yao sniffed and looked at Long Yangjun. The two of them took action together. Thousands of rays of light emanated from their crystal suits, blowing up the cave from the outside of the mountain to the cave. The entire pathway was blocked after the collapse.

Although the rocks were rather loose, it would still take the rebels outside at least half an hour to dig through the blockages.

"Let's go!"

Li Yao spoke to the overjoyed fatty.

The "Blue Bird"-level starship had long refilled the fuel and been prepared for a space jump, but the fatty was by himself and couldn't bid for the right to go first. Now that he had the support of Li Yao and Long Yangjun, the final test was done in only half a minute.

The starship entered the rail and moved upward helically, faster and faster, until it ripped apart the void and disappeared in the vortex in earsplitting shrieks!

Li Yao's team had escaped from the capital that was illuminated by Blackstar. The universe was open for them!

At the same time, the "Blue Bird"-level starship appeared abruptly in the vast space at the periphery of the Empyreal Terminus Sector, with mist-like space ripples around it popping up like hot steams.

The jump of the first phase had been completed successfully.

Because the coordinates were set randomly, they had no idea where exactly they were at in the vast space at the edge of the Empyreal Terminus Sector. They only knew that it was already far away from the capital planet and that the fleets of the reformists were too busy surrounding the capital planet and reclaiming the resourceful, steady space zones around the capital to be deployed so far away.

They were safe for now.

"My two… seniors."

The real owner of the starship, the greasy fatty, struggled to get out of the buffer gel and looked at Li Yao and Long Yangjun in fear before he said half in reverence and half in impractical illusion, "May I know the names of the seniors, and where you are going to? If you don't think that the Song family is too weak for you…"

As it turned out, the fatty was from the Song family too. It seemed that he was the same as Song Hongchang, the unfortunate leader of the Furious Tide Sect. Neither of them was the important person in their family.

However, his status would certainly be different if he could bring two experts in the Divinity Transformation Stage back to his family.

Li Yao glanced at him casually and focused his attention on the intelligence that he just collected as he discussed with Long Yangjun and Li Jialing about his plan.

After all, during the first space jump just now, when the computation load of the mainframe crystal processor of the starship was heaviest, the two kids had hacked it, controlled the password of the maneuver system, and slightly modified it.

Right now, without Li Yao's consent, it would be impossible for the captain or the crew to perform a second space jump.

They had stayed below the ground for too long, and they were in dire need of the latest intelligence on the ground to analyze the situation, especially the movements of the four Kurfürst families.

Just now, when they passed Netherwind City, they stole a lot of crystal processors by the way. The portable crystal processors of the nobles in the dock had been "impounded" by them ruthlessly too.

Li Yao and his companions analyzed and discussed for more than half an hour, and the Song-surnamed fatty waited for exactly the same time nearby pitifully, not having the courage to interrupt the two experts with any noise.

In the end, Li Yao finally showed a pair of coordinates to him. "We're going to this place."

"This is… not the territory of the four Kurfürst families!"

The Song-surnamed fatty was greatly surprised that the coordinates Li Yao gave him was at a peripherical world of the Imperium, which was among the most lawless and chaotic places in the universe. The "Business Alliance of Ten Thousand Sectors", the de facto greatest force in the place, was even the hardcore ally and the biggest sponsor for the reformists. He was less and less certain about who Li Yao and Long Yangjun were.

However, before he gave orders to the captain and the crew, the starship began to hum and shiver on its own, a sign that the space jump units were functioning at the maximal power!

"This is—"

The Song-surnamed fatty's pupils constricted to the size of needle tips, and his pants were soaked in a cold sweat. He had no idea why the starship was operating "automatically" as if there were ghosts on board.

"Just sit down."

Li Yao smiled and said to the Song-surnamed fatty, "You'll know in the future how lucky you were not to get killed today."

…

When the "Blue Bird"-level starship disappeared from the Empyreal Terminus Sector with the two—three—or maybe five most dangerous persons at the center of the cosmos, Netherwind City, the last stronghold of the Kurfürst families on the capital planet, was completely occupied by the reformists too.

The reformists hadn't planned to march so fast. They hoped that the Kurfürsten's army in the city could brutalize each other for a while longer.

But at the last moment, they seemed to be urged by certain influential persons and attacked furiously without bothering the cost unlike before. Before midnight, the flag of lightning trident purged by flames was raised at the highest peak of the Netherwind Mountain.

In the debris of the Netherwind Channel, Li Linghai, who was wearing casual clothes and keeping a low profile, stared at the damage caused by Li Yao and Long Yangjun a moment ago with her eyebrows furrowed.

Her nose was jerking, as if she could smell unusual air from the feeble ripples spreading in the air.

A few strands of black hair appeared on her snowy head.

"They've escaped."

Li Linghai spoke to a voice in her head. "It was half a day ago. If we had attacked half a day sooner, we would've had a chance to catch them."

"There's no need to be bothered. It was impossible."

Blackstar the Great replied casually in her head, "The two highly-dangerous monsters couldn't have been captured so easily. Even if we had occupied Netherwind City a day soon, they would've found another way to escape beyond the atmosphere and steal one of our starships for a space jump."

"Then—"

Li Linghai seemed very concerned. "What about your body, Your Majesty?"

"I can still keep my current form for a long time. That boy belongs to me, and he will come back obediently someday."

Wuying Qi said, "The escape of the few queer rats does not affect the situation. Except for the minor setback, our progress in other aspects has been quite good. Unexpectedly good, I would say.

"Right now, let's focus on the bottom of the capital and the peripheral worlds of the Imperium. As long as we can dig out 'that thing' from the bottom in time and win the support of the newly-rising warlords and Sector Masters at the edge of the Imperium, we will be able to surround the territories of the four Kurfürsten and force them to come to the capital for a strategic battle!

"By then, there will be nothing that can possibly stop the birth of a brand-new Imperium, an eternal Imperium, and an Imperium that rules over the entire sea of stars!"

Chapter 2439: Bribe Boss Bai

At the periphery of the fourth quadrant of the Imperium of True Human Beings…

The universe was boundless and naturally did not have directions or the so-called "quadrants".

However, if the territory of the Imperium was to be observed from the equator of the capital planet of the Imperium, it would look like a rather peculiar group of stars, which was basically in the shape of an oval.

If the north pole and beyond of the capital planet was regarded as "up", the omelet-shaped universe would be basically round. Then, the classification of "quadrants" would be meaningful.

The fourth quadrant had the most Sectors in the entire Imperium. They were almost twice as many as the other three quadrants.

However, the newborn Sectors were rather weak and barren, not enough to give birth to enormous forces that spanned Sectors. They were just separated, independent, and broken forces who minded their own business.

It was often the case that every Sector was ruled by a tyrant, and the local lords fought each other all the time. Blood was shed incessantly, and the fire of destruction never died out in the vacuum.

Since they were all members of the Imperium of True Human Beings, the battles and merges of the local lords were naturally not conducted by the regular army and were mostly carried out by privateers, or to be more direct, space pirates.

That was a battle form that was smaller and less intense but also much more cruel, shameless, and sleazy than regular battles. In the battles of space pirates, soldiers were thieves, and thieves were soldiers. Victims were bandits, and bandits could turn into new victims at any moment. It was the game rule that everybody knew.

This place was the hometown of "God of War" Lei Chenghu. In his younger days, the most prestigious serviceman of the Imperium today had organized hunting against the space pirates many times, with remarkable accomplishments. For a while, the space pirates at the edge of the fourth quadrant were almost entirely gone.

However, he could annihilate space pirates, but he could not get rid of the soil from which space pirates were born. It was the boundless universe as well as the ambitions and desires of human beings.

Therefore, after he led his main force to the frontline of the Imperium and the Covenant Alliance's confrontation, the space pirates at the edge of the fourth quadrant who had vanished were revived and got even worse, transforming the place into the most famous land of chaos and destruction in the entire Imperium again.

Speaking of which, the recent decades, and especially the recent years, were indeed the golden time for the space pirates.

At first, the Imperium mobilized tremendous troops to the frontline for the expedition against the Covenant Alliance. As a result, the rear was weakly defended and did not have enough soldiers to resist the space pirates.

The warlords and Sector Masters in the worlds who were unwilling to send their troops to the frontline to be killed often preferred to claim that their fleets had been destroyed by accident in space. But in fact, those fleets were only turned from regular troops into privateers, which would harass and ambush other Sectors when they were not defended.

While the Imperium's expedition army marched forward, one world after another was recovered, and inestimable assets had been shipped back too. Those assets significantly enlivened the business activities at the peripheral worlds of the Imperium, but they were also the fattest prey in the eyes of the space pirates.

By the time the reformists rose quickly and the "Business Alliance of Ten Thousand Sectors" intended to challenge the old business order, they secretly supported a lot of massive pirate gangs to resist the four Kurfürst families and other nobles with a great amount of money due to their lack of combat abilities.

The four Kurfürst families and other big nobles were naturally unwilling to give in. They either annihilated or appeased the space pirates, recruiting a lot of them for their own purpose and starting a cruel war of "space pirates against space pirates".

After a few years, quite a few space groups had expanded exponentially and were even capable of dealing with the elite deep-sky fleets. Their mysterious, stealthy, and unpredictable style gave their hostile forces quite a headache.

Zuo Tianying, for one, was having a strong headache right now.

He was a space pirate in the early days himself, and his pirate gang, "Mirage Eagle", had been quite famous too. It was perfectly normal. Few of the important persons at the peripheral worlds of the Imperium were not space pirates when they were young. Similar humiliating stains could be found in the pedigrees of a lot of warlords, leaders, and even Sector Masters too.

Just like all the powerful, influential persons, after Zuo Tianying became a deacon of the Business Alliance of Ten Thousand Sectors, it had been a long time since he had done such simple, crude, and unprofessional things like killing and fighting.

Not only did he refuse to do it anymore, but he also forbade his subordinates from talking about it. He sometimes even couldn't hold back his despise and disgust when he looked at other space pirates.

Zuo Tianying liked to call himself a "legitimate businessman" right now.

While everybody knew that businessmen and bandits couldn't be separated at the vast and dangerous universe at the edge of the Imperium, the Business Alliance of Ten Thousand Sectors was a group of merchants with great ambitions and visions after all. They barely looted other people or forced other people to make deals with them. Instead, they tried to establish a brand-new business order and be the regulator and arbitrator of the order.

So, Zuo Tianying's vaunt was not entirely wrong.

Although he loathed his past and other space pirates deep down in his heart, Zuo Tianying's job required him to deal with the people he disliked all the time.

He was the manager of the "Blue Sky Market" under the Business Alliance of Ten Thousand Sectors.

The Blue Sky Market was a free fair established inside a very inconspicuous stone belt centered at a desolate resource planet far away from the habitable planets, surrounded by the debris of countless starships and space fortresses.

People whose goods were still bloodstained and must be kept a secret, people who were unwilling to pay the special taxes to the authorities of the Imperium, and people who were wanted by the major worlds of the Imperium and chased by the bounty hunters, were the most frequent visitors of the free fairs like "Blue Sky Market".

Here, taxes were not required, nobody would check the source of the goods or the identity of the seller, and even the most dangerous prohibited items were purchasable. Even if you had stolen the dear mothers of the four Kurfürsten, somebody would be bold enough to buy them from you as long as the price was acceptable.

The bandits and criminals that everybody cursed and pursued in other worlds could still buy the most mysterious Heavenly Materials and Earthly Treasures, the most effective strengthening drugs, the magical equipment that guaranteed the best performance but did not guarantee quality, and of course, the most charming whores and the most fatal assassins. The so-called "free fair" was exactly such a murky, lawless, and yet absolutely free place.

The Business Alliance of Ten Thousand Sectors had established dozens of free fairs at the edge of the Imperium. Through the free fairs, the capitals, goods, intelligence, and talents from different worlds were gathered. The unattractive free fairs actually yielded astronomical revenues every day. They were also the foundation of the Business Alliance of Ten Thousand Sectors.

Among them, the scale and the flowing capitals in the "Blue Sky Market" definitely ranked the top five.

Having created an enterprise from scratch and growing from a Mr. Nobody to the manager of the Blue Sky Market, Zuo Tianying certainly had extraordinary skills. He was among the most distinguished deacons in the Business Alliance of Ten Thousand Sectors. Even Jin Yuyan, the General Deacon, had high hopes for him.

Therefore, although he barely had any fondness of and was even physically disgusted by the guy sitting on his opposite side, who was wearing a short T-shirt, sucking iced juice, and even placing his hairy legs on the desk arrogantly, he still controlled his expression tightly without letting any redundant emotion leak out.

Just as what the intelligence says, a cocky guy who doesn't know who he is at all.

Zuo Tianying quickly browsed through the files of this "Boss Bai", the head of space pirates, and sneered in his heart, "However, the more arrogant someone is, the easier it will be to trick them. Just let him feel good about himself for a while longer. It won't hurt anything. Chances are that I can even get more favorable terms if I offer him a few adulations!"

Deep down in his heart, Zuo Tianying did not take this "Boss Bai" seriously.

Yes, Boss Bai was a new-generation star who only rose in the circle of space pirates in recent years, and his accomplishments in the past were almost "marvelous". In only a few years, he had swallowed quite a few space gangs that were famous nationwide in the Imperium. He even had the splendid feats where he fought a top-tier fleet of the Imperium in a head-on clash and returned in one piece, and where he attacked seven resource planets controlled by the Imperium in only half a month. One might say that he was a once-in-a-hundred-year "genius" among space pirates.

But he was a young man who had just distinguished himself after all. The name "Boss Bai" was never heard of before. Judging from his brash and disobedient behavior today, how awesome could he have possibly been?

Zuo Tianying estimated that this Boss Bai was probably from a noble family or perhaps a family of generals. He had remarkable combat and commanding abilities, and he was desperate to make a name for himself and maybe to seek avenge. That was why he had been so impatient and glittering in the past two years without realizing the significance of a low profile at all. He was rather lucky to have met the war between the Imperium and the Covenant Alliance at first and the conflicts between the reformists and the Kurfürst families later. Everybody was too occupied to deal with him. Otherwise, a man who did not know what he was doing like him would've been beleaguered and squashed by the army!

Such kind of people, however strong they were, would only end up as the knives of other people.

The mission that Zuo Tianying accepted from the headquarters of the Business Alliance of Ten Thousand Sectors was exactly to recruit Boss Bai and other rogues in the fourth quadrant no matter the cost and to turn them into the knives in the hands of the Business Alliance of Ten Thousand Sectors and the reformists.

It was rather easy for him to get in touch with Boss Bai.

Since the Blue Sky Market was the largest and safest free fair in the few Sectors around, Boss Bai's "Big Space Pirate Gang" visited the place for the disposal of stolen goods as well as purchases and maintenance frequently too. While Zuo Tianying had never met Boss Bai in person before, they at least knew each other's name.

After he let the intermediary pass over the intention of the Business Alliance of Ten Thousand Sectors subtly, Boss Bai was already drooling and sending himself to him like a dog who had smelled meat.

Observing the man pretending to be arrogant yet unable to cover the greed and expectation in his eyes, Zuo Tianying couldn't help but chuckle to himself, knowing that the deal was probably a sure thing now.

Chapter 2440: Heroes Are Also Valued!

"Boss Bai, heroes are always valued. I know that you are the most distinguished space pirate who only rose to fame miraculously recently! Ambushing the Red Purple Planet, resisting the Flowing Wind Fleet, destroying the Hundred Consecutive Stars, merging the Ghost Eye Pirate Gang… Your classic cases can almost be written into a textbook of space pirates for other people to read and learn. Hahahaha!"

Zuo Tianying feigned a smile while he threw out the costless adulations. "The Blue Sky Market is always the most reliable base for all the space pirates. Many of them have been praising you when they maintain their starships or sell goods in my place, claiming that you are very likely to be a great hero who unites all the space pirates in the entire Imperium for the first time in the past thousand years as the future 'King of Pirates'! I always feel that my heart is pounding when they tell me about your magnificent stories, and it has been my wish to make friends with such a heroic person!"

"Hehehehe, the future king of pirates…"

Boss Bai finally put down the juice cup that he had been drinking from. Veins were protruding on his ferret face like blooming man-eating flowers. He grabbed a handful of luscious grapes from the desk and stuffed them into his mouth, with the juice splashing everywhere. Then, he hissed with a voice that seemed to have been rusted, "Are you suggesting that Boss Bai is not the king of pirates right now?"

"Of course you are. You are the well-deserved king of pirates right now!"

Zuo Tianying was almost laughing out loud. This Boss Bai was more arrogant and ignorant than he had thought. He truly wondered where the bumpkin came from exactly.

The territory of the Imperium was too vast, and there were too many unparalleled heroes and bandits. Even though Boss Bai had been invincible in the past few years and gathered a fairly-large team, he was not deeply-rooted at all. There were still a couple of pirate gangs of the same size to his, if not larger. Right now, three of them had been summoned to the Blue Sky Market.

If you wanted to be the king of pirates, you'd better ask the bosses of those pirate gangs and see if they agreed!

Well, the bosses of those pirate gangs would probably agree because space pirates were never interested in the useless titles. They all understood the logic that the tall trees caught wind first and that the more famous they were, the sooner they would die. Only the idiotic boys like Boss Bai who had just started the business would be passionate to be "king of pirates"!

Other people would never hesitate to offer the hat to him and push him to the frontstage to get killed!

While sneering and mocking secretly in his heart, Zuo Tianying spoke with such sincerity as if he were truly considering for Boss Bai. "However, Boss Bai, we are all poor folks who struggle to make a living in the treacherous sea of stars. I used to be a space pirate, and I understand the sufferings of space pirates too well. This business is a dead end. Even if you become the king of pirates, so what? When the army of the Imperium comes at you on a large scale, wouldn't you still be fleeing in a panic like mice that have encountered a cat?

"Those who steal a pendant will be killed, and those who steal a country will be knighted. Why don't you consider the longer term and… seize the entire Imperium together with us?"

"Seize the entire Imperium?"

Boss Bai bit the grapes while making loud noises quickly. Very soon, all that was left in his hands was a bare stem. He narrowed his eyes, with coldness beaming out, while he observed Zuo Tianying up and down for a long time. Then he said in a half smile, "Go on. How is the Imperium going to be seized?"

Zuo Tianying felt more or less creeped out being stared at by him. Spitting in his heart, he calmed himself down and unfolded a 3D map of the Imperium of True Human Beings. He said while pointing at the map, "Boss Bai, since you've been dealing with the Imperium's army for a long time, you must know the situation of the Imperium very well, which is like a turbulent tornado with drastic changes on a daily basis. The Imperium is entirely different from half a year or even three months ago!

"In the newly-recovered area, the expedition army of the Imperium are seething in impatience. The young, bottom-level officers are very dissatisfied with the conservativeness and hesitation of the four Kurfürst families. They are so disappointed that they are even getting out of control. Ever since the news that the Astounding Thunder Fleet has changed their banner, many young officers in the expedition fleets have been thinking to follow the example of 'God of War' Lei Chenghu, transforming themselves into a new, glorious army that defends the interests of the Imperium instead of a certain family!

"In the capital, the reformists have completely got rid of the last bit of strength of the old nobles. The corrupt, murky Council of Elders of the past has been demolished and replaced by the 'Reform Committee of the Council of Elders'. The queen has been elected as the First Member of the committee and the acting highest ruler of the Imperium.

"The Reform Committee of the Council of Elders has been ruling and operating well in the entire Empyreal Terminus Sector, which means that a brand-new government has blossomed and fruited at the heart of the Imperium!

"Outside of the capital, in the territory of the four Kurfürst families, the reign of the old nobles is hanging by a thread. Student movements and strikes are breaking out nonstop. Too many people have been suppressed pitifully! But despite the suppression, the fury of the people has been completely ignited and is flooding like magma. There is no stopping it at all!

"At the peripheral worlds of the Imperium where we are living, the Business Alliance of Ten Thousand Sectors is also actively contacting the great heroes like Boss Bai, planning to rise up and cooperate with the expedition army in the frontline, the Reform Committee of the Council of Elders in the capital, and the people and the low-level Immortal Cultivators who have had enough of everything in the territory of the old nobles!

"As long as the four parties work together, we will certainly sweep across the feeble fortresses of the old nobles destructively and renew the entire Imperium with the wrath of the people!

"Boss Bai, the trend of reform is unstoppable. Those who go with it, thrive; those who go against it, die! Join our greatest cause with your fleet as well as the seven affiliating pirate gangs who receive your protection and command. It's now or never!"

His face bushing, Zuo Tianying persuaded the man passionately.

Boss Bai, however, simply looked at the map casually and sucked the last juice on the stem before he said, "It means declaring war on the four Kurfürst families, doesn't it? The risk of this deal is rather high."

Zuo Tianying smiled cunningly and blinked his eyes. "Other people may be intimidated by the risk, but for Boss Bai, have you ever been scared of the four Kurfürst families? During the battle of Blue Star City, you were bold enough to loot the coalition auction of the four Kurfürst families. Not only were all the extraordinary treasures in the auction plundered by you, but even the nobles who participated in the auction were also captured. Then, you evaded the hunting of the four Kurfürst families for thirty-seven times in the next three months, forcing the enemy to pay the ransom in order to get the hostages back. If you are bold enough to make such an astonishing deal, how can you not have the courage to declare war on the four Kurfürst families?"

It was both a secret exaltation and an indirect reminder for Boss Bai—What you did in Blue Star City was too outrageous and blatant. You were already the thorns in the flesh for the old nobles after the smack on the faces of the four Kurfürst families. Did you have a second choice other than jumping on the tank of the reformists?

As he expected, when the "glorious feat" was mentioned, Boss Bai hooted like an owl and nodded his head gloatingly. "That's right, that's right. The universe is large, but there are few businesses that Boss Bai does not dare to do. However, a businessman is not scared of risks, only returns that are not abundant enough. Tell me about it, what will I gain if I stand on the side of the reformists, and what about my Big Bai Pirate Gang as well as the youngsters who make a living with me?"

Zuo Tianying coughed and took out a jade chip from his Cosmos Ring rather mysteriously.

Although it was a "jade chip", it was in the color of dark red and had been produced in the shape of a small scepter.

Holding it in both hands and delivering it to Boss Bai respectfully, he said, "Please take a look, Boss Bai, this is a warrant just issued by the Reform Committee of the Council of Elders. You have been nominated as the highest commander of the garrison of the Hundred Phoenixes Sector. You have also been accoladed as a 'vice admiral' of the army and an honorable 'viscount'.

"As for the fleets under your command, including the affiliating pirate gangs who have joined you, they will all be enlisted into the garrison of the Hundred Phoenixes Sector as the regular army of the Imperium. They are entitled to all the privileges of the army and will have sufficient supplies and monetary support. After the soldiers die, there will be high compensations too."

"Hundred Phoenixes Sector?"

Boss Bai touched the jade chip affectionately but pretended to hesitate. "Vice admiral? Viscount?"

"The Hundred Phoenixes Sector is one of the new worlds that have just been won from the Covenant Alliance. It is a resourceful place and full of hope, enough for anyone to realize their dream."

Zuo Tianying said unhurriedly, "However, since we are on the same side, I will not lie to Boss Bai. You will naturally never report for duty in the Hundred Phoenixes Sector. This is just a title. Only when you have the title will it be appropriate to offer you the corresponding privileges!

"All in all, let's leave the empty stuff behind and talk about the more concrete things. As long as you accept the warrant, you will turn from a space pirate that everybody spits on into a regular army that is legitimate and backed by the entire Imperium!"

"Huh?"

Boss Bai laughed and craned his rat-like head over. He looked peaceful and delighted, but there was a certain infinite horror and coldness in his voice. "Why, Deacon Zuo, do you feel that it is a shame to be a space pirate, and everybody should spit on them?"

Zuo Tianying was more or less stunned. Highly uncomfortable under Boss Bai's eyes, he struggled to reply, "That-That is not what I meant. Being a space pirate is certainly not bad, except… except that it may be inconvenient now and then. If you can be a general of the government army, why would you be a space pirate?"

"It does make sense."

Boss Bai suddenly retreated his aggressive eyes and grinned, as if it were not a big deal. "However, is the warrant presented by the Business Alliance of Ten Thousand Sectors reliable or not?"

Chapter 2441: Boss Bai, How Dare You!

"Of course it is reliable. Please trust us!"

Zuo Tianying said sincerely, "Everybody knows the relationship between the 'Business Alliance of Ten Thousand Sectors' and the reformists. In the beginning, it was thanks to the sponsorship of the Business Alliance of Ten Thousand Sectors that the reformists started their cause. One of the core concerns of the reformists is also to help us seize the business interests that are supposed to be ours.

"We've helped the reformists for a long time, and it is time for a harvest!

"Right now, Jin Yuyan, the General Deacon of the Business Alliance of Ten Thousand Sectors, is in charge of the finances of the new Imperium in the Reform Committee of the Council of Elders as a central leader of the reformists. How can his promise not be reliable?

"Just rest assured. As long as you accept the warrant, your career will immediately soar to the sky!"

"Is that so?"

Boss Bai held the dark red jade chip with his fingers and played with it again and again, but he never activated the jade chip to read the content inside. Instead, he looked at Zuo Tianying with a sophisticated smile and said, "I'm afraid that it can't be so simple, right? We've both been through too many dangers to not know that there is no such thing as free lunch. Hehe. Vice admiral, viscount, and commander of the Imperium's army! What do you want in exchange for those fabulous titles?"

"Great! Boss Bai, you are truly an outspoken man!"

Zuo Tianying raised his thumb and coughed again. Then, he threw out the real terms in a low voice, "Since Boss Bai is so straightforward, I'll stop beating around the bush. If you accept the warrant and become a glorious member of the 'new army', you will naturally have to listen to the command of the Reform Committee of the Council of Elders. I know that Boss Bai is a man who does not like restrictions and is used to freedom, but you have to take action with us at least on this one."

Mysteries beamed out of Boss Bai's eyes as he asked casually, "What kind of operation are we talking about?"

"Defend the 'Seven Seas Grand Market', the largest free fair of the Business Alliance of Ten Thousand Sectors, which is also where our organization is headquartered, together with us in the Seven Seas Space Zone!"

Zuo Tianying said solemnly, "According to the latest intelligence, in order to cut the connection between the reformists and the Business Alliance of Ten Thousand Sectors, and to cut the money and resources flowing from the edge of the Imperium to the heart incessantly, the four Kurfürst families have already gathered a coalition fleet, planning to clear the reformists' forces at the peripheral worlds of the Imperium. Their primary target is naturally the headquarters of the Business Alliance of Ten Thousand Sectors.

"The Business Alliance of Ten Thousand Sectors and the space pirates are interdependent. Once the Business Alliance of Ten Thousand Sectors is destroyed, your space pirates will have no place for maintenance, supplies, and exchange of assets and information, and you will be executed by the four Kurfürst families one after another.

"Therefore, the Business Alliance of Ten Thousand Sectors has been contacting the great men at the edge of the Imperium and preparing to fight a spectacular head-on battle to defend the Seven Seas Grand Market!

"Boss Bai, the reformists and the Kurfürsten are in a predicament at the center of the cosmos right now, but countless warlords and magnates from other worlds are still observing and hesitating. Those people are exactly 'the silent majority'. Whoever wins their support is likely to win this war and then seize the entire Imperium, ruling over the sea of stars!

"If the Business Alliance of Ten Thousand Sectors can win the battle to defend the Seven Seas Grand Market, the weakness and inability of the Kurfürst families will be completely revealed. By then, not only will all the worlds at the periphery of the Imperium side with the reformists, even the people within the territories of the Kurfürst families who crave for a reform will also rise up and turn the world upside down!

"The battle is of paramount importance for us. If Boss Bai can help us win this war, you will be a well-deserved great contributor to the reform. Do I need to tell you the fame, position, resources, and subordinates that will come with your achievement?"

"That explains a lot. And here I was wondering why the Business Alliance of Ten Thousand Sectors was asking for the help of so many pirate gangs so anxiously. So, the truth is that your nest is about to be occupied!"

Boss Bai said, "What about the reformists' own fleet? I was told that the reformists boast three main troops right now: the Deep Sea University, the Astounding Thunder Fleet, and the Imperial Guards. They are also working hard to sway the expedition army in the newly-conquered area. Once the expedition army changes to the reformists' side, they will be capable of a head-to-head battle against the Kurfürsten's fleets, right? Why are the fleets of the reformists not doing anything? Are the space pirates like us cannon fodder for you?"

Zuo Tianying thought to himself that, despite his arrogance, the sleazy-looking man was rather sober when it came to the critical things.

But that was within his expectation. If the man did not have such soberness, he wouldn't have become the most brilliant space pirate at the edge of the Imperium in recent years!

With even more sincerity on his face, Zuo Tianying said, "The specific military deployments are top secrets that I am not privileged to know, but I know that this is a critical battle for the reformists, and the best Deep Sea Fleet, the Astounding Thunder Fleet, and the Imperial Guards will not stand by.

"However, the universe is a vast place, and even the largest fleet is but a grain of sand. It will certainly be great if we can talk more men into joining the side of brightness and justice!

"Perhaps when the mantis is hunting the cicada, a sparrow is lurking behind to take the mantis. What if the elites of the reformists appear at their hometowns when the coalition fleets of the four Kurfürsten attack the Seven Seas Grand Market?

"How about it, Boss Bai? Almost forty pirate gangs have accepted the warrants and agreed to side with the reformists and the Business Alliance of Ten Thousand Sectors to resist the evil army of the Kurfürsten. Also, they all admire you very much and agreed that as long as you also accept the warrant, they will accept your command in the battle to come. This is a rarely-seen opportunity and the greatest glory for any space pirate!"

Zuo Tianying exclaimed in excitement, as if he were hoping to replace Boss Bai as the leader of the alliance of space pirates.

Boss Bai's breath became apparently hot.

Several red spots burst out on his face that looked like orange peel.

His throat was rolling nonstop while he swallowed hard.

"I am not a man of vanity, hehehehe…"

He finally changed his attitude and said, "Or rather, I think more highly of the practical things than vanity. Things like viscount, vice admiral, commander, or alliance leader are too far away. Let's just talk about the benefits in front of us! Deacon Zuo, about the things you promised when you invited me to come to the Blue Sky Market…"

"The things are all in the dock waiting for Boss Bai's men to pick up!"

Zuo Tianying knew that the guy had finally bitten the hook. He chuckled to himself and pulled the curtain of the office.

Through a clear window, it could be seen that there was a huge dock outside that extended all the way to the boundless space.

The parking places, like the branches of a tree, attracted bizarrely shaped and aggressive starships. Tremendous heavy tractors and spiritual puppets were busy shipping and unloadings like ants and bees.

"Twenty rapid stealth warships of the latest model, five thousand standard crystal suits of the Imperium's army, and ten heavy carriers' worth of crystals, fuel, and ammunitions!"

Zuo Tianying waved his hands and declared generously, "This is the welcome gift that the Business Alliance of Ten Thousand Sectors offers to Boss Bai—no, for Vice Admiral Bai!

"Not that I'm bragging about us, but we are never short of money when we have controlled the business lifelines at the peripheral worlds of the Imperium. Even if there is any magical equipment we can't produce, we can smuggle them out from the territories of the four Kurfürst families. Look at those starships and crystal suits. They are still hot!

"We are only short of people to control them; the good men who roam the sea of stars like Boss Bai!"

"Hmm…"

Looking at the brand-new starships and the boxes of crystals and cannons, Boss Bai finally grinned in satisfaction. "Good, good, good! The Business Alliance of Ten Thousand Sectors''sincerity' is so touching that I'm almost in tears!"

Overjoyed, Zuo Tianying asked, "So, has Boss Bai agreed to lead the many space pirates?"

"Of course I have. Why wouldn't I?"

Baring his sharp teeth, Boss Bai said slowly, "As a matter of fact, I agreed a long time ago!"

"Huh?"

Zuo Tianying was slightly dazed as he perceived extreme danger from Boss Bai's smile. Before he figured out what was going on, deafening explosions had happened outside.

BOOM! BOOM! BOOM! BOOM! BOOM!

Quite a few starships in the dock had exploded uncannily, and the furious flames immediately spread into the Blue Sky Market. Intense smoke was everywhere, swallowing people's lives mercilessly like rampant black beasts.

Hundreds of starships, like crocodiles lurking in a swamp, finally lunged out of the dark and arrived at the Blue Sky Market. They opened their bloody mouths and spurted out a storm of barrages, sabotaging the defensive positions of the Blue Sky Market ruthlessly.

"Enemy incoming! Enemy incoming! Enemy incoming!"

Earsplitting alarms echoed in and out of the Blue Sky Market.

"Huh?"

Seeing that the Blue Sky Market that he had worked on for decades was reduced to debris in the blink of an eye, Zuo Tianying was shocked first and then grabbed by fury.

If he still did not realize what was going on, he wouldn't have been qualified to be a deacon of the Business Alliance of Ten Thousand Sectors. "Boss Bai, you are out of your mind to attack the Business Alliance of Ten Thousand Sectors!"

As the manager of one of the largest free fairs of the Business Alliance of Ten Thousand Sectors, Zuo Tianying had the combat ability of the Nascent Soul Stage too. Despite his fury, his nerves and his muscles were not affected at all. While he kicked at Boss Bai's chest, a bluish, poisoned dagger ejected from the tip of his foot, and a dark gold brilliance flowed all over his body, constituting a crystal suit that covered him.

However, the crystal suit that he had been trained with so many times that he could put on in half a second was disrupted by Boss Bai when the helmet and the breastplate were about to be closed.

Zuo Tianying did not even notice how Boss Bai blinked from the poisonous dagger on his foot at all when his throat was already pinched by a pair of burning pincers.

The enemy shook slightly, and the crystal suit that had yet to be put on fell apart like a snake skin!

How is it possible?

Zuo Tianying was scared out of his wits. He was already an expert in the Nascent Soul Stage, but the enemy had captured him even without a crystal suit.

Who was this Boss Bai exactly? And what level was he actually at?

Chapter 2442: Commander Bai, of the National Protection Army!

"Hehe, hehehehe!"

Boss Bai sneered nonstop. The aggressiveness as if he could swallow a tiger alive popped up on his ugly, rat-like face as he picked up Zuo Tianying, the expert in the Nascent Soul Stage, with only one hand and smashed him to the floor heavily.

BOOM!

Not counting his level, Zuo Tianying was still a muscular man almost two meters tall with a weight of almost a hundred and fifty kilograms. Yet, he had been pressed into the floor by Boss Bai who was short and skinny. Stones were splashing everywhere, and a human-shaped dent appeared on the ground.

Zuo Tianying's internal organs were almost entirely shattered because of the smash. He was more confused than ever too. According to the intelligence, Boss Bai was in the Nascent Soul Stage and on par with him at best.

It was also the most important reason why he had the courage to meet with a brutal man like Boss Bai alone.

The intelligence work of the Business Alliance of Ten Thousand Sectors was most effective in the entire Imperium. They had summarized Boss Bai's combat ability by analyzing all of Boss Bai's battles, including his performance when he was pushed into corners by the Imperium's army.

How on earth could he have been so terrifying?

Was it possible that this Boss Bai never tried his best when he was beleaguered by the Imperium's army and his life was at risk time and time again?

How could there be such a monster?

Also, where were the assassins he had kept outside? He had arranged for fifty tough assassins to hide next to the office, who could've charged out any moment and teach Boss Bai a lesson about the Business Alliance of Ten Thousand Sectors' capability. Where the hell were those guys?

"You small-minded dealers really need to change your pettiness. Even your warrants are so stingy."

Boss Bai pressed Zuo Tianying tightly to the ground and spat next to his head, before he chuckled and said, "A garrison commander of a minor Sector, a vice-admiral of the army, and a… f*cking viscount… Viscount! Are you trying to recruit me or insult me? Is the name of 'Boss Bai' only worth a viscount for you?

"Here, the four Kurfürsten are obviously much more sincere than you. The rewards they are offering are the Sector Master of a whole world, a lieutenant-general of the army, and an earl of the Imperium. Do you hear me? An earl!

"Also, they have offered me ten main-force warships in advance, whose firepower is five times fiercer than the rapid attack warships that you give me. Tsk, tsk, tsk. Any man would've been tempted after they saw it. How do you want me to choose, huh?"

Zuo Tianying was so shocked that his soul was almost frozen. Finding it hard to breathe, he shrieked, "Boss Bai, you have taken sides with those 'illegitimate Kurfürsten'!"

"Their legitimacy is the least of my concerns. We are all space pirates making a living between the edges of the blades. Isn't it only natural for us to work for those who are stronger and promise better rewards? The four Kurfürsten are far more powerful than the reformists and your Business Alliance of Ten Thousand Sectors in the first place. They've given us more money and greater titles. Something would be wrong with my head if I chose you hopeless idiots over them only to be beheaded together with you after you fail, wouldn't it?"

Boss Bai strangled his neck harder and harder while he said with a smile, "Right, I almost forgot to tell you. Didn't you mean to start an alliance of dozens of pirate gangs? Your trouble is unnecessary. We've already done it under the witness of the special envoy of the Kurfürsten! Thanks to the trust of my dear friends, they have elected me as their leader. I am now the commander of the 'National Protection Army'.

"This commander is certainly much more awesome than the commander that you offered to me. Haha. Hahahaha!"

His face gray, Zuo Tianying was rendered speechless. There was no telling whether it was because of Boss Bai's choking or because of his fury, but the expert in the Nascent Soul Stage was almost cramping and foaming.

"Just look at all the pirate gangs outside. Do you think that they've been attracted here by your terms to pledge loyalty to you? You can't be more idiotic!"

Boss Bai continued, "Let me tell you, the twenty-nine pirate gangs have already formed a blood alliance known as the 'National Protection Army', which serves and swears loyalty to the government. We are now the legitimate army of the government right now. The conquest of the Blue Sky Market today is just an appetizer. The 'Seven Seas Grand Market', the nest of the Business Alliance of Ten Thousand Sectors, is our real target!

"Tsk, tsk, tsk. The Seven Seas Grand Market, the allegedly wealthiest place at the edge of the Imperium, where many treasures that could not be found even in the capital could be found. Robbing the Seven Seas Grand Market is almost the ultimate dream of every space pirate.

"We are all thieves and murderous bandits who would feel itchy at night if we did not rob anyone during the day, and you want us to defend the Seven Seas Grand Market? With all due respect, what's wrong with the reformists and the small merchants like you?"

Zuo Tianying finally couldn't hold it back any longer. A mouthful of foams mixed with blood was spurted out, and his face turned from pale to gray.

They had truly opened the door for the burglars!

Stupid. They were truly stupid. Or rather, they far underestimated the shamelessness and greediness of those space pirates!

But there was no time to regret right now.

The flames and explosions outside were the best punishment for their naivety.

As one of the largest free fairs of the Business Alliance of Ten Thousand Sectors, the Blue Sky Market naturally had the most thorough defense.

Also, since it was the time of war, the guards of the market had been increased to the limit. Space torpedoes and all kinds of serial defense arrays were deployed in the stone belts outside.

It was extremely difficult for the Kurfürsten's fleets to conquer the iron can that had been armed to teeth from outside. They would have to pay a terrible price.

However, the Blue Sky Market was a place for business, and it could not reject the pirate gangs who often stopped by for maintenance and exchange of information and goods.

This time, in order to recruit the great pirate heads including Boss Bai, hundreds of warships of the space pirates had been allowed to park in the Blue Sky Market. A thousand small warships, half-armed business starships, and comprehensive supply ships were anchored outside too.

Who could've thought that those space pirates would attack at the same time?

Right when the space pirates sabotaged the Blue Sky Market, churning the defense into holes from inside to outside, a more terrifying enemy appeared from the vast place. It was the regular army with the emblem of the Imperium and the symbols of the four Kurfürst families.

Cooperating with the pirate gangs, the regular army dug through the belt defenses outside of the Blue Sky Market by force and arrived over the head of the panicked members of the Business Alliance of Ten Thousand Sectors. It was truly a disaster.

When a storm of firepower was rained down, blatantly destroying the firing points on the ground and the warships of the Business Alliance of Ten Thousand Sectors that had yet to take off, the short battle came to an end.

In the standard of "small merchants", the warriors of the Business Alliance of Ten Thousand Sectors were certainly loyal and brave enough.

Perhaps it was because they knew clearly that they would still be killed even if they surrendered.

Perhaps they had been space pirates themselves, and they knew how ferocious pirate gangs were and how cruel their outcome would be if they fell to the hands of the space pirates. So, they simply fought on until their last breath.

The space pirates were the most cunning guys who valued themselves more than anything. Naturally, they stayed away from the rabid dogs.

The Kurfürsten's army, on the other hand, was determined to bite the hard bone that was the Blue Sky Market, which would be a wonderful overture for the battle in the Seven Seas Grand Market to come. As they attacked without caring about the sacrifices, they blew up the last resisting fortress half a day later after losing a hundred starships.

The Blue Sky Market had fallen.

"Aoooooo!"

The ravenous space pirates wandered about in the Blue Sky Market, blowing up the gates of the warehouses with their storm bolters and searching for assets with their bloodshot eyes.

The free fairs were the ultimate dreams for all space pirates, but since the Business Alliance of Ten Thousand Sectors was wealthy and powerful before, and they needed the free fairs for sales and supplies, it had always been inappropriate for them to attack.

Now that they were backed by the four Kurfürsten, their wish was finally fulfilled. They naturally wasted no time sabotaging, looting, and enjoying themselves.

Boss Bai, on the other hand, dragged Zuo Tianying, who was devastated and had been tied up by electromagnetic whips, to the No. 1 dock of the Blue Sky Market that was still burning.

A daunting starship that looked like a giant whale with one horn had arrived from the sky and landed here.

A middle-aged man, wearing a pure white cape, with a face as smooth as that of a woman and filled with pride and indifference, was waiting rather in impatience.

He looked at the fire and mess in the Blue Sky Market in disgust while he wiped his nose that had nothing dirty at all with a gold-lined handkerchief, as if it were a great insult for him to breathe the same air with the space pirates.

"My lord!"

Seeing that the man was waiting, Boss Bai hurried to approach him; his arrogance when he was faced with Zuo Tianying a moment ago entirely gone. In a sycophantic smile, he fell on one of his knees and looked at the man's foot. "Greetings, my lord. Thanks to my lord's meticulous planning, we have captured the head of the rebels in the Blue Sky Market successfully. Please look, my lord. This is Zuo Tianying!"

Boss Bai tossed Zuo Tianying to the ground as if he were a bag of garbage.

Zuo Tianying rolled on the ground to the feet of this "lord". He was immediately refreshed when he saw who the man was. Losing his calmness, he shouted, "Li Wuji!"

Li Wuji was an old acquaintance, or an old opponent, for the Business Alliance of Ten Thousand Sectors.

As the hereditary "Marquess Yongchun", he was one of the powerful persons in the Li family with a rather pure bloodline.

Also, Li Wuji had been engaged in the intelligence work of the four Kurfürst families. He had been striking, sabotaging, and bribing the intelligence network of the Business Alliance of Ten Thousand Sectors.

In the past decades, too many spies of the Business Alliance of Ten Thousand Sectors had been captured, tortured, and killed by Li Wuji.

Naturally, a lot of people had also betrayed the Business Alliance of Ten Thousand Sectors and joined the four Kurfürst families under coercion, causing great damage to the development of the Business Alliance of Ten Thousand Sectors

He did not expect that Li Wuji had supervised the recruitment of Boss Bai and other space pirates as well as the conquest of the Blue Sky Market. Zuo Tianying swallowed hard, his eyes dimming, and he lost all hope.

Chapter 2443: My Loyalty for My Lord Is Unquestionable!

Li Wuji extended his foot and kicked Zuo Tianying in a relatively clean part on his body in order to see his face clearly. After recognizing him, he snorted without giving approval or disapproval.

A personal guard immediately moved forward and kneeled on the ground respectfully, wiping the tip of Li Wuji's foot with a giant handkerchief.

It was almost like his delicate, sharp-pointed boot had been tarnished by Zuo Tianying's clothes.

Li Wuji accepted the service of his guard fully at ease. Glancing at Boss Bai who was still on one of his knees and who seemed loyal and dutiful, he said casually, "Rise. You've done a good job this time."

"Thank you, my lord!"

Boss Bai rose in a rather sleazy way, with his small eyes rolling quickly. He rubbed his hands, as if he were greatly delighted. He looked like a totally different person from the great bandit who drank juice in Zuo Tianying's office with his legs akimbo a moment ago.

"Of the many rebels of the Business Alliance of Ten Thousand Sectors, this Zuo Tianying is not among the most important ones, but he is still something. It wasn't easy to catch him."

Expressionlessly, Li Wuji said in a high-pitched voice, "We couldn't have caught this thief in time if you hadn't snuck into the Blue Sky Market. If we can send him back before the new emperor is crowned, it will be a fairly fabulous gift. Commander Bai, you have made great contributions!"

"I daren't say that!"

Boss Bai declared passionately, "It was all thanks to my lord's planning and your marvelous fleet that those scoundrels were suppressed. I barely did anything.

"Even though—hehe—even though I have made any minor contribution, I do not have any courage to ask for rewards, and I only hope that the things that I did before can be pardoned. About that…"

Boss Bai rubbed his hands, as if he did not quite know how to phrase it, fearing that the four Kurfürst families would punish him for what he did in the past.

Amused, Li Wuji glanced at Boss Bai's shoulder.

The guard nearby immediately understood. Throwing away the handkerchief that was used to clean his boot, the guard took out a new one that was big, soft, and fragrant and covered Boss Bai's shoulder with it.

With the handkerchief in the middle, Li Wuji patted Boss Bai's shoulder neither too heavily nor too lightly and said in encouragement, "Have you ever heard that even the gods will condone the mistakes that young people make?

"I like young people best, particularly those who are both smart and loyal. You have shown your loyalty in the conquest of the Blue Sky Market this time. Are the absurdities you did in your younger days really a big deal?

"Of course, there are indeed many dissidents within the family who have been speaking ill of the space pirates. Also, your robbery of the coalition auction of the four Kurfürst families was truly quite huge and pissed off a lot of people in the families.

"But so what? Only the capable guys are able to achieve something that huge. If you were an unknown, useless man, you wouldn't have been qualified to be the leader of dozens of pirate gangs and the commander of the 'National Protection Army', would you?

"Therefore, I have already told all the dissidents that you—Boss Bai, Commander Bai—are recruited by me in person, which means that you are my man! I have vouched for you that you have recognized your wrongdoings and changed into a different person. Whoever goes against you will be insulting me! Tell me about it, is there anything that you should be worried about?"

"Thank you, my lord!"

Moved to tears, Boss Bai fell on his knees again. "My-My loyalty for my lord is certainly unquestionable. I will always follow my lord's instructions in the future! As long as my lord wants it, I will simply march forward with my eyes closed even if a mountain of blades and an ocean of fire are ahead of me. So much as I waver for a single moment, 'Boss Bai' shall be cursed in the Imperium for all eternity!"

"Haha. Hahahaha!"

Li Wuji burst into laughter. "Rest assured. Rest assured. When I recruited you, I promised you that I would offer you a great future. Why would I put you in danger?

"However, well, so many pirate gangs have been gathered here under your command, and half of my starships have to stay here for maintenance due to the heavy losses in the assault just now. They will be under your command after they are repaired too!

"So many of the most advanced starships and bravest warriors have been given to you. I'm sure that you know what I want in return. I only hope that you can distinguish yourself or even play a vital role in the conquest of the Seven Seas Grand Market later.

"If you can achieve that, even greater wealth and glories will be waiting for you. The fourth quadrant is the hometown of 'God of War' Lei Chenghu. Years ago, he fought his way out of this place through real battles in person too. But he is old now. It's time to let the new-generation fleet commanders emerge. From a pirate to a general, and from a general to a lord—such examples are not rare in the history of the Imperium. Who knows whether or not you are going to be the next?"

"Rest… Rest assured, my lord!"

Hot steams spurted out of Boss Bai's nostrils. His face was red, and veins were protruding on his forehead. "I will die to return my lord's appreciation!"

"Okay!"

Li Wuji nodded his head in satisfaction. He was about to pat Boss Bai's shoulder again, but his hands stopped in the midair.

Slightly frowning, he glanced at his personal guard.

His personal guard, as if slapped hard by an electromagnetic whip, almost jumped to his feet in fear. He hurried to take away the second handkerchief and replaced it with a third clean, new one.

Observing carefully to make sure that there was no dust or sweat on the handkerchief, Li Wuji finally patted Boss Bai's shoulder again. "A prodigal who returns is more precious than gold. Young man, work hard. Remember that you are a glorious lieutenant-general of the regular army right now.
