As long as you serve the country dutifully, I will certainly take care of all the other things for you. There's no need to worry about anything.

"Alright, the mess in the Blue Sky Market will be left to you to clean it up. I am leaving now for other duties. Let's meet again in the 'battle of Seven Seas' to come."

"My lord…"

Boss Bai hesitated for a moment. Glancing at the huge, one-horned warship behind Li Wuji, he said, "I do have another request. I wonder, is it possible that my lord could stay here for another half day?"

"Oh?"

Li Wuji retreated his feet that stepped forward. "Why?"

"The Blue Sky Market has been occupied by the space pirates—no, the 'National Protection Army'. In the next second, I'm planning to hold a grand celebration dinner. Although it is a celebration dinner, it is meant to awe the disobedient heads of space pirates and make them completely obey me."

Boss Bai said earnestly, "My lord, you should know that those space pirates are all brutal and rebellious. Even though they all agreed that I should be the commander of the 'National Protection Army' on the surface, a lot of them actually do not want to obey me and even want to replace me.

"While I have earned a tiny bit of fame in space during the past few years, I am not deeply-rooted after all and may not be able to compete with the seniors who have been infamous for decades. If I cannot suppress them hard, it will be inconvenient to command them in the future.

"Therefore, while my lord is still here, if you could join our celebration dinner and endorse me to intimidate those seasoned thieves, it will be easier for me to mobilize and command them!"

Li Wuji slightly frowned. "Huh…"

"My lord—"

Boss Bai continued, "The battle of Seven Seas is underway. My National Protection Army will be an unexpected army in the battle. What matters most in a surprising attack is commanding and administration. If the pirate gangs cannot be united, the surprising attack will not be as effective as planned. Even though I am really determined to die for my lord, I would not be able to return your appreciation if those seasoned thieves do not obey me!"

His words convinced Li Wuji.

"Fine."

The third-level lord who was loyal to the four Kurfürst families nodded his head and said after a moment of consideration, "I'll stay for another half day to back you up!"

Half a day later, on the major square of the Blue Sky Market, the scents of meat and wine were soaring to the sky. The brutal bandits from dozens of pirate gangs, mostly in bizarre clothes with scars all over their faces, had been gathered. Donned in the fancy garment they just stole from the warehouses and holding the alluring whores in their arms, they drank and ate and roared to their hearts' content.

Of course, theoretically speaking, those hideous fellows were no longer notorious space pirates but the "National Protection Army", a regular army acknowledged by the Council of Elders.

"Haha. This is truly a gratifying battle and also a gratifying loot! The Business Alliance of Ten Thousand Sectors is truly affluent. Even the Blue Sky Market, which only ranks in the top five, has so many treasures. I truly wonder what the 'Seven Seas Grand Market', which ranks top, looks like."

"Hehe. You'll have an opportunity to check it out in a few days. When the day comes, we will loot the hell out of it exactly like we did today!"

"What 'loot'? Remember, we are a legitimate army of the government now. Haha. We are not space pirates in a robbery, but government soldiers settling a rebellion. Naturally, we will need to confiscate the belongings of the rebels after they are settled!"

The space pirates were all drunk, but Boss Bai was still toasting them while carrying a giant tank of wine.

The space pirates were all experienced, high-leveled Immortal Cultivators who were highly wary when they were sober. They would not be drunk even if they drank a thousand cups of regular alcohol. Also, they often forbade themselves from getting drunk and kept themselves a hundred percent sober every second.

But today was different. What Boss Bai offered today was the wine mixed with powerful excitants and Heavenly Materials and Earthly Treasures that could paralyze the nerves and the soul of the high-level experts.

Everybody on the spot was aware of that. Even Li Wuji had given connivance.

Drinking and getting drunk was not because they were drunkards; it was only a demonstration of power.

I am the commander of the National Protection Army and the leader of the alliance of space pirates. When I offer you wine, what I want to see is how you make a fool of yourself after you get drunk. Will you drink it and get drunk?

If you don't, you will be showing no respect for me, violating my order, and not regarding me as a commander and your boss, won't you?

If you do not drink any wine and never get drunk, how can I trust that you will completely follow my command and carry out my orders without any hesitation on the battlefield in the future?

Chapter 2444: Arrogance of the Scoundrel!

There was not much trust among the space pirates. They were merely gathered together because the Kurfürst families were powerful and gave abundant rewards.

Letting Boss Bai be the commander of the jumbled "National Protection Army" was also meant for him to take the bullet for everyone when things didn't go well.

The space pirates never hesitated to shift sides. While the Kurfürsten did look stronger for now, what if something went wrong and the reformists really defeated the Kurfürsten in the end?

Later, when the reformists assigned blames, the commander of the "National Protection Army" would naturally be the first to be executed!

For those heads of space pirates who had raged the sea of stars for decades, none truly agreed with Boss Bai's leadership.

However, Li Wuji, a legitimate noble of the Imperium and a powerful person of the Kurfürst families, was standing right behind Boss Bai. Further away, tremendous starships of the Imperium were also watching.

With that in mind, when Boss Bai toasted them with giant bowls, it was impossible for them to turn down the "kindness".

It couldn't be helped. For the sake of Li Wuji… and so many starships, they had to be drunk today.

"Brothers, the Blue Sky Market now completely belongs to the space pirates—no, the National Protection Army! There can't be a starship of the Business Alliance of Ten Thousand Sectors or the reformists in the millions of kilometers around. Also, we have controlled all the space zones nearby that are suitable for space jumps. Even a feature would be burnt up immediately after it is teleported! So, just rest assured. This place is absolutely safe. You can put down all your concerns and enjoy the dinner. Whoever is still awake after the dinner can slap me in the face. If you think that the dinner is not enjoyable enough for you, and if you don't slap me in the face, it will mean that you are not showing any f*cking respect for Boss Bai! Haha! Hahahaha!"

Boss Bai was the first to get drunk and soon revealed his real appearance of a scoundrel who did not know who he was at all. He seemed rather restrained and batted the alloy tank of wine hard with the steel bowl, barely able to speak clearly.

His clothing was almost that of a textbook parvenu.

The battle in the Blue Sky Market was just over, and the temperature was still high despite manual adjustment. However, he was wearing a red leather coat that he stole from the warehouse as well as a chimney-like hat that weighed almost ten kilograms. At the top of the hat was a colorful, glittering marrow crystal that was glowing like a searchlight. He couldn't have seemed more extravagant.

It was a shame that the priceless leather coat and the hat were designed for the muscular men more than two meters tall living on the frigid planets. Boss Bai, on the other hand, was a skinny, emaciated shorty. When he was covered in such a huge coat, he was like a kid who had stolen the clothes of the adults. It was needless to say that the big hat was falling all the time and covering his face, turning into a headgear that did not have holes for the eyes and the mouth. As a result, he was forced to poke the hat with his fingers now and then in order to keep it steady. He did not leave the impression that he was a magnificent man at all, and even the most uneducated space pirate couldn't help but think that he was a monkey in the clothes of a human!

That was not all of it. In order to stuff the coat and the hat, Boss Bai had also inserted a lot of rarely-seen cloths into his coat. The things were great decorations, but they were of little use when he simply stuffed them into his arms.

He did not give himself away yet when he gave the "inauguration speech of the commander of the National Protection Army", but things gradually went wrong as he toasted the space pirates table by table. His face was redder and redder, and his movement was greater and greater. As a result, the cloths were either dropped to the ground or dragged behind him, making him look like a red-skinned mouse that had grown out variegated tails.

A few guys from the Big Bai Pirate Gang had noticed it, but they did not have the courage to remind their boss. Looking at each other in bewilderment, they could only follow Boss Bai while holding the cloths that had accidentally fallen. The scene was so hilarious that even the most experienced and heartless bandit would've burst into laughter.

As for the giant Cosmos Rings on his fingers, they were probably just stolen from the warehouses, and Boss Bai was so busy to show himself off that he did not have time to crack the retrieval code. It was needless to mention the trivia, such as his pockets that were brimming with crystals, with a few dropping to the ground after every step he made forward.

Most of the space pirates were too greedy to care about their image, but even they felt that the "superstar" Boss Bai, who miraculously rose to fame in the past two years, was too humiliating!

It also proved the opinion of many seasoned thieves on him. He certainly had his capabilities, but he was too arrogant and ostentatious. Such people were like firecrackers, who would fall as fast as they rose. So, he was the perfect choice for them to use as a shield.

He could be the commander of the National Protection Army if he wanted to. After all, if the coalition army of the nobles won, they would earn benefits by the way. By then, if the few experienced space pirates joined their hands, would it be a problem at all to make the arrogant little boy a figurehead? Shooting the back of his head on the battlefield and carving up his Big Bai Pirate Gang wouldn't be too hard either!

Should the coalition army of nobles fail and the reformists won, they would immediately "rectify their behavior" by tying up the "commander of the National Protection Army" and sending him to the guillotine of the reformists. By estimation, the followers like them who were "forced to rebel" but who had a "battlefield uprising" later would not be punished severely.

The seasoned thieves had both fought and collaborated in the past decades. As both enemies and friends, they knew each other too well to say it out and understood what each other was thinking only with eye contact. For a moment, all of them were wearing mocking and weird smiles.

Of course, the smiles quickly disappeared and would never be discovered by Boss Bai. Since the man was so hotheaded and thought that he was the king of the world, just let them adulate him and exalt him to even higher places.

Therefore, the atmosphere of the dinner couldn't have been warmer.

"Captain Zhao, do you remember the last job we did together with your 'Tiger Devil Pirate Gang'? Hahahaha. It felt so good! So good! I was thinking back then when we could ever do a job again together. Today, the opportunity has finally come. We are about to work together to rob—no, to settle the rebels in the Seven Seas Grand Market, and I, Boss Bai, am your commander now! Come on, bottoms up! I know that you have a great alcohol tolerance. This keg—this keg is all yours. When the day comes, I will let your 'Tiger Devil Pirate Gang' march in first. The… the… the sexiest women will all belong to you. All of them!

"Captain Sun, we—hehehehe—we wouldn't have known each other without the fight, right? I'm truly sorry for the business in the Black River Space Zone last time, where you lost so many hands. However, for the space pirates, business is business, and friends are friends. Boss Bai almost admires the unparalleled fame of 'Ghost Blade'! That's enough talking. These three cups are to show respect for you and to apologize for my offense last time. After this, we will be brothers. If you are still not satisfied, just vent your anger in the Seven Seas Grand Market with us!"

"Master… Master Qian, you are a well-respected senior in the circle of space pirates, and you started the business more than a hundred years ago, right? It must've been a tough journey. When you began your career, I was still using diapers, haha, hahahaha! The commander of the National Protection Army was supposed to be a place for a renowned senior like you, and it shouldn't have been my turn at all. But I was told that you were having health issues because of previous injuries and that you offered to give the place away? The way I see it, you look rather vigorous. What injuries can possibly hurt you, the 'Ocean Saber'? Come on, this is the best medical wine, 'Tiger Collapse', which is made of the marrows of dozens of ferocious animals. Although the alcohol is tough and you will be drunk for the night, it will be the best treatment to warm up the bones, boost the spiritual energy, and repair the damaged body! Come on, I'll pour the wine for the senior in person. Senior, please!

"Brothers… Brothers, may I have your attention?"

Boss Bai staggered and toasted everyone at the dinner. Naturally, he talked more than he drank, but he had poured buckets of wine into the throats of the space pirates. The lesser space pirates dare not disobey him, while the powerful space pirates, intending to build up his arrogance, also drank plenty of wine to show respect for him.

As a result, even the most tough-looking and quick-witted thieves were more or less drunk now.

Right then, Boss Bai suddenly jumped to the top of a wine bucket and raised his cup high like a drunk bear. "I have a question for everyone. Whose men do you think we are right now?"

The space pirates looked at each other in bewilderment. Some shouted that they were the men of the Imperium's army and the four Kurfürsten.

"Yes, we are, of course, the men of the army and the four Kurfürsten. However—"

Looking around, Boss Bai suddenly bulged his eyes and shouted aloud, "Space pirates are the most disgusting wolves and the greediest swine in the eyes of the regular nobles. None of them would bother to look at us unless it was absolutely necessary. It was all thanks to Marquess Yongchun's negotiation and mediation that the four Kurfürst families finally accepted us!

"Are humans any different from jackals if they are ungrateful? So, remember clearly now. Just leave the Imperium and the four families behind. We are first and foremost men of the Marquess Yongchun. We are the lord's loyal subordinates!

"Whoever the lord tells us to attack, we will attack. Whoever the lord asks us to bite, we will bite. That is exactly the 'National Protection Army'. If who can't do that, don't blame me for cutting him into pieces!

"Come on, let's give our lord a toast to show our respect. No, no, no. One cup is far from enough to express our admiration and gratitude for our lord! One bucket! One bucket for everyone! Fill it up! Fill it up! Here's to the lord!"

Chapter 2445: He Can't Be That Insane, Right?

After hearing that, the space pirates were obliged to drink now. While his words were vulgar, he did have a point. Space pirates were nothing but chamber pots and cleaning cloths for the nobles, who were only giving them the name "National Protection Army" in order to strike the reformists. But thieves were always thieves. Who knew whether or not they would be the next after the reformists were indeed annihilated someday, and they would be kicked away like the filthy, stinky chamber pot?

Therefore, if they could win the support of the powerful persons in the four families, say, Li Wuji who had come to recruit him in person, it would certainly be for the best.

On the other hand, for Li Wuji, if he had a bunch of strong and obedient space pirates at his disposal, it would be quite beneficial for him to expand his influence in the four families and the Council of Elders.

Now that both parties needed each other, they were obviously full of sincerity. Led by Boss Bai, the captains of all the pirate gangs, as well as the lone bandits, raised their wine buckets high and poured the liquids in. The amber-colored wine poured down like a waterfall. Thankfully, as Immortal Cultivators, they were capable of controlling their internal organs and vaporizing the water. Otherwise, their stomachs would've been broken when the buckets of wine were filled into them.

But as a result, when the water was vaporized, the nutritional tonics in the wine was diluted faster too, making them drunk even more quickly.

Seeing that the heads of space pirates were all blushing because of drinking, and the atmosphere was rather heated, Li Wuji slightly nodded his head to show satisfaction.

With his mysophobia, he would certainly not drink the same way as those pirates, but since so many people were toasting him at the same time, it would be inappropriate to not do anything. Therefore, he took out his personal cup and drank a cup of wine, which immediately raised a round of cheers. Naturally, Boss Bai's voice was the loudest and most excited of all.

"In the next—"

Seeing that everybody had chugged a whole bucket of hard liquor, Boss Bai smiled and said, "The battle of Seven Seas is about to come. This is the end of the Business Alliance of Ten Thousand Sectors but the first battle of the National Protection Army, which will be the beginning of our journey to the top of all the troops in the Imperium. Let's drink another bucket of wine to celebrate the triumph of the battle in advance!"

Both the space pirates and the regular soldiers were most superstitious before great battles. Many things were best left unsaid, but now that Boss Bai had proposed to "celebrate the triumph in advance", they naturally had to drink the second bucket of wine.

Otherwise, it would've been a curse that they would fail or die in the battle.

Immediately, a lot of soldiers of the Big Bai Pirate Gang carried hundreds of buckets of hard liquor over. After the seals and the covers were removed, it turned out to be translucent ambers and gems inside the buckets. When the heaters were aimed at the ambers and gems and shined on them for a while, they were immediately "unfrozen" into fragrant wine.

Then, a few metal balls that could adjust temperature were thrown into every bucket, setting the temperature of the wine to the best level. It felt like the wine could crawl into their mouths on its own without them drinking as long as they raised their heads.

"Come on, let's drink up and fight bravely on the battlefield. We are not to humiliate our lord!"

Boss Bai batted the bucket hard and shouted.

He was already staggering, his tongue blurred, and he could barely raise the wine bucket now.

While he was unable to raise the bucket, other people certainly could not follow his example, because Li Wuji and other nobles were watching them coldly!

All the space pirates knew that the dinner was, in fact, a test for them to see if the disobedient space pirates were docile and loyal.

Then, what else could they say? They'd finally found a chance to join the four families. Besides, the Blue Sky Market had been looted by them, meaning that the Business Alliance of Ten Thousand Sectors and the reformists hated their guts right now. They had no choice except to be loyal right now. So, just drink it up!

Therefore, the second bucket of wine was poured in.

But this time, many people were drinking obviously more slowly than previously. Their bellies were rising, and they were surrounded in mists, as if they were in the middle of a sauna bath.

After they finally poured the hard liquors with tremendous drugs that were specifically refined for the Immortal Cultivators into their stomachs, the wretched Boss Bai, out of their expectation, exclaimed again, "Everyone, everyone, I was told that our new emperor will ascend to the throne after the rebels of the Business Alliance of Ten Thousand Sectors are annihilated. While we are all unrefined folks, since we are 'National Protection Army', we certainly cannot forget our loyalty to His Majesty! It is not a tradition to toast only twice. The third bucket of wine is to our future emperor. May the Imperium of True Human Beings thrive and unite the sea of stars!"

All the heads of space pirates looked at each other in bewilderment, feeling a headache.

Although they had committed tremendous murders and other crimes, they barely touched hard liquors, which were essentially powerful aesthetics.

Since they barely drank anything, it was particularly troublesome when they did get drunk. Now that they were drinking such a great quantity of wine, which was also a mix of multiple different kinds of hard liquors, they felt that furious flames were burning their internal organs and flowing into their brains along the veins and nerves, corroding and paralyzing their souls.

If they were to activate their strength, they could naturally resolve the alcohol with their Cultivation.

However, how could they sit cross-legged and press out the alcohol right in front of Boss Bai and Li Wuji?

Many people were already seeing colorful stripes or even stars before their eyes.

Now that Boss Bai announced a third round of toasts, they all cursed in their hearts that the pretentious boy was too boring.

But now that Boss Bai had said that it was for the "future emperor", there was no excuse for them to avoid drinking now. They had already toasted for Li Wuji with a bucket of wine. If they were to refuse to toast His Majesty, not just Boss Bai, even Li Wuji would not agree.

When the toast was for Li Wuji, you drank it, but when it was for the future His Majesty, you refused to drink. Then, was this a "National Protection Army" or Li Wuji's private troop?

Li Wuji certainly knew better than leaving such a weakness for other people to criticize him.

Hardly had Boss Bai concluded his sentence when Li Wuji strode forward and raised his cup, not caring if the space pirates at the dinner were hesitant. "Exactly. The reformists and the Business Alliance of Ten Thousand Sectors are just ants trying to shake a tree in front of the might of His Majesty. When His Majesty ascends to the throne, the iron army of the Imperium will immediately crumble over all the heinous rebels! Come on, everyone, here's to His Majesty and the Imperium!"

He was holding a cup, but what the soldiers of the Big Bai Pirate Gang carried over this time were buckets that were even larger than the previous two rounds.

It was also perfectly reasonable. His Majesty was apparently much higher than a Marquess Yongchun. Naturally, the buckets of wine to toast His Majesty should be larger than those for the Marquess Yongchun.

The competition within the four Kurfürst families was also very intense. It was always the most sensitive period when a new emperor claimed the crown. One moment of carelessness, and even the smallest mistake could become other people's weapons.

Even though they had lost the capital, they hadn't lost their great tradition of inner fights.

One of the reasons why Li Wuji did not want to socialize with the rude space pirates more than he needed to was that their lack of manners would add to his trouble. But out of his expectation, what Boss Bai did was rather flawless. He couldn't help but nod his head in approval.

The heads of the space pirates, however, looked awful. Some of the cautious thieves even grew wary.

To be honest, it was not unusual to attack the enemy under the excuse of meetings or dinners.

But things were different this time.

The battle between the four families and the reformists was fierce. The space pirates, which were large in number, were an important leverage and would tip the scales whichever side they were to join.

They might get removed when they were not needed anymore, but that time had not come yet. So, Li Wuji should be recruiting them sincerely.

Boss Bai, as the "mastermind", had pissed off the Business Alliance of Ten Thousand Sectors with the attack on the Blue Sky Market. He certainly had no reason to betray Li Wuji and the four families and swallow them at such an absolutely inappropriate timing.

Even though Boss Bai had expanded a lot, they had twenty-nine pirate gangs gathered here, and Li Wuji's regular army was also observing coldly. One Big Bai Pirate Gang was no match for them from any perspective.

However insane Boss Bai was, he couldn't be hoping to swallow twenty-nine pirate gangs as well as a fleet of the Kurfürsten on his own, could he?

All the heads of space pirates thought again and again, only to find no suspicious points. So, they believed that the Bai-surnamed boy was only showing off now that things had gotten his way, and it was best to just wait and watch.

Raising their necks, they poured the third bucket of wine into their stomachs, before they all cheered, "We appreciate the lord and His Majesty!"

"Long live His Majesty, long live the Imperium!"

Boss Bai also held the bucket and drank it hard.

But he was too drunk to keep the bucket in his arms. So, he simply tossed it to the ground into pieces, with the wine splashing like blood. "We are all life-and-death brothers after the three buckets of wine! Whoever is disloyal to the Imperium or to his brothers shall be torn into shreds that will turn into rats mauling and biting each other, experiencing the pain more miserable than hell!"

He was completely drunk and began to talk nonsense.

"Commander Bai, this is enough."

Looking at him coldly, Li Wuji said.

"No, it's… it's not enough."

Boss Bai waved his hands uncontrollably and said, "Hahahaha. Today is the happiest day of my life. Can you imagine that…"

He almost waved his hands that were stained with wine to Li Wuji's face. Immediately coming back to himself, he was covered in a cold sweat.

Li Wuji stepped back, his eyebrows almost rising, as he glared at him angrily. "Commander Bai, this is truly enough. I think the atmosphere is heated enough, and other people have shown enough respect for you as their commander."

"Yes, of course."

His lips shivering and his face reddened, Boss Bai said, while the odor of wine spurted out of his mouth, "I'm just too happy and want to serve my lord too much."

Chapter 2446: Safe Journey, My Lord!

Li Wuji took another step back to dodge the odor of wine from Boss Bai's mouth. Covering his nose with a handkerchief, he glanced at his guard.

His guard went forward and announced, "Please enjoy yourselves at the dinner. Our lord has to leave in advance for other duties. By the time the Seven Seas Grand Market is conquered, our lord will hold a party again and entertain the generals here!"

"Goodbye, my lord!"

Boss Bai crossed his hands and bowed deeply, bidding farewell to Li Wuji together with the space pirates.

But as it happened, probably because he had drunk too much wine, or probably he was too excited to digest it for a moment, his stomach roared when he bowed, and he burped so loud that he was almost vomiting the wine out.

Li Wuji was so shocked that his entire face was darkened. He hurried to leave with his nose covered.

The other heads of space privates wanted to laugh but did not have the courage to. Seeing how Boss Bai was making a fool of himself, they threw away the anger that they were forced to drink three buckets of wine a moment ago, and they further believed that the smug guy couldn't have any scheme.

Boss Bai made two acidulous burps and struggled to press the wine in his throat back to his stomach. Knowing that he had greatly humiliated himself and probably pissed off Li Wuji, he hurried to clean up his clothes, wipe the greasy sweat on his face, and chase after Li Wuji.

"My lord, my lord!"

Boss Bai chased after Li Wuji on the road to the No. 1 dock.

"Commander Bai, is there anything else?"

Li Wuji turned around after hearing the calling. He said coldly, "If there's nothing else, just go back and gather your troops. Like you said, it is not an easy task to unite so many pirate gangs. The Imperium has been treating you well. You have to serve the country dutifully to return the favor."

"Yes, yes, I-I'll certainly devote my life and soul to the country. Well…"

Boss Bai stammered, not knowing what he was saying at all. He said in embarrassment, "Well, I was slightly drunk just now. I hope that my lord wouldn't… wouldn't…"

Li Wuji tried to hold his laughter back and put on a smile.

This time, Boss Bai's leather coat had been stained with wine and oil. It was greasy and stinky and couldn't have looked more terrible. Li Wuji, with his mysophobia, wouldn't pat his shoulder in person even if there were ten handkerchiefs on it.

His personal guard knew his master's temper very well. He simply stepped forward on behalf of Li Wuji and patted Boss Bai's shoulder.

"Commander Bai, everything about you is perfect, except that I didn't know that you had a drinking problem."

Li Wuji said thoughtfully, "I know that space pirates can be quite stressed when you make a living for yourselves, and if your nerves are tightened for too long, it is possible for you to get deranged. So, some of you like to soothe the nerves and the soul with special medical wines. It is also some sort of special training.

"However, a great war is coming near, and you have just recruited those disobedient guys, which needs you to be a hundred percent sober. If you fail your mission because of drinking, even I wouldn't be able to save you."

"Of course!"

Boss Bai was suddenly refreshed. He held his chest high and declared, "Rest assured, my lord. I will not touch any drop of alcohol from this moment on. If I ever do, please cut my tongue, my lord!"

"I do not want your tongue. What I want is the Seven Seas Grand Market as well as the lives of the revels of the reformists and the Business Alliance of Ten Thousand Sectors!"

Li Wuji narrowed his eyes and gnashed. Then, he softened his voice and said solemnly, "Commander Bai, don't ever think that I'm simply regarding the likes of space pirates as pawns and cannon fodder. At the very least, you are different from other people.

"If you were a pawn or cannon fodder, your life and death would be none of my concerns. Would I care if you drink at all? There would be no need for me to say any of this to you.

"However, I truly have high hopes for you and believe that you are more than a humble space pirate. Or rather, you have only joined the circle of space pirates soon, and it is still possible to rescue you.

"Come on, there are no other space pirates here. Let's just be honest with each other. Do you think the four Kurfürst families' recruitment of the space pirates is sincere? Is it possible that they will get rid of the space pirates after they are no longer needed?"

Boss Bai was slightly dazed, as if he were caught unprepared by Li Wuji's subtle questions. Not knowing what to say for now, he sweated even harder. "Well, about that, I truly don't know. Well, it's not my place to say."

"You don't know, or you don't want to know?"

With a smile, Li Wuji said unhurriedly, "In fact, you should know that the space pirates who have been in the business for decades are already rotten to the bones. Even though they are under the banner of a legitimate army, they still can't stand restraints. After enough endurance, they will commit wrongdoings again because of the unchangeable nature.

"Therefore, the cooperation with those people is only temporary. One day, the National Protection Army will be further regrouped to clean the mark of space pirates in the past. That's when it will be reborn into a real regular army.

"Just now, you said that you were my men, but are the space pirates really qualified to be my men?

"But you—Commander Bai, Boss Bai—are the only exception. I truly look forward to your future, and I'm raising you as a trusted subordinate. I will also invest in the regrouped National Protection Army in the future without caring about the cost and help you build an unprecedented deep-sky fleet that roams a hundred worlds invincibly.

"In short, I have no doubt that you should be one of the toughest fleet commanders in the Imperium in the next hundred years, but not a clandestine space pirate.

"Now, you should understand how important you are. Young man, a great future is ahead of you. Just try to seize it."

Boss Bai seemed to be greatly shocked, as if he did not expect that Li Wuji would be so straightforward or think so highly of him.

Every wrinkle on his lips and his face was shivering, until he was shedding tears in silence in the end.

"My lord, you… you have given me so much appreciation that you are almost another parent for me!"

Boss Bai cried, "Although… although it was my parents who gave me my life, my life is solely yours now, my lord! It's been many years since my birth parents passed away, and I've been making a living in space on my own for all those years. Nobody taught me any life lesson. Also, because of the environment, I was forced to embark on the wrong path in helplessness. Actually, I regret very much that I became a despicable space pirate.

"My lord has given me a new life. You have raised me so considerately without bothering my humble background. I really don't know what to say right now. My lord, I do have another request. If it is too outrageous, please just consider it my drunk talk. The request is—well, I've been lonely for a long time since my birth parents died, but my lord's kindness for me makes me feel the parental warmth again. Then, it somehow occurs to me how great it would be if my lord adopts me!

"Well, I know it is inappropriate—too inappropriate. My lord's identity is too honorable for you to adopt someone like me as a son. However, considering my lord's age, you are actually in the generation of my grandfather. I'm told that your sons are all strong and well-acknowledged pillars of the country. How fortunate would I be if I could be adopted by one of them and be my lord's foster grandson!"

Slightly shocked, Li Wuji observed Boss Bai carefully.

Somewhat embarrassed under his eyes, Boss Bai chuckled and lowered his head.

"You are really…"

Li Wuji thought for a long time, only to come up with no word to describe Boss Bai. He simply waved his hands and said, "Let's talk about it after the battle of Seven Seas Grand Market is over and the rebels of the Business Alliance of Ten Thousand Sectors are taken care of. Since you are loyal enough, it is not entirely unnegotiable."

"Thank you, my lord!"

Overjoyed, Boss Bai kneeled again.

But this time, he was not falling on one of his knees, but two.

Li Wuji was quick enough to step away. His personal guard also supported Boss Bai to prevent Boss Bai from kneeling.

"Don't do that. You are a general of the Imperium after all, and you'd better have the corresponding demeanor."

Li Wuji couldn't hold it back anymore. Pointing at Boss Bai's leather coat, he said, "Also, hurry to change your clothes. Haven't the regular uniforms and the battle uniforms of the Imperium been distributed to you? A regular soldier should be like a regular soldier!"

"Yes, my lord is right!"

Boss Bai immediately began to take off his clothes, as if he were determined to be naked before Li Wuji.

Li Wuji sighed and couldn't take it anymore. He hurried to go.

"Wait, my lord, please wait!"

Boss Bai chased after him while taking off his clothes.

"Are you not done yet?"

Li Wuji had run out of patience. He said gloomily.

"Nothing. Nothing important. But please be careful on your way."

Boss Bai said pitifully, "You have left half of the fleet here and are leaving with only the other half. I hope nothing will go wrong."

Li Wuji sniffed and said, "The guards of the Business Alliance of Ten Thousand Sectors have been destroyed in the ports of the Blue Sky Market without any survivors. The rest of their fleets are marching to the Seven Seas Grand Market crazily, ready to focus and defend their nest. As for the other fleets of the reformists, they are cleaning up the mess in the capital and have no time to deal with us for now, don't they? What can possibly go wrong?"

Realizing that he had misspoken, Boss Bai said, blushing, "I just want to remind my lord to be wary of… the Cultivators!"

"The Cultivators?"

Li Wuji stopped his foot that was about to step away. "It's been a long time since I heard them. Why, is this place harassed by the Cultivators?"

"Yes, it is."

Boss Bai said solemnly, "The Cultivators are even more brutal and horrible than space pirates. They do not have any personal desires or weaknesses, which means that it is impossible to negotiate with or recruit them. There is no telling what they think, but they seem unafraid of failure or death.

"It had been many years since any Cultivators caused trouble in this area, but they began to raise turmoil again recently. Their general soldiers are not a big deal, just a few farmers and miners who have stolen some chainswords and storm bolters. But their leader, 'Fist King' Lei Zonglie, is really a tough one."

Chapter 2447: The Damn Cultivators!

"Oh?"

Seeing the hate on Boss Bai's face, Li Wuji somehow grew interested. "Why, have you fought him? Is he very tricky to deal with?"

"We are all making a living at the edge of the Imperium. If the Cultivators get more, the space pirates will have less. Naturally, there are frictions."

Thinking for a moment, Boss Bai said, "Most of the Cultivators are not exactly tricky, but they can be more persistent and better at running than us.

"We space pirates do not have many supply and maintenance points in the vast space. After all, most of the cities are scared of us. Before, apart from the free fairs under the Business Alliance of Ten Thousand Sectors, there were only several ports of space pirates where we could be anchored. If we missed the supply points and encountered a space storm, we would be dead for sure.

"Those Cultivators, on the other hand, seemed particularly good at activating the ordinary people. They could also hide themselves among the ordinary people. In every resource planet or every mine, no matter to whom those places belonged, they could always find a way out or melt themselves into the local people.

"In the past twenty years, the Imperium had an expedition first and then… a rebellion later. While the space pirates were developing, the Cultivators were developing too, even faster than the space pirates in a lot of places. That's really unbelievable!

"I've considered it for a long time, and I think that an ancient saying summarizes it quite well. Those who are barefooted are not scared of those who are wearing shoes, and those who are powerful are scared of those who are dogged!

"Compared to the government army and the Business Alliance of Ten Thousand Sectors, all the space pirates are barefooted. We have nothing but our worthless lives, and the battles are always in our favor no matter how they are fought.

"Those wretched Cultivators, on the other hand, are even more destitute and unafraid of death than us. We are all traveling in space to earn money and make a living. Since there are not many profits, we are often reluctant to attack the Cultivators, but those poor wretches often assault us when we have just done a big job, with our arsenals empty and our trophy warehouses full. Once they succeed, they will immediately move on to the resource planets and distribute the stuff they stole from us to the local miners and farmers.

"As you can see, my lord, after those people get benefits from the Cultivators, they will naturally side with the Cultivators and give them information. They will help treat the wounded and fabricate identities, and they will even ask the youngsters in their families who cannot live on anymore to join the team of the Cultivators—the damn 'Starlight'.

"The space pirates always highlight a timely evacuation when an attack fails. How can we march to the resource planets to annihilate the Cultivators? We can only sit and watch them buying people over with the stuff that we looted through all the trouble. What's the outcome? We take all the blame, and they have all the benefits. Yet, there is no way that we can settle the scores with them. It's truly maddening!"

Li Wuji nodded his head and sighed. "Yes. While the Cultivators are just minor trouble compared to the Covenant Alliance and the rebelling reformists, they have been active for a thousand years since the fall of the Star Ocean Republic. During the thousand years, the minor trouble was never completely resolved, and it would be revived after every decade. It is undeniable that the Cultivators do have high expertise in deceptions. Since even the government army has failed to extinguish them, it is only natural that you can't either.

"Right, let's talk about that 'Fist King' Lei Zonglie. I've vaguely heard about the name before when I was in the Li family. He is an expert in the organization of the Cultivators who only rose recently, isn't he? How good is he compared to you?"

"The Fist King is very strong. I've fought him several times. Neither of us was a winner."

When Lei Zonglie was mentioned, Boss Bai immediately grew solemn. Considering for a long time, he said, "However, the deepest impression that the man left on me is not his combat ability. If we are talking about combat ability, so many experts were sent by the four Kurfürst families when they hunted me, but none of them could do anything about me, could they?

"But this 'Fist King' Lei Zonglie is also a specialist of crystal processors and the Spiritual Nexus besides fighting. He is best at hacking the control system of the hostile starships, thereby interfering with and taking over the whole starship.

"When my Big Bai Pirate Gang encountered the Cultivators' fleet of Starlight, 'Fist King' Lei Zonglie could hack my command system and mess up my previously delicate battle formation while he fought with me.

"Damn it. Why is he nicknamed 'Fist King' when he is obviously a specialist of crystal processors? How shameless!

"That was truly the most humiliating thing ever since I began my career. I intended to keep it a secret forever, but since my lord has been treating me so well, would I even deserve to be a human being if I kept anything from my lord? All in all, I suffered a notable setback from 'Fist King' Lei Zonglie, and it was quite fortunate of me to have returned in one piece.

"I was among the lucky dogs. I was told that quite the local garrisons of quite a few Sectors had also encountered the Cultivators' fleet led by 'Fist King' Lei Zonglie, and many of their starships had been hacked and stolen. Under his leadership, the crushed members of 'Starlight' who fled to this place in a panic have been redeveloped and revived, and it took only less than one year.

"I'm thinking that since the war between the four Kurfürst families and the reformists is coming near, the Cultivators are unlikely to sit by and watch but will certainly jump out and stir trouble. Now that the Business Alliance of Ten Thousand Sectors tried to recruit us, who knows whether or not they would recruit Starlight? That's why I asked my lord to be careful on the way."

"Huh…"

Considering for a long time, Li Wuji slightly nodded his head. "The information is very important for our next step in our operation. Commander Bai, it is very considerate of you."

Having been complimented by his master, Boss Bai smiled as delightedly as a blooming flower. "My lord is both strong and blessed. Even though you do encounter that 'Fist King' Lei Zonglie, my lord will certainly crumble him into pieces. It was unnecessary for me to say anything."

"No, whoever the enemy is, one can't be too careful in dealing with them. It's better to be safe than sorry."

Li Wuji looked at Boss Bai with great satisfaction. "You're quite right on this one. It was indeed necessary to report it to me. If you have any other intelligence in the future, just contact me via the private communication channel that I gave to you. Remember, that is a channel only you know. Of all the space pirates, it is your voice that I want to hear."

"Understood!"

Boss Bai smiled in ecstasy. "I will certainly live up to my lord's trust and appreciation!"

"Very good. Now go back and restrain your troops. If they are too greedy and wild, they will have no combat ability at all."

Li Wuji waved his hands. "I've stayed long enough in the Blue Sky Market. If you have anything else, just contact me through the secret letter."

"Of course. Farewell, my lord."

Boss Bai saluted in an unprofessional way first, before he bowed to the ground, his pointy and skinny bottom poking backward.

By the time Li Wuji boarded on "Eternal Spring", his flagship, Boss Bai was still in the dock of the Blue Sky Market like a black, little nail.

Having returned to his personal chamber on his flagship, Li Wuji did not have any smile or trust on his face anymore as he observed Boss Bai through dozens of surveillance cameras.

Through the feedback of the dozens of surveillance cameras, Boss Bai's every movement and every change of expression during the past half day were scanned and analyzed carefully a hundred times.

"My lord, the soundwaves of every word this Boss Bai said just now, as well as his facial changes and other biophysical changes, including heartbeat and blood flow, have been analyzed in detail just now. There… shouldn't be a problem."

A young, handsome adjutant who looked similar to Li Wuji stepped forward and brought him a cup of green tea first before he inputted a comprehensive analysis report into Li Wuji's portable crystal processor. He said boldly, "However, I'm still not fond of him."

The adjutant who had the privilege to enter Li Wuji's personal chamber freely was naturally not a random person. He was the biological son of another powerful person in the family and sort of a nephew for Li Wuji.

It was a tradition that the powerful persons in the family struck an alliance. They would entrust their biological sons to each other for tutelage in exchange for a more valid alliance and deeper trust so that they would be condensed into an inseparable community of interests.

Therefore, the adjutant could be counted as a half true disciple for Li Wuji. Plus the noble identity of his father, he did not need to be too careful about how he talked.

"The Bai guy is certainly not a likable man. When I saw him, I thought of a rat grinding its teeth in the gutter."

His eyes closed, Li Wuji took over the refreshing perfume that the adjutant handed over and smeared it on the sides of his nose, soothing the disgust of dealing with the stinky space pirates. "However, I didn't recruit him for his personal charisma. Even a rat has a rat's usage, doesn't it?"

"He is a treacherous scoundrel."

The adjutant retrieved a few pictures where Boss Bai dragged Zuo Tianying to walk. Then he said, "Please take a look, my lord. How arrogant the Bai guy was when he was faced with Zou Tianying, and how adulatory and shameless he was before you! He even wanted my lord to adopt him? That's truly daydreaming. How can the noble blood of the Li family be tarnished by that kind of people?"

"He is a space pirate. If he is not a shameless scoundrel, are you expecting him to be someone chaste?"

Li Wuji was still not bothered. He even laughed in amusement. "Despite the clumsiness, he was still pledging loyalty to me. Don't bother about the small details.

"Your analysis of micro-expressions and biophysical parameters should support that too. Except for his brazenness and desire for power, his face does not imply any other scheme, right?

"I've seen too many such people. He may be insane and crazy when he is a space pirate, but he is just waiting to sell himself for a good price!"

"I just don't understand."

The adjutant stared at Boss Bai in the picture gloomily.

At this moment, the fleet that Li Wuji brought had taken off and left the Blue Sky Market, but until the last starship left, Boss Bai had been standing there to see them off.

Boss Bai's spiteful behavior made the adjutant uncomfortable. The pure adulation and shamelessness was something that a noble descendant like him couldn't understand. "Why do we have to recruit those scumbags?"

Chapter 2448: He Can Only Be a Dutiful Dog for My Lord!

"A rat and a scumbag can also be useful under the right circumstances."

Li Wuji continued to teach his adjutant who was also a half disciple for him. "Space pirates gather and disperse unpredictably, yet they can cause great sabotages. They are like annoying flies that hum next to your ears all day and can be a great distraction.

"If you try to hunt those rats and scumbags, you may not be able to catch all of them, but as long as they begin to resent you, they will jump out and seek revenge from you in the next ten years there is a chance.

"Think about it. If such people are recruited by the Business Alliance of Ten Thousand Sectors and the reformists, who invest tremendous resources to arm and upgrade them, what great trouble will they grow into?

"Therefore, the 'National Protection Army' we established is not expected to have a great performance. It will be worth it as long as they are not inclined to the Business Alliance of Ten Thousand Sectors and the reformists.

"Besides, those scoundrels are adept at certain things that the regular army is not good at or unable to do due to their identity. If we use them well, they can be an army that catches everyone unprepared in both the war between the four Kurfürst families and the reformists right now and the competition within the families for me and your father to rise to higher places!

"Trading their loyalty with the resources of the family is always a great bargain no matter how questionable the loyalty is.

"One more thing. It's true that I think highly of this Bai guy. He seems different from regular space pirates. How is the investigation on his identity going?"

"There's no result yet."

The adjutant shook his head. "His identity is truly mysterious. I've searched the official file databases of dozens of Sectors, but none of them contain the information of such an expert. It's almost like he jumped out of nowhere.

"Is that so? That's quite understandable."

Li Wuji said, "The peripheral worlds of the Imperium have always been messy and disordered. Even the strongest family of Immortal Cultivators may be demolished, with everybody killed, in the blink of an eye. Those who luckily survived will also escape further into the space without leaving any information, as if the family had never existed.

"However, I have studied many of his battle cases and found the hint of the regular army in his tactical style, which indicates that he is probably associated with the army. Perhaps his father was a serviceman? All in all, he is neither from the family of space pirates nor with the rebelling reformists, and he should naturally be on our side.

"Boss Bai can't be his real name, and his hideous face right now must've been carefully devised too. But it doesn't matter. Up until so far, I can't find any reason why he would betray me. Standing on our side is his best choice. He can't be crazy enough to piss off the reformists and the four families at the same time, can he? Hahahaha!"

"You do have a point, my lord."

The adjutant said, "Whoever he was in the past, now that he has cooperated with us and razed the Blue Sky Market, he has declared war on the Business Alliance of Ten Thousand Sectors and the reformists, and he can only take our side to be my lord's dog now!"

"Yes, that's quite right, but I'm not putting a hundred percent trust in him either."

Li Wuji said, "I've left a few spies in the twenty-nine pirate gangs that make up the 'National Protection Army'. On the other hand, the half fleet that I entrusted to the Bai guy's disposal will supervise him. Should he have any anomaly in the conquest of the Seven Seas Grand Market, the half fleet and the spies in the pirate gangs will teach him a good lesson about 'loyalty'.

"My plan is absolutely flawless. What's there to be worried about?"

The adjutant nodded his head and filled Li Wuji's cup with hot water.

"Right—"

Sipping the hot tea, Li Wuji suddenly said, "How's the intelligence work on the Cultivators going? The Cultivators have caused quite a fuss in the peripheral worlds of the Imperium. A lot of information has come from various channels. Even the Bai guy told me a lot just now. Even a minor trouble can be quite tricky when it happens at a critical moment.

"The Cultivators' fleet attacked seven military warehouses in the Red Fat Space Zone and disappeared after plundering tremendous assets."

The adjutant said, "We don't know whether they are upgrading their warships with our military assets, preparing for another big job, or it is an even worse possibility—that they have truly been recruited by the Business Alliance of Ten Thousand Sectors.

"In my opinion, the regular Cultivators are not a big deal at all. Just a bunch of worthless mobsters. But that 'Fist King' Lei Zonglie is truly an extremely dangerous person, even more dangerous than this shameless Boss Bai.

"That's right. 'Fist King' Lei Zonglie escaped from the Land of Sins of the Martial Meritocrats Sector. Getting to the bottom of it, wasn't the dead man Li Lingfeng to be blamed for everything?"

Li Wuji said after a long sigh, "That pair of siblings is really interesting. The brother founded the Land of Sins and started the slaughter stream, only to broadcast the magnificent killing spree of the Cultivators to all the people of the Imperium. Not only was it a great humiliation for all the Immortal Cultivators, but he also let the crushed members of Starlight run loose. As a result, the tides of Cultivators have been breaking out nationwide in the past year. Even many Immortal Cultivators found their beliefs collapsed after watching the great collapse of 'Manjusaka, the City in the Sky'.

"The sister, on the other hand, is even more 'incredible'. She has changed from the queen of the Imperium to the leader of the reformists and the temporary archon of the Imperium, about to swallow her family back. The Li family has truly suffered a lot. Otherwise, it would've been unnecessary for us to recruit all those flies, bugs, and rats.

"Are those siblings the heaven's punishment for the Li family?"

The topic was too sensitive. Even though Li Lingfeng had died and Li Linghai had rebelled, Li Wuji could comment on them, but his adjutant, as a junior, did not have the courage to. He simply chuckled in acknowledgment.

"Alright, just go now. Let the captain perform a space jump after sailing to the designated steady area. I'm going to take a nap. There will be no need to wake me up."

Li Wuji adjusted his chair, transforming it from a hard seat to a soft gel-like bed, before he lied down.

His adjutant quietly cleaned up the tea utensils and left the room.

Since he was on his own flagship, Li Wuji relaxed quickly and forgot the discomfort he had when he was dealing with the space pirates. But when he was half asleep, he was dwelling in new annoyance again.

It was not exactly about the Cultivators.

Even though the Cultivators did have a leader like "Fist King" Lei Zonglie, they would be no trouble in front of the powerful Imperium even if they were ten times stronger.

His concerns were not even the reformists or the Covenant Alliance.

After all, the reformists were too shallow and short of soldiers. They might have seized the capital, but so what?

In the past hundred years, after the rise of the four Kurfürst families, the heart of the Imperium had been beating more and more slowly in the first place. The symbolic significance of the capital's fall was much greater than the actual significance.

Of course, the Empyreal Terminus Sector where the capital was at was a unique convenient intersection of space jumps. Losing it would increase the trouble for the Kurfürst families to communicate or gather their fleets, but there was nothing too severe.

As for the Covenant Alliance, it had been crippled in the great expedition in the past two decades, and it was an even lesser problem.

What Li Wuji was worried about was still the internal relationship of the four families.

The four families were both friends and foes. Before every new emperor ascended to the throne, a storm of blood had always been raised regarding the choice of the new emperor.

Queen Li Linghai had been through such competitions in person. That was why she was bold enough to kill Emperor Shenwu atrociously. It hit the critical part of the four families.

It was exactly Li Linghai's goal to let the four families fight over the choice of the new emperor, which was an open, undisguised scheme.

But Li Wuji discovered, to his sadness, that even though they knew it was a scheme, they had no choice except to bite each other hard.

If the four families could be united, the Covenant Alliance, the reformists, the space pirates, and the Cultivators would've been annihilated a long time ago, and today's devastation would be spared!

Li Wuji thought in pitifulness, "It's a shame that nobody is really selfless. People of the four families understand it too well!"

Such national concerns only hovered for one moment in his head.

When the idea that "nobody is really selfless" popped in his head, he began to focus all his attention on his future interests.

The twenty-nine pirate gangs had been recruited by him. If the battle of Seven Seas went well, even more space pirates would join him, which would be a force not to be underestimated. If he could grasp them in his hands…

The capital had been conquered by the reformists, and there were a lot of vacancies in the Council of Elders after the casualties. Everybody wanted to move one step higher, and he seemed to have a great shot…

While thinking, Li Wuji fell asleep.

The microchips in the soft bed, sensing the changes of his biophysical parameters, switched to the sleep protection mode automatically. The gel became softer and gradually enveloped him, which could soothe the discomfort in the space jump later.

Li Wuji had been through countless space jumps. Also, he had invested a huge amount of money on the modification of his personal chamber. The wall, the floor, and the ceiling had all been embedded with buffer and stabilization rune arrays. There would not be much shaking even during a space jump, and his thinking and his rest would not be disrupted.

However, soon after he just entered his dream, perhaps only several seconds, a deafening explosion, as if the whole world had been turned upside down, shocked him out of his soft bed of gels.

"This is—"

Li Wuji's pupils constricted violently as he sensed fatal cracks on both the starship and himself. It seemed that an invisible, uncanny force was tearing apart the entire starship and all the crew members in the most brutal way, trying to rip them into four-dimensional fragments!

Chapter 2449: The Silence Assassin

Li Wuji seemed to have been caught in a weird nightmare.

The wall around and the famous paintings hanging on it were suddenly stretched to somewhere far, far away, as if they were ten thousand meters from him, before they were suddenly bounced back to his face, almost whizzing into his eyes.

He extended his hands, only to discover that his hands were like blossoming flowers. Everything, from the fat and the veins to the nerves and the bones, was clearly visible, but he could not sense the slightest pain. The fundamental law of the three-dimensional world had been completely violated.

The deafening explosion just now was more than soundwaves too, and it was flowing, roaring, and corroding his soul that was exposed outside of his brain in glamorous colors!

It took Li Wuji a long time before he finally gathered a tiny bit of thinking ability and realized his status.

His flagship "Eternal Spring" and him were in a certain state of transition, or "superimposition", when it was breaking into the four-dimensional space for a space jump.

However, the space jump did not succeed, and his starship or even his fleet had been bounced back mercilessly.

It was almost like a high-speed train running into an iron wall.

No, it was even worse than that.

It was because they were not bounced back because of the problems of the space jump units or other faults, but because the wormholes that they ripped were blown apart by bombardments, and they were thrown back to where they came!

They were assaulted by the enemy in the middle of a space jump. It was the most dreadful nightmare that no starship commander wanted to experience!

BOOM! BOOM! BOOM! BOOM! BOOM! BOOM!

The deafening explosions congregated into a flood raging inside every pathway on the starship, collapsing and swallowing everything on its way—the crystal suits, the space shuttles, the arsenals, and the panicked Immortal Cultivators.

As a powerful person of the four Kurfürst families, Li Wuji had a fleet that was equipped with the most advanced and high-quality starships. The defense of his flagship "Eternal Spring" was only slightly lower than that of the general starship of the family. It was an out-and-out "super warship".

Under normal circumstances, one Eternal Spring was enough to deal with a dozen hostile starships without losing when it was not with a fleet. It could even resist the fierce firepower of a space fortress the hard way and fire back at the enemy.

However, it was a whole different case when a starship entered the space jump state.

No matter how sturdy the alloy armor, how strong the spiritual shield, and how thick the interferential magnetic field was in the three-dimensional world, it was absolutely meaningless for a starship that had been unfolded in the four-dimension.

When unfolded in the four-dimension, all their internal parts would be exposed. How could the armor on their bodies help at all however solid it was?

When the enemy lurking in the dark suddenly dropped the disguise and launched the fiercest firepower at the fleet that was in the middle of a jump, it was not an ambush or a war but a sheer slaughter.

Eternal Spring and its owner, Li Wuji, were finally bounced back to the three-dimensional state.

But the damage caused by the intense explosions and the severe interferences were irreversible.

"Ahh…"

Li Wuji stared at his hands in a daze. During the process when he was bounced from the four-dimensional state to the three-dimensional state, space ripples had been flying freely inside the starship like blades without thickness and, other than reaping the lives of too many Immortal Cultivators outside, cut his three fingers and half of his ear. The blood was spurting crazily, staining the delicate woolen carpet on the floor into a mess. He was both disgusted and in pain.

His case was among the better ones.

His personal chamber had the best protection. So, the rampage of space ripples did not cause much damage.

He couldn't imagine what a terrifying picture it would be outside, particularly near the engine compartment and the space jump units.

How did it happen?

His face pale and covered in a cold sweat, Li Wuji was completely lost.

Even the dumbest starship commander knew that, while space jump was the most important technology for mankind to conquer the universe, it was also the most vulnerable, defenseless moment for every starship and every passenger onboard and the most likely moment for the enemy to attack.

Therefore, a space jump should only be performed in the most steady area after ensuring that no enemy was around.

If possible, it would be better to ensure that they would be met by their own people at the destination of the jump, and they had large space gates for navigation. Only by doing so could the space jump be riskless.

Even though the commander on Li Wuji's fleet was not the most famous general in the Imperium, he was still from a noble background and was a professional with decades of training and a hundred years of commanding experience.

The jump point he had chosen was the traditional, steadiest point in space near the Blue Sky Market. Every nine out of ten starships that passed the Blue Sky Market would choose to jump from here. Nothing had ever gone wrong.

All the warships in the Blue Sky Market that belonged to the Business Alliance of Ten Thousand Sectors had been destroyed or disarmed. It was absolutely impossible for them to take off and attack Li Wuji and his fleet.

Was it done by the space pirates?

Absolutely impossible. All the pirate warships were docked in the Blue Sky Market obediently under the watch of the other half of his fleet closely. Li Wuji did not receive any report of the anomaly at all!

A third force that was neither the space pirates nor the Business Alliance of Ten Thousand Sectors had suddenly jumped out to attack them?

That was too preposterous!

The universe was too vast, and the distance was too far. Technically speaking, nobody could "suddenly jump out" at all.

Li Wuji's starship could've detected the waves of the rushing starships within hundreds of thousands of kilometers. If a fleet had been coming at them aggressively from millions of kilometers away or even further, his commander, being neither blind nor deaf, couldn't have neglected it at all or decided to perform a space jump under such circumstances in order to kill themselves, could he?

After all the impossibilities were ruled out, there was only one explanation that was most ridiculous and horrifying was left.

Li Wuji suddenly felt that his mouth was dry, and his heart had sunken into a bottomless canyon.

The only possibility was that there was indeed a hostile fleet, one that did not come at them aggressively from millions of kilometers away but had been ambushing right next to them all the time.

To be more exact, they had been ambushing in the steady space that was most suitable for space jumps near the Blue Sky Market.

Turning off the power units, retreating the spiritual shields, maintaining radio silence, and even lowering the artificial gravity and air circulation inside the starships to the minimum, they tried not to spread out the slightest spiritual wave.

If there was indeed such a silent fleet, which had stayed in the state of rocks and garbage for the past few days or even longer, it was indeed possible that they could've avoided the scanning of Li Wuji's fleet under the cover of the stone belts outside of the Blue Sky Market.

However… However… However—

"How can there possibly be such an insane tactic!"

Li Wuji let out a scream of incomprehension!

In order to carry out such a tactic, the enemy must've arrived here a few days in advance to set up the ambush, and to not release many spiritual waves, the living environment on the starships must've been extremely harsh. Chances were that the temperature was below zero, and the air was stinky. Everybody had to wear their crystal suits and live through the few days in sufferings with the self-circulating respirators as tough as iron.

The mysterious, silent fleet was like an assassin who hid inside a swamp, holding the breath, the pores, and even the heartbeat, just in order to wait for an opportunity to cut the enemy's throat.

Li Wuji discovered painfully that he seemed to have fallen into a fatal trap.

The mysterious enemy with such willpower and such a high control over the fleet was certainly not waiting for any random business group or pirate gang. The only prey that was worth their price was him, Li Wuji!

"My lord! My lord!"

His adjutant's desperate screams echoed from outside the door.

Before Li Wuji answered, his adjutant, covered in blood and snot, had already barged in.

Li Wuji looked around and picked the flowery scarf on the table. Grabbing the adjutant's collar with the scarf as a blockage, he shrieked, "Don't panic! What exactly has happened?"

"A-A mysterious fleet that came out of nowhere launched an assault at us while we were carrying out the space jump!"

The adjutant cried, "Doomed! Our fleet is doomed! Most of the starships have been deformed by the space ripples and lost the abilities to attack, defend, or move. The remaining starships have also lost their commandership and become loose sand!"

"Idiot!"

Had it not been the blood and snot on his adjutant's face, Li Wuji would've slapped him hard. "What do you mean by saying 'they have lost their commandership'? What are the captains doing? Hurry and raise the spiritual shields and gather the starships that are still mobile! Even a skinny camel is still larger than a horse. My fleet is among the best at the peripheral worlds of the Imperium. Even though we've been attacked halfway through the space jump, who can be certain to swallow all my starships?"

"It's… It's impossible to raise the spiritual shield or send any message to other starships!"

The adjutant replied, almost crying, "Our control system and even the entire battle network have been compromised and sabotaged by the enemy!"

"What?"

Feeling that his head was dizzy, Li Wuji shouted at the loudest of his voice, "How is it possible? Eternal Spring has state-of-the-art technologies in cyberwarfare. After we set sail and the dynamic defense system is activated, the password for every interface is changed more than a hundred times every second. Who could've hacked our battle network so quickly?"

"It was not hacked just now."

His adjutant wailed, "When docked in the Blue Sky Market for maintenance and fuel refill, Eternal Spring was once connected to the database of the Blue Sky Market to collect the business secrets of the Business Alliance of Ten Thousand Sectors. Billions of entries of information were copied. Somebody must've snuck in at that time and left a 'backdoor' in our system.

"By the time we had a space jump and the mainframe crystal processor of the starship was most vulnerable, the enemy kicked on the 'backdoor' and broke in like ferocious wolves!"

Chapter 2450: Arrival of the Iron Fist!

"You… You—"

Li Wuji felt that the world was swirling before his eyes in his fury. He finally slapped the adjutant in the face brutally despite the filth. "Idiot, somebody left a 'backdoor' on the mainframe crystal processor of the flagship and even the battle network of the entire fleet, and you had no response at all? Were you all dead?

"Also, even when we were anchored, it would still take a long time for the enemy to compromise our dozens of layers of defense, right? How could the enemy have achieved that?"

"You're quite right, my lord."

The adjutant said gloomily, "At that time, the Blue Sky Market was just conquered by us. Fires and explosions were still occasionally happening in the warehouses. As a result, the network system inside the market was unstable, and intense waves were happening every few minutes. Sometimes, the chaotic pulses from the explosions would affect our starships too. They are all the regular phenomena when a port is conquered. We never thought much of it back then. But now that we think of it again, that was probably when the enemy snuck in under the cover of the interferences and the pulses.

"As for the time, well, we did not plan to stay for long in the Blue Sky Market, which might've given the enemy an opportunity, but later, my lord—my lord was invited to the celebration dinner by the Bai guy. A lot of time was delayed. On your way back to the starship, my lord was stalled by the Bai guy again, who ranted on so many things, like asking to be your foster son, reminding you to be wary of 'Fist King' Lei Zonglie. A lot of time was wasted again. Perhaps it was during that time…"

Li Wuji's eyeballs protruded out of his eye sockets slowly, like the eggs being squeezed out of the birth canal, as he stared at his adjutant.

His adjutant had been too focused on talking eloquently to help his master analyze the current situation. It was not until this moment that he finally noticed his master's murderous eyes. Flabbergasted, he hurried to stop and looked at his master pitifully.

"Get lost!"

Li Wuji bellowed and shoved his adjutant before he ran out of his personal chamber to the bridge.

"Whatever it takes, gather the soldiers that are still able to move. Let's retreat to the Blue Sky Market with the unreachable starships as our cover!

"Drastic changes must've happened in the Blue Sky Market right now. We have to return immediately and suppress the Bai guy and then resist the newly-emerging mysterious enemy based on the defense system at the edge of the Blue Sky Market. This is the only way!

"If Boss Bai seizes the other half of our fleet in the Blue Sky Market and controls the whole defense system of the Blue Sky Market, we will be doomed for good!"

Li Wuji shrieked as he rushed away, not even noticing that one of his boots was missing.

Before him, the previously straight pathway had been twisted into a broken screw. Shockingly huge cracks were everywhere on the wall. Almost all the tubes and cables had been broken, either spurting out high-pressure air currents or unleashing spluttering sparks. Now and then, he saw the most terrible picture—a lot of Immortal Cultivators and the wall were melted into an inseparable whole, and metal particles had filled their internal organs. They had died the most miserable death.

They were all the consequences of the failed space jump.

When the crew members and the wall were dismembered, ready to pass the four-dimensional space, they suffered a heavy strike, which blew them together in the four-dimensional state like a tornado. After they were bounced back to the three-dimensional space again, they were already a tragedy.

It was also the first time that Li Wuji had ever seen such horror in person.

The scene was no worse than the cruelest torture for an unsullied noble like him.

He tried his best not to throw up on his way to the bridge.

It was a pity that the bridge of Eternal Spring was also mired in flames. Everything was a mess. The crystal processors and the seats of the crew were lying on the ground, and the small explosions never stopped.

The leadership, who should be controlling the starship and commanding the fleet, had mostly put on their crystal suits and were fighting the rogue fires and the balls of lightning with the foam extinguishers.

Li Wuji calmed himself down and meant to discuss it with the captain.

But to his surprise, the captain was standing before the mainframe crystal processor in a daze, staring at the main operation menu on the light beam in hopelessness and desperation.

Li Wuji looked at where the captain's shivering eyes were looking, only to discover that the main operation menu of his flagship was empty. All the data and information had been shattered into glittering stars, and the stars were gradually condensed into a gigantic swirl, revolving slowly.

Very soon, at the center of the swirl, which looked like a black hole, an… iron fist slowly rose!

The iron fist was filled with the virile and barbaric blast. Rivets, screws, and rust were all over its surface, and steams that looked like white dragons were jetting from the joints. The sound of it squeaking after being filled up by fury was almost audible.

Then, from the top of the picture, a crown made of gears fell down on the fist in a bam.

The iron fist wearing the crown of gears silently watched the Immortal Cultivators who were in a mess.

"Fist King!"

The adjutant lost his cool and shouted behind Li Wuji, "It's 'Fist King' Lei Zonglie!"

Li Wuji's heart that had been falling in the valley was finally grabbed by that iron fist.

…

Five minutes earlier, in the Blue Sky Market, neither the space pirates nor the half fleet that Li Wuji left behind for supervision foresaw the slaughter among the broken stars five minutes later.

Li Wuji's departure was actually quite a relief on the rude and disobedient space pirates, like the employees whose stern boss had just left. Everybody revealed their real appearance, screaming and shouting, pushing the joy of the party to the apex.

Even the seasoned marauders who had roamed the sea of stars for a hundred years had never looted such a famous, prosperous city at the edge of the Imperium like the Blue Sky Market before.

Whenever they came here for supplies and rest, they had to follow the management of the Business Alliance of Ten Thousand Sectors and needed to behave themselves even if they were visiting the brothels!

Today, after the conquest of the Blue Sky Market, the myriad of treasures in the warehouses were indeed great eye-openers for them, making them forget everything else.

The titles such as colonels or lieutenants of the Imperium further added to their confidence and courage, giving them the illusion that nobody would dare to ask for trouble since the four families were on their back.

It was needless to mention the leaders of the smaller pirate gangs, who had long abandoned their alert and wariness after the three buckets of hard liquor.

In the meantime, while Boss Bai had an unattractive look and only just began his career, he seemed to have been a space pirate ever since he was in his mother's womb, and he knew what the space pirates thought and liked perfectly. Not only was everything that he said exactly what other people had in mind, but even the performances that he had arranged were also particularly spectacular too. Also, he had mediated between quite a few pirate gangs who had been holding grudges against one another for a long time, winning the cheers of everyone at the party and slighting altering the space pirates' impression of him.

"Boss Bai is truly trying to be the commander of this National Protection Army. He is free to do whatever he wants. After all, we won't lose anything."

"We now work for the four Kurfürst families. Who is bold enough to challenge us here?"

"Even if any of them wants to challenge us, look, half of Marquess Yongchun's fleet is still here, commanded by 'Li Yuanzhen', the lord's trusted general. Even a madman wouldn't attack us at such a moment."

"So, let's just indulge us for half a day. The transformation from space pirates to a legitimate army does call for celebration!"

Thinking as such, they never said no to the drinks that Boss Bai's soldiers brought over. The wine of this round was made of dozens of fresh fruits and tasted more like cool juice than wine. According to Boss Bai, the alcohol percentage of the wine was very low, and nobody would get drunk. It was most suitable to refresh their stomachs that had just been burnt by the hard liquor.

While everybody was talking and laughing, nobody cared if it was right. But as it happened, after they drank the cool fruit wine, the dizziness just now was indeed much better, and they found themselves drinking more and more of it uncontrollably!

When the leaders of the pirate gangs were having a celebration dinner at the central square, the space pirates under their lead were certainly not idle either. Accompanied by the soldiers of the Big Bai Pirate Gang, they gambled and drank and had great fun at the edge.

Boss Bai also meant to integrate the many pirate gangs with the celebration dinner. Hinted by him, the soldiers of the Big Bai Pirate Gang gathered the members of other pirate gangs with the excuses of drinking games and dispersed them, leading them to different corners in the market.

As a result, it was true that the soldiers of the pirate gangs made a lot of new friends, but their old buddies who were in their own gang were all lost!

As time went by, the party was more and more heated and uncontrollable.

Even the officers and soldiers that Li Wuji left in the Blue Sky Market were moved by such an atmosphere and had a great time under the service of the members of the Big Bai Pirate Gang.

Proud of their identity, they naturally did not join the carnival of the space pirates, but they did not reject the delicious wine and the cuisines offered by Boss Bai either.

Perhaps there was only one person in the entire Blue Sky Market who retained the basic clearness.

He was Li Yuanzhen, a vice-admiral of the Imperium and the commander of the half fleet that Li Wuji had left behind.

Li Yuanzhen was Li Wuji's cousin in the family and one of his trusted subordinates. Now that he had been left to supervise, his steadiness and his loyalty were certainly unquestionable.

Although all the enemies had been suppressed or imprisoned, and not a single force within millions of kilometers was strong enough to threaten them, the excessive revelry where nobody had the slightest restraint still made the well-trained, regular warrior naturally disgusted and wary.

"Commander Bai, this is about enough."

Li Yuanzhen finally found Boss Bai. Bulging his brown eyes, he said coldly, "The Blue Sky Market is still unstable. Chances are that some people of the Business Alliance of Ten Thousand Sectors and the reformists are still lurking in the dark. Also, our starships are all seriously damaged and in dire need of maintenance and refill of fuel and ammunitions. Should we call it a day?"
